# DLM-034｜憲法

```yaml
RUNTIME_CARD:
  DLM_ID: "DLM-034"
  DOMAIN_NAME: "憲法"
  PRIMARY_SUBJECT: "A"
  INTERNAL_PLANE: "FOUNDATION_AUTHORIZATION"
  STRUCTURAL_ROLE: "FOUNDATIONAL_MOTHER"
  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_NARROWED"
  CURRENT_LAW_STATUS: "STABLE_METHOD_ONLY__POSITIVE_LAW_TO_VERIFY"
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_I_DLM_001_114"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# 40｜DLM-034｜憲法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_I/V0.38-EXP/B12"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "B:司法／爭端／強制裁判、E:人／家庭／勞動／社會關係"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留公權力來源、分權、基本權限制與憲法控制的總母層；選舉、政黨、立法、地方自治等專門卡取得優先細節。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-034-CONSTITUTIONAL_LAW"
  chinese_name: "憲法"
  english_name: "Constitutional Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "國家權力從哪裡來、分給誰、哪些權力不能集中於同一主體、人民有哪些國家不能任意侵犯的基本權，以及當民主多數、政府效率與個人自由衝突時，什麼制度有資格作最後的憲法控制？"

  historical_reason:
    "國家需要立法、行政、司法、財政、安全與緊急處置能力，但最危險的權力往往不是無權力者，而是已具有合法身份與強制工具的國家機關。憲法因此不只宣告權利，也配置權限、建立權力分立與制衡、規範民主形成程序、保障基本權，並透過司法審查或其他憲法控制機制阻止權力因自身存在而自我正當化。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "民主多數決 vs 基本權"
    - "政府有效治理 vs 權力分立／制衡"
    - "政治責任 vs 司法審查"
    - "憲法穩定性 vs 社會變遷"
    - "國家安全 vs 個人自由"
    - "中央統一 vs 地方／聯邦自治"
    - "法律明確性 vs 政策彈性"
    - "緊急狀態效率 vs 權力永久化風險"
    - "憲法最高性 vs 民主立法自主"
    - "基本權普遍性 vs 權利衝突"

  shortest_maxim:
    "憲法第一眼不是『這件事公不公平』；先問『誰在行使哪一種國家權力、權力從哪條憲法／法律鏈取得、碰到哪個基本權或制度界線、由誰審查』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 40.1 第一眼｜ACTOR → CONSTITUTIONAL SOURCE → COMPETENCE → RIGHT / STRUCTURE → LIMITATION → PROCEDURE → REVIEW

```text
ACTOR
＝國會？總統／政府？法院？地方政府？獨立機關？軍警？

CONSTITUTIONAL SOURCE
＝憲法本文？修憲？憲法慣例？組織法？國際人權義務？

COMPETENCE
＝這個機關有沒有權做這件事？

RIGHT / STRUCTURE
＝基本權？權力分立？聯邦／地方權限？選舉？司法獨立？

LIMITATION
＝限制權利有沒有法律依據、正當目的、比例與核心界線？

PROCEDURE
＝立法／任命／彈劾／緊急權／修憲程序有沒有完成？

REVIEW
＝哪個法院／憲法機關有權審？
```

核心：

```text
POLITICAL IMPORTANCE
≠ CONSTITUTIONAL POWER
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 40.18 證據特徵

憲法案件不只看抽象法條，還可能需要：

- 立法紀錄；
- 政府政策資料；
- 統計；
- 權利影響；
- 緊急狀態事實；
- 替代措施；
- 比較資料；
- 選舉資料；
- 任命程序；
- 預算；
- 行政實施；
- 實際歧視效果；
- 監控規模；
- institutional practice。

核心：

> **憲法審查的「事實」常是制度性事實，而不是只有單一事件。**

---

## POSITIVE_LAW_GATE｜SOURCE-DERIVED / NOT RE-VERIFIED IN BUILD01

> **法位提醒：以下段落直接承接來源卡；BUILD01 未執行 web/current-law refresh。若案件依賴條約狀態、生效日期、國內轉置或最新法規，主系統必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## 40.20 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依特定憲政體制現查：

1. 憲法文本與修正；
2. 憲法慣例；
3. 國家組織與權限；
4. 國會程序；
5. 行政權；
6. 司法權；
7. 地方／聯邦制度；
8. 基本權清單；
9. 基本權限制測試；
10. 平等審查；
11. 選舉與政治權；
12. 緊急權；
13. 修憲；
14. constitutional review；
15. standing / justiciability；
16. horizontal effect；
17. 國際人權法在國內法位；
18. constitutional remedies。

## 40.19 常見誤分類

```yaml
COMMON_MISCLASSIFICATION:
  - "我覺得不公平＝違憲。"
  - "法律存在＝一定合憲。"
  - "基本權存在＝任何限制都違憲。"
  - "公共利益重要＝任何基本權限制都合憲。"
  - "機關互相影響＝一定違反權力分立。"
  - "法院可以審查＝法院可以替政府做所有政策選擇。"
  - "緊急狀態＝憲法暫停。"
  - "私人公司侵害利益＝一定直接違反憲法。"
  - "修憲票數夠＝所有內容都必然可修。"
```

# DLM-048｜國籍法

```yaml
RUNTIME_CARD:
  DLM_ID: "DLM-048"
  DOMAIN_NAME: "國籍法"
  PRIMARY_SUBJECT: "A"
  INTERNAL_PLANE: "FOUNDATION_AUTHORIZATION"
  STRUCTURAL_ROLE: "STATE_IDENTITY_SPECIALIZATION"
  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_SPECIALIZATION"
  CURRENT_LAW_STATUS: "STABLE_METHOD_ONLY__POSITIVE_LAW_TO_VERIFY"
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_I_DLM_001_114"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# 54｜DLM-048｜國籍法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_I/V0.38-EXP/B16"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "E:人／家庭／勞動／社會關係"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別處理國家成立／承認、國家繼承與國籍連結；都涉及國家身分，但法律主體與效果不可互換。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-048-NATIONALITY_CITIZENSHIP_LAW"
  chinese_name: "國籍法"
  english_name: "Nationality / Citizenship Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一個自然人何時、依哪一國法律取得與該國的國籍法律連結；國家如何在出生、血統、歸化、婚姻、收養、國家繼承與喪失／剝奪國籍時維持法律確定性、平等與防止無國籍？"

  historical_reason:
    "國籍決定誰被一國視為其政治共同體成員，並影響入境權、政治權、護照、外交保護、公共職位、移民待遇與家庭身份。國籍傳統上高度屬於國家主權，但任意剝奪、性別歧視、國家繼承與出生登記缺失會讓人失去任何國家的法律保護。國際法因此逐步建立 right to a nationality、反任意剝奪、兒童取得國籍與防止 statelessness 的最低保障。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "國家決定國民資格 vs right to a nationality"
    - "jus soli vs jus sanguinis"
    - "國籍安定 vs 反詐欺／國安剝奪"
    - "單一國籍政策 vs dual / multiple nationality"
    - "血統連結 vs 平等與性別中立"
    - "出生事實 vs 文件證明"
    - "歸化裁量 vs 法治／反歧視"
    - "國家繼承 vs 避免無國籍"
    - "公民身份權利 vs 非公民仍有人權"
    - "國籍法定身份 vs 族裔／文化認同"

  shortest_maxim:
    "國籍法第一眼不是『他是哪裡人』；先問『哪一國依哪一條法律、在什麼時間點，因出生、血統、歸化或其他連結把他視為國民；有什麼文件能證明；取得、喪失或剝奪會不會造成無國籍』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 54.1 第一眼｜STATE → LEGAL BASIS → LINK → ACQUISITION → PROOF → MULTIPLE NATIONALITY → LOSS / DEPRIVATION → STATELESSNESS

```text
STATE
＝哪一國國籍？

LEGAL BASIS
＝哪一版 nationality law？出生當時還是現在？

LINK
＝出生地？父母？婚姻？收養？居住？國家繼承？

ACQUISITION
＝at birth？registration？naturalization？declaration？

PROOF
＝birth record？passport？nationality certificate？registry？

MULTIPLE NATIONALITY
＝是否同時有其他國籍？

LOSS / DEPRIVATION
＝自願放棄？自動喪失？行政剝奪？法院？

STATELESSNESS
＝結果是否無任何國籍？有無防止機制？
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 54.25 證據特徵

- birth certificate；
- parents' nationality records；
- family register；
- passport；
- nationality certificate；
- naturalization decision；
- marriage / adoption；
- renunciation；
- deprivation decision；
- citizenship registry；
- state succession documents；
- residence history；
- consular records；
- administrative correspondence；
- court judgments；
- laws in force at relevant dates；
- proof of how foreign nationality law operates in practice。

核心：

> **國籍案件不是看「這個人像哪國人」，而是做一條跨時間的法律身份證據鏈。**

---

## POSITIVE_LAW_GATE｜SOURCE-DERIVED / NOT RE-VERIFIED IN BUILD01

> **法位提醒：以下段落直接承接來源卡；BUILD01 未執行 web/current-law refresh。若案件依賴條約狀態、生效日期、國內轉置或最新法規，主系統必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## 54.27 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依 relevant State 與 relevant date 現查：

1. nationality at birth；
2. jus soli；
3. jus sanguinis；
4. registration；
5. foundlings；
6. otherwise-stateless children；
7. naturalization；
8. marriage；
9. adoption；
10. ART / surrogacy；
11. dual nationality；
12. renunciation；
13. automatic loss；
14. deprivation / denaturalization；
15. procedural review；
16. gender equality；
17. state succession；
18. statelessness determination；
19. 1954 Convention applicability；
20. 1961 Convention applicability；
21. passport / proof rules；
22. diplomatic protection；
23. nationality effect on migration / refugee law。

# DLM-061｜選舉法

```yaml
RUNTIME_CARD:
  DLM_ID: "DLM-061"
  DOMAIN_NAME: "選舉法"
  PRIMARY_SUBJECT: "A"
  INTERNAL_PLANE: "FOUNDATION_AUTHORIZATION"
  STRUCTURAL_ROLE: "AUTHORIZATION_SPECIALIZATION"
  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_SPECIALIZATION"
  CURRENT_LAW_STATUS: "STABLE_METHOD_ONLY__POSITIVE_LAW_TO_VERIFY"
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_I_DLM_001_114"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# 67｜DLM-061｜選舉法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_I/V0.38-EXP/B21"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "E:人／家庭／勞動／社會關係、G:資訊／知識／數位"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別承擔選舉、政黨／政治資金、立法程序與地方自治的民主授權／分權專門鏈。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-061-ELECTION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "選舉法"
  english_name: "Election Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "人民如何以自由、平等、週期性且可驗證的程序把政治偏好轉成公職與代表席次；國家如何在 voter/candidate eligibility、選區、競選、媒體、政治資金、投票技術、計票與爭議處理上建立中立規則，使執政者不能利用既有權力決定下一次誰有資格競爭與勝選？"

  historical_reason:
    "民主選舉的風險不只是假票或計票作弊。掌權者可以透過選民登記、候選資格、選區、媒體、行政資源、競選規則、選舉機關、投票技術與救濟時程影響競爭。選舉法因此把民主授權拆成一條完整 process：誰能參與、競爭是否真實、票是否自由秘密、結果是否可驗證、爭議能否在職權移轉前被有效處理。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "人民主權 vs 具體選舉制度設計"
    - "普遍選舉權 vs 合理資格門檻"
    - "平等選舉 vs 地理／代表性考量"
    - "競選自由 vs campaign integrity"
    - "政府行政能力 vs election-management independence"
    - "快速確定結果 vs 有效爭議救濟"
    - "投票秘密 vs 選舉可驗證性"
    - "數位便利 vs cybersecurity / auditability"
    - "選舉法穩定 vs 必要改革"
    - "國家資訊治理 vs 選舉言論自由"
    - "反假訊息 vs censorship risk"
    - "當選證書安定 vs 可證明之重大違規救濟"

  shortest_maxim:
    "選舉法第一眼不是『誰贏了』；先問『誰能選／被選、規則何時形成、誰管理、競爭是否公平、票怎麼進系統、怎麼被算出來、誰能在結果生效前提出可用的救濟』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 67.1 第一眼｜ELECTION TYPE → ELECTORATE → CANDIDATE → DISTRICT / FORMULA → ADMINISTRATION → CAMPAIGN → VOTE → COUNT → CERTIFICATION → DISPUTE

```text
ELECTION TYPE
＝presidential？parliamentary？local？referendum？

ELECTORATE
＝citizen？age？residence？registration？disqualification？

CANDIDATE
＝nomination？party list？independent？deposit？incompatibility？

DISTRICT / FORMULA
＝single-member？PR？mixed？threshold？seat allocation？

ADMINISTRATION
＝誰管理、任命、監督、採購？

CAMPAIGN
＝media、speech、state resources、finance、security？

VOTE
＝paper？machine？postal？early？internet？assistance？

COUNT
＝polling station？central count？recount？audit？

CERTIFICATION
＝誰正式確認結果？

DISPUTE
＝complaint、appeal、deadline、remedy？
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 67.29 證據特徵

- constitution / election statute；
- district map / population data；
- voter registry；
- nomination file；
- EMB appointment / minutes；
- campaign rules；
- public-resource records；
- media monitoring；
- campaign finance；
- ballot design；
- polling-station logs；
- paper ballots；
- machine logs；
- ICT audit；
- observer reports；
- count protocols；
- certification；
- complaints / judgments；
- recount records。

核心：

> **選舉案真正要重建的不是「我覺得選舉不公平」，而是從規則、競爭、投票、計票到救濟的哪一個節點發生了可證明的資格破壞。**

---

## 67.31 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依 election type、jurisdiction、date 現查：

1. Article 25 / constitutional voting rights；
2. electorate / citizenship；
3. voter registration；
4. disenfranchisement；
5. candidate eligibility；
6. nomination / ballot access；
7. electoral system；
8. districting；
9. election administration；
10. campaign period；
11. media；
12. political finance；
13. state resources；
14. political ads；
15. voting methods；
16. absentee / postal / early voting；
17. accessibility；
18. voting technology；
19. cybersecurity；
20. secrecy；
21. counting；
22. recount / audit；
23. certification；
24. complaints / appeals；
25. invalidation / re-run；
26. election observation；
27. referendum rules；
28. election-law amendment timing。

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別承擔選舉、政黨／政治資金、立法程序與地方自治的民主授權／分權專門鏈。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-062-POLITICAL_PARTY_POLITICAL_FINANCE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "政黨／政治資金法"
  english_name: "Political Party / Political Finance Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "公民如何自由組成、加入、退出與競爭政黨；國家可以為透明、民主競爭、反貪腐與國安設定哪些 registration、funding、reporting、campaign-finance 與 dissolution 規則，又如何避免藉監管政黨而控制政治思想與反對力量？"

  historical_reason:
    "政黨把分散的政治偏好轉成候選人、政策、國會組織與政府，因此既是私人結社，也是民主基礎設施。若完全不規範，秘密金錢、國家資源、外國控制與 oligarchic capture 可能扭曲政治競爭；若規範過度，執政者又可以用 registration、finance audit 或 dissolution 消滅反對黨。政黨法的核心因此是『保障政治結社＋限制權力與金錢扭曲』的雙重結構。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "freedom of association vs party regulation"
    - "party autonomy vs internal democracy / member rights"
    - "private donations vs political equality"
    - "financial privacy vs transparency"
    - "state funding vs party independence"
    - "foreign influence control vs cross-border political association"
    - "anti-corruption vs opposition harassment"
    - "party discipline vs representative autonomy"
    - "party ban vs democratic self-defence"
    - "campaign spending freedom vs level playing field"
    - "third-party advocacy vs circumvention"
    - "online fundraising / crypto vs traceability"

  shortest_maxim:
    "政黨／政治資金法第一眼不是『這個黨好不好』；先問『它如何成立、誰控制、錢從哪裡來、怎麼花、國家用什麼規則監督、這個監督是在保護民主競爭還是在替執政者清場』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 68.1 第一眼｜ASSOCIATION → REGISTRATION → MEMBERSHIP → GOVERNANCE → NOMINATION → FUNDING → SPENDING → DISCLOSURE → OVERSIGHT → SANCTION / DISSOLUTION

```text
ASSOCIATION
＝公民如何組黨？

REGISTRATION
＝notification？registration？legal personality？

MEMBERSHIP
＝誰可加入／退出？public servant / military restrictions？

GOVERNANCE
＝constitution、leadership、internal vote、discipline？

NOMINATION
＝候選人如何產生？

FUNDING
＝membership dues？donation？state funding？loans？foreign sources？

SPENDING
＝party ordinary spending？campaign spending？

DISCLOSURE
＝誰知道 donors、beneficial source、expenses？

OVERSIGHT
＝election body？audit office？court？

SANCTION
＝fine？funding loss？criminal referral？suspension？dissolution？
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 68.29 證據特徵

- party constitution；
- registration；
- membership rules；
- candidate-selection file；
- donation ledger；
- donor identity / beneficial ownership；
- loans；
- in-kind valuation；
- state funding；
- campaign expenses；
- third-party ads；
- bank records；
- audit；
- regulator correspondence；
- sanctions；

核心：

> **政治資金案不能只看帳面上的「誰匯款」，而要追到實際提供利益的人、資金控制者、第三方支出與最終政治用途。**

# DLM-063｜國會／立法程序法

```yaml
RUNTIME_CARD:
  DLM_ID: "DLM-063"
  DOMAIN_NAME: "國會／立法程序法"

  STRUCTURAL_ROLE: "AUTHORIZATION_SPECIALIZATION"
  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_SPECIALIZATION"
  CURRENT_LAW_STATUS: "STABLE_METHOD_ONLY__POSITIVE_LAW_TO_VERIFY"
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_I_DLM_001_114"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# 69｜DLM-063｜國會／立法程序法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_I/V0.38-EXP/B21"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "B:司法／爭端／強制裁判"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別承擔選舉、政黨／政治資金、立法程序與地方自治的民主授權／分權專門鏈。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-063-PARLIAMENTARY_LEGISLATIVE_PROCEDURE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "國會／立法程序法"
  english_name: "Parliamentary / Legislative Procedure Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "民選代表與其他有提案權主體如何把政策轉成具有普遍拘束力的法律；憲法、兩院制、committee、quorum、readings、amendments、debate、voting、promulgation、delegation、opposition rights、privilege 與 judicial review 如何確保『取得多數』與『取得法律資格』不是同一件事？"

  historical_reason:
    "選舉只把人送進議會，不會自動把其政治意志變成法律。立法會改變所有人的權利義務，因此民主制度需要 proposal、公開、委員會、辯論、修正、表決、跨院、公布與司法／憲法控制。程序不只是效率工具，而是讓少數、反對黨、專家、公眾與受影響者在不可逆的法律效果形成前仍有輸入與反駁入口。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "majority rule vs minority / opposition rights"
    - "legislative efficiency vs deliberation"
    - "parliamentary autonomy vs constitutional review"
    - "political negotiation vs transparency"
    - "committee expertise vs plenary legitimacy"
    - "executive agenda control vs legislative independence"
    - "urgent lawmaking vs consultation / quality"
    - "delegated legislation vs democratic accountability"
    - "parliamentary privilege vs ordinary legal accountability"
    - "party discipline vs representative role"
    - "public consultation vs representative decision"
    - "stable law vs rapid policy response"

  shortest_maxim:
    "國會／立法程序法第一眼不是『票數夠不夠』；先問『誰有提案權、法案走了哪個程序、誰被允許審議／修正、quorum 與表決是否成立、另一院／元首／法院有無角色、何時才真正生效』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 69.1 第一眼｜COMPETENCE → INITIATION → DRAFT → PUBLICATION → COMMITTEE → AMENDMENT → PLENARY → VOTE → SECOND CHAMBER / EXECUTIVE → PROMULGATION → REVIEW

```text
COMPETENCE
＝這件事由中央／地方／哪個 chamber 立法？

INITIATION
＝政府？議員？委員會？人民 initiative？

DRAFT
＝政策與法律文字如何形成？

PUBLICATION
＝何時公開？是否可閱讀？

COMMITTEE
＝哪個委員會？hearings？experts？

AMENDMENT
＝誰可修？修正是否 germane / within scope？

PLENARY
＝debate？readings？time？

VOTE
＝quorum？majority threshold？recorded vote？

SECOND CHAMBER / EXECUTIVE
＝另一院？assent？veto？override？

PROMULGATION
＝正式公布？

ENTRY INTO FORCE
＝立即？特定日期？vacatio legis？

REVIEW
＝constitutional court？procedural review？
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 69.32 證據特徵

- constitution；
- rules of procedure；
- bill text / versions；
- explanatory memorandum；
- impact assessment；
- consultation record；
- committee minutes；
- hearing transcript；
- amendment history；
- attendance / quorum；
- roll-call vote；
- chamber transmission；
- executive assent / veto；
- gazette publication；
- delegated regulation；
- parliamentary inquiry；
- ethics disclosure；
- constitutional-court decision；
- ex post evaluation。

核心：

> **立法案件的證據不是只看「最後通過的法條」，而是要能追蹤這個規範如何取得提案、審議、表決、公布與生效資格。**

```yaml
RUNTIME_CARD:
  DLM_ID: "DLM-089"
  DOMAIN_NAME: "地方自治法"

  INTERNAL_PLANE: "FOUNDATION_AUTHORIZATION"
  STRUCTURAL_ROLE: "AUTHORIZATION_SPECIALIZATION"
  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_SPECIALIZATION"
  CURRENT_LAW_STATUS: "STABLE_METHOD_ONLY__POSITIVE_LAW_TO_VERIFY"
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_I_DLM_001_114"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# 95｜DLM-089｜地方自治法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_I/V0.38-EXP/B30"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "C:金錢／資本／信用／風險流、E:人／家庭／勞動／社會關係"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別承擔選舉、政黨／政治資金、立法程序與地方自治的民主授權／分權專門鏈。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-089-LOCAL_GOVERNMENT_LOCAL_SELF_GOVERNMENT_LAW"
  chinese_name: "地方自治法"
  english_name: "Local Government / Local Self-Government Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "local authority 的法律人格、territory、elected bodies、competence、by-law power、finance、taxation、staff、municipal enterprises、intergovernmental relations、delegated tasks 與 judicial protection如何由 constitution / statute / charter建立；中央／區域政府在何種情況可監督、糾正、接管或重組地方權力？"

  historical_reason:
    "公共服務與民主參與往往最接近居民發生，但國家又必須保持整體法秩序、財政均衡與最低標準。地方自治因此不是地方脫離國家的主權，而是 constitutional / statutory distribution of public power。成熟制度必須同時處理 subsidiarity、own competence、delegated functions、adequate finance、elected accountability、administrative supervision、territorial reform、inter-municipal cooperation 與 judicial remedy。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "local autonomy vs national uniformity"
    - "elected legitimacy vs statutory competence"
    - "subsidiarity vs economies of scale"
    - "own functions vs delegated functions"
    - "local revenue autonomy vs national fiscal control"
    - "equalization vs local incentives"
    - "State supervision vs political interference"
    - "municipal enterprises vs regulatory neutrality"
    - "metropolitan coordination vs municipal identity"
    - "local participation vs representative government"
    - "territorial reform vs community continuity"
    - "emergency centralization vs local knowledge"
    - "local innovation vs minimum national rights standards"

  shortest_maxim:
    "地方自治法第一眼不是『市長是民選的所以可以決定』；先問『哪個 local authority、哪項 competence 從哪個法源來、這是 own 還是 delegated function、誰出錢、中央監督到哪裡、哪個地方機關真正有決策權』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 95.1 第一眼｜STATE STRUCTURE → LOCAL ENTITY → TERRITORY → COMPETENCE → ORGAN → LEGAL INSTRUMENT → FINANCE → SERVICE → SUPERVISION → COOPERATION → REMEDY

```text
STATE STRUCTURE
＝unitary？federal？regionalized？

LOCAL ENTITY
＝municipality？county？district？city-region？

TERRITORY
＝boundary / population / merger?

COMPETENCE
＝own / exclusive？shared？delegated？

ORGAN
＝council？mayor？executive？administration？

LEGAL INSTRUMENT
＝by-law？ordinance？administrative act？contract？

FINANCE
＝tax？fee？transfer？grant？borrowing？

SERVICE
＝water？waste？planning？transport？social service？

SUPERVISION
＝legality review？approval？substitution？audit？

COOPERATION
＝inter-municipal？metropolitan？cross-border？

REMEDY
＝local authority challenge？citizen challenge？constitutional court？
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 95.33 證據特徵

- constitution；
- local-government act / charter；
- territorial map；
- delegation instrument；
- by-laws；
- council minutes；
- mayoral authority；
- budget；
- tax / fee authority；
- intergovernmental-transfer formula；
- grant conditions；
- municipal debt；
- municipal-company charter；
- interlocal agreement；
- State supervision order；
- audit；
- election / conflict disclosures；
- emergency order；
- judicial challenge。

核心：

> **地方自治案不能只看「這是地方事務」；要問誰把它定義成地方事務、地方哪個 organ 有權做、錢從哪裡來，以及中央現在是在審合法性、執行委託還是直接取代地方政治判斷。**

## POSITIVE_LAW_GATE｜SOURCE-DERIVED / NOT RE-VERIFIED IN BUILD01

> **法位提醒：以下段落直接承接來源卡；BUILD01 未執行 web/current-law refresh。若案件依賴條約狀態、生效日期、國內轉置或最新法規，主系統必須觸發 current-law gate。**

以下必須依 State structure、entity、function、date 現查：

# DLM-125｜國家成立／承認法

```yaml
RUNTIME_CARD:
  DLM_ID: "DLM-125"
  DOMAIN_NAME: "國家成立／承認法"
  PRIMARY_SUBJECT: "A"
  INTERNAL_PLANE: "FOUNDATION_AUTHORIZATION"
  STRUCTURAL_ROLE: "STATE_IDENTITY_SPECIALIZATION"
  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_SPECIALIZATION"

  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_II_DLM_115_183"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# G2-25｜DLM-125｜國家成立／承認法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_II/V0.61-EXP/B42"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "B:司法／爭端／強制裁判"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別處理國家成立／承認、國家繼承與國籍連結；都涉及國家身分，但法律主體與效果不可互換。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-125-STATEHOOD_RECOGNITION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "國家成立／承認法"
  english_name: "Statehood / Recognition Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一個entity在international law上是否具有Statehood；其population、territory、government／effective authority與capacity for international relations等要素如何被證明；self-determination、secession、occupation、foreign recognition與illegality如何影響分析；recognition of State與recognition of government各自產生什麼法律／實務效果；UN admission、observer status、bilateral recognition與declaration of independence又為何不能互相代替？"

  historical_reason:
    "國際法大量規則都預設先知道『誰是State』：條約締結、外交關係、主權、豁免、國家責任、UN membership、war與peace都依此展開。但Statehood沒有一個全球中央登記機關。Montevideo Convention Article 1提供著名的四要素表述，卻是區域條約；recognition在實務上極重要，卻又不能簡化為『被多少國承認就算國家』。殖民解放、國家解體、單方獨立宣言、長期未承認實體與外國占領，更迫使分析分開Statehood、recognition、self-determination、territorial integrity與international-organization membership。"

  specialization_note:
    relation_to_dlm_006:
      "DLM-006處理國際公法的一般actor/source架構；本卡把Statehood、recognition、declaration of independence、UN membership與government recognition專門化。"
    relation_to_dlm_048:
      "DLM-048處理個人的nationality/citizenship；DLM-125處理entity是否為State以及誰代表它。國民身分存在不等於Statehood自動成立。"
    relation_to_dlm_120:
      "DLM-120處理international organizations；UN membership是組織會員資格，不是由組織單獨創造Statehood的萬用證書。"
    status: "SPECIALIZATION_REFINEMENT_CANDIDATE"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "effectiveness vs legality"
    - "declaratory theory vs constitutive effects of recognition"
    - "self-determination vs territorial integrity"
    - "defined territory vs unsettled boundaries"
    - "effective government vs civil war / occupation"
    - "recognition of State vs recognition of government"
    - "political recognition vs legal qualification"
    - "bilateral recognition vs multilateral membership"
    - "State continuity vs temporary governmental collapse"
    - "non-recognition duties vs factual control"
    - "secession claims vs no general automatic right to Statehood"
    - "legal personality vs practical access to courts / assets / diplomacy"

  shortest_maxim:
    "國家成立／承認法第一眼不是『宣布獨立了』『很多國承認了』或『進了某個國際機構』；先把Statehood criteria、territorial/effective facts、legality與recognition分開，再問UN membership、government representation與具體法律效果。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-25.1 第一眼｜ENTITY → TERRITORY → POPULATION → GOVERNMENT / EFFECTIVITY → EXTERNAL CAPACITY → LEGALITY / SELF-DETERMINATION → RECOGNITION → UN / IO STATUS → REPRESENTATION → CONSEQUENCES

```text
ENTITY
＝what political unit is claimed?

TERRITORY
＝what territory is claimed / controlled?

POPULATION
＝stable population nexus?

GOVERNMENT / EFFECTIVITY
＝who exercises authority?

EXTERNAL CAPACITY
＝capacity to conduct international relations?

LEGALITY / SELF-DETERMINATION
＝colonial context? occupation? unlawful force? secession?

RECOGNITION
＝which States recognize what: State or government?

UN / IO STATUS
＝member? observer? applicant? no status?

REPRESENTATION
＝who speaks for entity in international forums?

CONSEQUENCES
＝treaties, immunity, assets, courts, nationality, succession?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-25.24 Evidence architecture

- constitutional / founding instruments；
- declaration of independence；
- territorial maps and control evidence；
- population / residence evidence；
- governmental institutions；
- external-relations acts；
- bilateral recognition statements；
- derecognition / changed recognition policy；
- UN Security Council recommendations；
- General Assembly admission resolutions；
- credentials decisions；
- IO membership / observer documents；
- treaties concluded by entity；
- foreign-court judgments on entity status；
- occupation / annexation facts；
- self-determination context；
- relevant ICJ opinions / international practice。

核心：

> **Statehood不是票數，也不是一紙宣言；recognition不是創造所有法律事實的魔法。每一個效果都要回到「這個entity到底取得了哪一種法律資格」。**


## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別處理國家成立／承認、國家繼承與國籍連結；都涉及國家身分，但法律主體與效果不可互換。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-126-STATE_SUCCESSION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "國家繼承法"
  english_name: "State Succession Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "是否真的發生一State在某territory的international relations responsibility上被另一State取代的State succession；屬newly independent State、separation／secession、dissolution、unification或transfer of territory中的哪一類；既有treaties、boundaries、territorial regimes、State property、archives、debts、nationality、international-organization membership、claims與State-responsibility consequences哪些continuity、哪些clean slate、哪些需agreement或另有custom／lex specialis；相關Vienna Conventions與ILC materials目前各自有多少法律法位？"

  historical_reason:
    "國家不是公司股權過戶；當殖民地獨立、聯邦解體、兩國合併、領土移轉或新State分離時，整個國際法律網路都會同時問『舊State的條約、邊界、債務、檔案、資產、國籍、會員資格與責任是否跟著走』。不同題目的答案不一致。1978 Vienna Convention規範treaty succession並已生效但締約國有限；1983 property/archives/debts Convention至今未生效；nationality有ILC articles／GA材料；State responsibility succession的ILC專題更在2026年決定不再繼續。這使本域特別需要反對『successor inherits everything』與『clean slate erases everything』兩種穩定錯誤。"

  specialization_note:
    relation_to_dlm_118:
      "DLM-118處理一般treaty life cycle；DLM-126處理State succession事件對既有treaties的特殊影響。"
    relation_to_dlm_048:
      "DLM-048處理一般nationality/citizenship；DLM-126處理State succession造成nationality continuity、choice與statelessness risk。"
    relation_to_dlm_121:
      "DLM-121處理State responsibility一般規則；responsibility是否／如何succession不是ARSIWA自動回答，本域須另查。"
    status: "SPECIALIZATION_REFINEMENT_CANDIDATE"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "continuity vs clean slate"
    - "stability of legal relations vs sovereign consent"
    - "predecessor obligations vs successor autonomy"
    - "boundary stability vs political transformation"
    - "State property transfer vs creditor protection"
    - "archives continuity vs sovereign control"
    - "public debt continuity vs negotiated allocation"
    - "nationality continuity vs individual choice / statelessness prevention"
    - "IO institutional autonomy vs State continuity claims"
    - "customary law vs low-participation codification treaties"
    - "State succession vs mere government change"
    - "completed wrongs vs continuing responsibility"

  shortest_maxim:
    "國家繼承法第一眼不是『新國家接手舊國家的一切』或『獨立後全部歸零』；先證明真的發生State succession、分類是哪一型，再對treaty、boundary、property、archive、debt、nationality、membership與responsibility分軸找各自法源。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-26.1 第一眼｜SUCCESSION EVENT → CATEGORY → PREDECESSOR / SUCCESSOR → TERRITORY → TREATIES → BOUNDARIES → PROPERTY / ARCHIVES / DEBTS → NATIONALITY → IO MEMBERSHIP → RESPONSIBILITY → AGREEMENTS / PRACTICE

```text
SUCCESSION EVENT
＝replacement in responsibility for international relations of territory?

CATEGORY
＝newly independent? separation? dissolution? unification? transfer?

PREDECESSOR / SUCCESSOR
＝which State continues? which new State emerges?

TERRITORY
＝which territory is affected?

TREATIES
＝1978 Convention? custom? agreement?

BOUNDARIES
＝boundary / territorial regime continuity?

PROPERTY / ARCHIVES / DEBTS
＝agreement? domestic law? custom? 1983 Convention status?

NATIONALITY
＝who becomes whose national? statelessness risk?

IO MEMBERSHIP
＝new application or continuity under constituent instrument/practice?

RESPONSIBILITY
＝past / continuing wrongs? no automatic universal rule?

AGREEMENTS / PRACTICE
＝succession agreements, declarations, IO decisions, third-State acceptance
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-26.25 Evidence architecture

- predecessor / successor constitutional acts；
- date of succession；
- territory affected；
- independence / dissolution / unification instruments；
- succession agreements；
- recognition statements；
- UN / IO decisions；
- treaty depositary notifications；
- notification of succession；
- bilateral treaty correspondence；
- boundary treaties / maps；
- property inventories；
- archive inventories；
- debt schedules；
- nationality legislation；
- statelessness data；
- foreign-court decisions；
- claims registers；
- State responsibility practice；
- 1978 / 1983 Convention party status；
- ILC / GA materials。

核心：

> **State succession不是一個繼承按鈕。它是一個事件入口；每一類法律關係都要再問一次「什麼被接走、什麼沒被接走、誰同意、哪個法源、哪一天開始」。**

# PLANE｜EXTERNAL_SOVEREIGNTY


# DLM-001｜海洋法

```yaml
RUNTIME_CARD:
  DLM_ID: "DLM-001"
  DOMAIN_NAME: "海洋法"
  PRIMARY_SUBJECT: "A"
  INTERNAL_PLANE: "EXTERNAL_SOVEREIGNTY"
  STRUCTURAL_ROLE: "EXTERNAL_SPECIALIZATION"
  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_SPECIALIZATION"
  CURRENT_LAW_STATUS: "STABLE_METHOD_ONLY__POSITIVE_LAW_TO_VERIFY"
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_I_DLM_001_114"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# 4｜DLM-001｜海洋法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_I/V0.38-EXP/B01"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "H:土地／資源／基礎設施／物理移動"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

承擔海域管轄、武裝衝突、條約、外交領事、國際組織、國家豁免與武力使用等對外專門鏈。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-001-LAW_OF_THE_SEA"
  chinese_name: "海洋法"
  english_name: "Law of the Sea"

  domain_core_question:
    "海洋空間不是單一領土；國家、船舶、個人與國際社會在不同海域中，分別擁有什麼權利、管轄權、自由與義務？"

  historical_reason:
    "海洋同時具有交通、資源、安全、環境與全球公共空間性質。若只用陸地主權思維處理，會把所有海域錯看成領土；若只用公海自由思維處理，又會抹掉沿海國對近岸空間與資源的特殊利益。因此海洋法形成一套按海域、權能與用途分層配置主權、主權權利、管轄權與自由的制度。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "沿海國控制 vs 國際航行與海洋自由"
    - "主權／主權權利 vs 他國合法使用"
    - "資源開發 vs 海洋環境保護"
    - "安全利益 vs 航行、飛越與通過制度"
    - "國家管轄 vs 國際共同利益"
    - "海域空間分類 vs 具體活動功能"

  shortest_maxim:
    "海上第一問不是『誰擁有海』，而是『這是哪一種海域、哪一種權能、哪一種活動』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 4.1 第一眼

海洋法案件進來，第一步不是先問：

> 「這是不是某國領海？」

也不是先問：

> 「某國有沒有主權？」

第一步是把三個問題拆開：

```text
WHERE?
＝在哪一種海域？

WHO?
＝誰在主張權利／權力？

WHAT?
＝正在做什麼活動？
```

海域分類、主體與活動缺一不可。

同一艘船、同一個國家、同一個行為，換一個海域，法律資格可能完全不同。

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 4.7 證據特徵

海洋法案件常不是只靠證人說法。

重要材料可能包括：

- 座標與航跡；
- 海圖；
- 基線資料；
- AIS／VMS 等航行資料；
- 船旗、船籍與登記；
- 航海日誌；
- 衛星資料；
- 捕撈／作業紀錄；
- 執法命令與登臨紀錄；
- 海域公告；
- 海洋環境／污染監測；
- 國家聲明、外交照會與正式法律立場；
- 條約締約狀態與聲明。

核心：

> **海上的法律爭議常先是一個空間—活動—權能的證據問題。**

---

## POSITIVE_LAW_GATE｜SOURCE-DERIVED / NOT RE-VERIFIED IN BUILD01

> **法位提醒：以下段落直接承接來源卡；BUILD01 未執行 web/current-law refresh。若案件依賴條約狀態、生效日期、國內轉置或最新法規，主系統必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## 4.9 Positive Law Gate

以下任何一項未確認，不得靠心法完成個案結論：

1. 涉及國家是否為 UNCLOS 或其他相關條約締約方；
2. 是否有聲明、保留、選擇性排除或特別協定；
3. 海域具體法律性質及基線；
4. 船旗、船籍、港口及沿海國連結；
5. 國內法是否提供額外程序或執法依據；
6. 爭端解決機制與管轄；
7. 是否涉及特殊軍事、國安、漁業、污染或資源制度。

---

## FAILURE_MODES

## 4.8 常見誤分類

```yaml
COMMON_MISCLASSIFICATION:
  - "看到 200 海里就說『國家領海』。"
  - "看到外國軍艦／商船就直接推論有或沒有通行權。"
  - "看到海上執法就只問國內法授權，不問國際法上的管轄基礎。"
  - "看到漁業爭議就只當財產／行政案件，不先確認海域與資源權利。"
  - "看到海洋污染就只找環境法，不確認船旗國、沿海國、港口國與公約制度。"
  - "把海洋法與海商法混成同一個領域。"
```

# DLM-006｜國際公法

```yaml
RUNTIME_CARD:
  DLM_ID: "DLM-006"
  DOMAIN_NAME: "國際公法"
  PRIMARY_SUBJECT: "A"
  INTERNAL_PLANE: "EXTERNAL_SOVEREIGNTY"
  STRUCTURAL_ROLE: "EXTERNAL_MOTHER_ROUTER"
  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_NARROWED"
  CURRENT_LAW_STATUS: "STABLE_METHOD_ONLY__POSITIVE_LAW_TO_VERIFY"
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_I_DLM_001_114"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# 12｜DLM-006｜國際公法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_I/V0.38-EXP/B02"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "—"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留國際法法源、國際主體、一般義務與國家對外法律關係總路由；116、118–126及001承擔專門化問題。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-006-PUBLIC_INTERNATIONAL_LAW"
  chinese_name: "國際公法"
  english_name: "Public International Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "在沒有單一世界立法者、世界政府與一般強制法院的情況下，國家與其他國際法主體如何產生、承擔、爭執與執行國際法律義務？"

  historical_reason:
    "國家彼此主權獨立，卻無法在戰爭、外交、海洋、貿易、人權、環境與跨境風險中孤立生存。國際公法因此不是把國內法放大到世界，而是處理主權平等主體如何透過條約、習慣、制度與責任規則共同受法律約束。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "國家主權平等 vs 國際法律義務"
    - "國家同意 vs 國際共同利益"
    - "國內管轄 vs 國際責任"
    - "武力禁止 vs 自衛／集體安全等例外"
    - "條約拘束力 vs 國家政策自主"
    - "國際法形成的分散性 vs 法律確定性"
    - "國家責任 vs 個人刑事責任"

  shortest_maxim:
    "國際公法第一眼不是『哪個國家比較有理』；先問誰是國際法主體、哪個法源拘束它、誰有管轄、哪個行為能歸責。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 12.1 第一眼｜ACTOR → SOURCE → JURISDICTION → RESPONSIBILITY

國際公法案件先跑四步：

```text
ACTOR
＝國家？國際組織？個人？非國家行為者？

SOURCE
＝條約？習慣？一般法律原則？其他合法法源材料？

JURISDICTION / CONSENT
＝哪個法院／仲裁庭／機構有資格處理？

RESPONSIBILITY
＝行為能否歸責？義務是否違反？是否有排除不法性／抗辯？法律後果是什麼？
```

沒有管轄：

> **不等於沒有國際法義務。**

有國際法義務：

> **也不等於某一法院當然能審。**

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 12.10 證據特徵

國際公法的證據常同時是「事實」與「法源形成材料」：

- 條約文本；
- 批准／加入／生效資料；
- 保留與聲明；
- 外交照會；
- 國家官方聲明；
- 國內立法與行政實踐；
- 軍事／執法命令；
- 國際組織文件；
- 聯合國表決與發言；
- 國家長期實踐；
- 國際法院／仲裁裁判；
- 地圖、座標、邊界文件；
- 官方調查報告。

尤其習慣法分析中：

> **材料多不等於法源已閉合。**

還必須分辨：

```text
STATE PRACTICE
vs
OPINIO JURIS
```

---

## POSITIVE_LAW_GATE｜SOURCE-DERIVED / NOT RE-VERIFIED IN BUILD01

> **法位提醒：以下段落直接承接來源卡；BUILD01 未執行 web/current-law refresh。若案件依賴條約狀態、生效日期、國內轉置或最新法規，主系統必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## 12.12 Positive Law Gate

以下必須現查：

1. 條約締約狀態、批准／加入、生效日期；
2. 保留、聲明、退出、終止；
3. 條約對具體主體、時間、地域、事項是否適用；
4. 習慣國際法的實踐與 opinio juris 證據；
5. 國際法院／仲裁庭的管轄同意；
6. 國家豁免、外交豁免及國際組織豁免；
7. 國家責任與行為歸責；
8. 武力、自衛與安理會授權；
9. 國際組織設立文件與權限；
10. 國內法與國際法在特定法域內的接軌方式。

```yaml
  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_SPECIALIZATION"
  CURRENT_LAW_STATUS: "STABLE_METHOD_ONLY__POSITIVE_LAW_TO_VERIFY"
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_II_DLM_115_183"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# G2-3｜DLM-116｜國際人道法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_II/V0.61-EXP/B39"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "B:司法／爭端／強制裁判、E:人／家庭／勞動／社會關係"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

承擔海域管轄、武裝衝突、條約、外交領事、國際組織、國家豁免與武力使用等對外專門鏈。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-116-INTERNATIONAL_HUMANITARIAN_LAW"
  chinese_name: "國際人道法"
  english_name: "International Humanitarian Law / Law of Armed Conflict"

  domain_core_question:
    "事實是否已達international或non-international armed conflict門檻；哪些treaty/customary rules對哪些parties、persons、objects與territories適用；特定attack、detention、occupation、siege、weapon或humanitarian-access conduct是否符合distinction、proportionality、precaution、humane treatment與其他IHL規則？"

  historical_reason:
    "國際法無法保證戰爭不發生，因此發展出另一層規則：即使武力已經開始，交戰者也不能把敵人、平民、傷病者、戰俘、醫療設施與一切物體都視為無限制手段。IHL故意把『誰有權開戰』與『戰爭已存在時可以怎麼打』分開，使最低人道限制不依賴某一方是否政治正當、是否承認對手或是否被稱為恐怖組織。"

  specialization_note:
    relation_to_dlm_056:
      "DLM-056繼續處理國內國防組織、文人統制、部署授權、軍紀與軍事制度；本卡把armed-conflict classification與IHL substantive conduct獨立成專門第一眼。最終整合時應對DLM-056既有IHL段落作去重／路由審計。"
    status: "SPECIALIZATION_REFINEMENT_CANDIDATE"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "military necessity vs humanity"
    - "distinction vs battlefield uncertainty"
    - "military advantage vs incidental civilian harm"
    - "speed of attack vs feasible precautions"
    - "combatant/fighter status vs civilian protection"
    - "detention security vs humane treatment / review"
    - "occupation control vs sovereign title"
    - "siege/blockade pressure vs starvation prohibition"
    - "humanitarian access vs security screening"
    - "new technology vs existing principles"
    - "reciprocity instinct vs non-reciprocal humanitarian obligations"
    - "State responsibility vs individual criminal responsibility"

  shortest_maxim:
    "國際人道法第一眼不是『誰先開戰、誰比較邪惡』；先問『armed conflict是哪一類、這個人／物在IHL裡是什麼status、這個具體行為受哪條rule拘束、當時可合理取得哪些資訊、distinction／proportionality／precaution有沒有逐步成立』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-3.1 第一眼｜CONFLICT CLASSIFICATION → PARTIES → APPLICABLE SOURCES → PERSON / OBJECT STATUS → CONDUCT → HOSTILITIES / PROTECTION RULE → PRECAUTIONS → DETENTION / OCCUPATION → INVESTIGATION → RESPONSIBILITY

```text
CONFLICT CLASSIFICATION
＝IAC? NIAC? occupation? no armed conflict?

PARTIES
＝States? organized armed groups?

APPLICABLE SOURCES
＝GCs? AP I/II? customary IHL? special weapons treaty?

PERSON / OBJECT STATUS
＝civilian? combatant? POW? hors de combat? medical? military objective?

CONDUCT
＝attack? detention? transfer? destruction? starvation? pillage?

HOSTILITIES RULE
＝distinction? proportionality? precaution?

PROTECTION RULE
＝humane treatment? medical care? judicial guarantees?

DETENTION / OCCUPATION
＝authority? safeguards? effective control?

INVESTIGATION
＝what facts known at the time?

RESPONSIBILITY
＝State responsibility? discipline? war crime?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-3.37 Evidence architecture

- conflict chronology；
- identity/organization of parties；
- command structure；
- territorial control；
- weapons；
- target intelligence；
- target verification；
- civilian presence；
- expected harm estimate；
- military advantage assessment；
- warning；
- battle damage；
- medical/protected status；
- detention records；
- orders/ROE；
- occupation control facts；
- humanitarian-access requests；
- satellite / video / sensor data；
- witness testimony；
- treaty status；
- customary-law basis；
- contemporaneous decision records。

核心：

> **IHL不能只用屍體數量判斷，也不能只用軍方標籤判斷。它要求重建「衝突類型、目標status、當時資訊、預期傷害、軍事利益與可行預防」這一整條ex ante決策鏈。**

## G2-3.39 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依conflict、party、place、date、conduct現查：

1. IAC / NIAC classification；
2. occupation；
3. Geneva Convention applicability；
4. AP I / AP II party status；
5. customary IHL；
6. civilian/combatant/fighter status；
7. direct participation；
8. hors de combat；
9. POW；
10. military objective；
11. distinction；
12. indiscriminate attacks；
13. proportionality；
14. precautions；
15. warnings；
16. human shields；
17. medical protection；
18. cultural property；
19. detainee protection；
20. judicial guarantees；
21. occupation duties；
22. property destruction / pillage；
23. siege / blockade；
24. starvation；
25. humanitarian relief；
26. forced displacement；
27. weapons treaties；
28. Article 36 review；
29. cyber operations；
30. environment；
31. neutral States；
32. investigation；
33. State responsibility；
34. war-crime referral to DLM-117。

```yaml
RUNTIME_CARD:
  DLM_ID: "DLM-118"
  DOMAIN_NAME: "條約法"
  PRIMARY_SUBJECT: "A"
  INTERNAL_PLANE: "EXTERNAL_SOVEREIGNTY"
  STRUCTURAL_ROLE: "EXTERNAL_SPECIALIZATION"
  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_SPECIALIZATION"
  CURRENT_LAW_STATUS: "STABLE_METHOD_ONLY__POSITIVE_LAW_TO_VERIFY"
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_II_DLM_115_183"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# G2-9｜DLM-118｜條約法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_II/V0.61-EXP/B40"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "B:司法／爭端／強制裁判"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

承擔海域管轄、武裝衝突、條約、外交領事、國際組織、國家豁免與武力使用等對外專門鏈。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-118-LAW_OF_TREATIES"
  chinese_name: "條約法"
  english_name: "Law of Treaties"

  domain_core_question:
    "一份國際文書究竟是不是treaty；誰有權代表State／international organization；何時簽署、批准、加入或以其他方式表示consent to be bound；reservation／declaration如何改變關係；何時生效、如何解釋、與其他規範如何衝突；又在何種嚴格條件下可主張invalidity、termination、withdrawal或suspension？"

  historical_reason:
    "國際法沒有一個全球立法機關替所有國家制定共同契約。國家與其他國際法主體需要靠條約把合作、邊界、貿易、戰爭、人權、環境與組織權限轉成可追溯義務。條約越重要，就越不能只看標題或政治聲明；必須分清binding intent、consent、entry into force、reservation、interpretation、domestic implementation與termination。1969 Vienna Convention將大量既有與發展中的規則整理成一套條約生命週期，但它本身仍有party、time與subject scope。"

  specialization_note:
    relation_to_dlm_006:
      "DLM-006繼續處理國際公法的actor/source/jurisdiction/general responsibility；DLM-118把treaty formation、consent、reservations、interpretation、invalidity、termination與conflict專門化。最終整合時應對DLM-006條約段落作去重／路由審計。"
    status: "SPECIALIZATION_REFINEMENT_CANDIDATE"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "consent vs stability"
    - "text vs object and purpose"
    - "State sovereignty vs pacta sunt servanda"
    - "domestic constitutional limits vs international reliance"
    - "reservation flexibility vs treaty integrity"
    - "subsequent practice vs disguised amendment"
    - "treaty stability vs fundamental change"
    - "jus cogens vs party autonomy"
    - "conflicting treaties vs systemic coherence"
    - "multilingual authenticity vs semantic divergence"
    - "formal depositary practice vs substantive validity"
    - "treaty rules vs customary international law"

  shortest_maxim:
    "條約法第一眼不是『這份文件叫Convention／MOU所以一定有拘束力或一定沒有』；先問『誰以什麼權限對哪個文本表示了何種consent、何時對哪些parties生效、有哪些reservation／declaration、用哪套解釋規則讀、終止或失效的門檻是否真的成立』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-9.1 第一眼｜INSTRUMENT → PARTIES → AUTHORITY → CONSENT → RESERVATIONS → ENTRY INTO FORCE → APPLICATION → INTERPRETATION → CONFLICT → INVALIDITY / TERMINATION → PROCEDURE / DEPOSITARY

```text
INSTRUMENT
＝treaty? convention? protocol? exchange of notes? MOU?

PARTIES
＝States? IOs? both?

AUTHORITY
＝Head of State? government? foreign minister? full powers?

CONSENT
＝signature? ratification? accession? approval? exchange?

RESERVATIONS
＝reservation? interpretive declaration? objection?

ENTRY INTO FORCE
＝globally? for this party? provisionally?

APPLICATION
＝territory? time? subject matter?

INTERPRETATION
＝Art 31? 32? 33? subsequent agreement/practice?

CONFLICT
＝successive treaties? UN Charter Art 103? jus cogens?

INVALIDITY / TERMINATION
＝error? fraud? coercion? material breach? fundamental change?

PROCEDURE / DEPOSITARY
＝notification? objection? registration? records?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-9.33 Evidence architecture

- authoritative treaty text；
- authentic languages；
- signature page；
- ratification/accession instrument；
- full powers；
- depositary notifications；
- entry-into-force clause；
- reservation/declaration；
- objection/acceptance；
- withdrawal/denunciation notice；
- amendment/protocol；
- subsequent agreements；
- State practice；
- travaux；
- UN registration；
- domestic approval/incorporation；
- treaty-status database；
- court/tribunal interpretation。

核心：

> **條約案不能從「大家都知道這份公約存在」開始。要把文本、締約能力、consent、party status、entry into force、reservation、interpretation與termination逐層固定，才能知道某一句義務此刻究竟對誰成立。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "TREATY_STATUS_VERIFIED__STATE_PARTY_OPTIONAL_PROTOCOL_AND_MISSION_FACTS_CHECK_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "international/current framework status only; domestic merits not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

VCDR and VCCR remain in force. Current UNTC status records 193 VCDR Parties and 182 VCCR Parties. The compulsory-settlement Optional Protocols have materially narrower participation (70 and 52 Parties), so treaty membership cannot be inferred from membership in the base conventions.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **UN Treaty Collection — Vienna Convention on Diplomatic Relations**
  - URL: https://treaties.un.org/pages/viewdetails.aspx?chapter=3&clang=_en&mtdsg_no=iii-3&src=treaty
  - Verified point: Status 2026-08-06: in force; 193 Parties.
- **UN Treaty Collection — Vienna Convention on Consular Relations**
  - URL: https://treaties.un.org/Pages/ShowMTDSGDetails.aspx?chapter=3&lang=en&mtdsg_no=III-6&src=UNTSONLINE&tabid=2
  - Verified point: Status 2026-08-13: in force; 182 Parties.
- **UN Treaty Collection — VCDR Optional Protocol on Compulsory Settlement**
  - URL: https://treaties.un.org/Pages/ShowMTDSGDetails.aspx?chapter=3&lang=en&mtdsg_no=III-5&src=UNTSONLINE&tabid=2
  - Verified point: Status 2026-08-13: in force; 70 Parties.
- **UN Treaty Collection — VCCR Optional Protocol on Compulsory Settlement**
  - URL: https://treaties.un.org/Pages/ViewDetails.aspx?chapter=3&clang=_en&mtdsg_no=III-8&src=TREATY
  - Verified point: Status late July 2026: in force; 52 Parties.

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

承擔海域管轄、武裝衝突、條約、外交領事、國際組織、國家豁免與武力使用等對外專門鏈。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-119-DIPLOMATIC_CONSULAR_RELATIONS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "外交／領事關係法"
  english_name: "Diplomatic / Consular Relations Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "某人／場所／文件究竟具有外交還是領事status；sending State與receiving State間的appointment、agrément、notification與persona non grata程序是否成立；其personal inviolability、premises inviolability、criminal/civil jurisdiction immunity、functional immunity、tax/customs privileges、communications與waiver究竟到哪裡；又何時因任務終了、國籍／居留、家屬資格或濫用而改變？"

  historical_reason:
    "國家要能和平共存，必須讓彼此代表在外國境內安全溝通、談判、保護國民與執行官方職務。這要求receiving State在自己的領土與司法權中讓出一部分空間；但這些privileges and immunities不是把外交官變成境外主權者或無責特權階級，而是為了確保mission與consular functions獨立運作。外交與領事制度功能不同，因此其人身、場所與司法豁免程度也不同。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "receiving-State sovereignty vs mission independence"
    - "personal immunity vs accountability"
    - "premises inviolability vs law enforcement"
    - "confidential communications vs abuse prevention"
    - "family privileges vs personal conduct"
    - "diplomatic status vs private commercial activity"
    - "consular protection vs host-State criminal process"
    - "waiver vs sending-State institutional interests"
    - "persona non grata discretion vs diplomatic stability"
    - "functional necessity vs privilege inflation"
    - "national protection vs dual-nationality complications"
    - "mission security vs protest / public order"

  shortest_maxim:
    "外交／領事法第一眼不是『他有外交護照所以不能碰』；先問『他究竟是哪種official status、何時被receiving State接受／通知、這項行為是官方還是私人、適用的是personal immunity還是functional immunity、誰能waive、任務是否已終止』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-10.1 第一眼｜RELATION → STATUS → ACCREDITATION / NOTIFICATION → PERSON / PREMISES / COMMUNICATION → IMMUNITY TYPE → CONDUCT → WAIVER → PERSONA NON GRATA → TERMINATION → REMEDY / STATE RESPONSIBILITY

```text
RELATION
＝diplomatic mission? consular post? special mission? IO representation?

STATUS
＝ambassador? diplomatic agent? admin staff? consul? honorary consul? family?

ACCREDITATION
＝agrément? appointment? exequatur/notification?

PERSON / PREMISES
＝person? residence? embassy? consulate? archives? bag?

IMMUNITY TYPE
＝criminal? civil/admin? functional? inviolability?

CONDUCT
＝official act? private commercial act? traffic? employment?

WAIVER
＝who may waive? express?

PERSONA NON GRATA
＝receiving State response?

TERMINATION
＝mission ended? reasonable period? residual official-act immunity?

REMEDY
＝sending-State prosecution? waiver? expulsion? State claim?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-10.30 Evidence architecture

- diplomatic list；
- accreditation note；
- agrément；
- appointment letter；
- receiving-State notification；
- identity/status card；
- mission staff category；
- family/household status；
- nationality/residency；
- act official/private；
- mission premises records；
- bag/courier documentation；
- waiver communication；
- persona non grata notice；
- end-of-function date；
- consular exequatur/notification；
- detention notification；
- host/sending State correspondence；
- VCDR/VCCR party/reservation status。

核心：

> **外交豁免案真正的第一個問題不是「事情嚴不嚴重」，而是「這個人在這個時間、這個角色、對這個行為究竟有哪一種immunity」。嚴重性可以觸發外交反應、waiver要求或sending-State責任，但不能跳過status與scope。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "CURRENT_FRAMEWORK_VERIFIED__ORGANIZATION_INSTRUMENT_HOST_AGREEMENT_AND_TREATY_STATUS_CHECK_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "international/current framework status only; domestic merits not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

International-organization privileges/capacity cannot be globalized into one code. The 1946 UN privileges convention is in force with 162 Parties, while the 1975 Convention on State representation before international organizations still had only 34 Parties and was not yet in force in August 2026.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **UN Treaty Collection — Convention on the Privileges and Immunities of the United Nations**
  - URL: https://treaties.un.org/Pages/ViewDetails.aspx?chapter=3&clang=_en&mtdsg_no=III-1&src=IND
  - Verified point: Status 2026-07-19: in force; 162 Parties.
- **UN Treaty Collection — 1975 Vienna Convention on Representation of States in Relations with International Organizations**
  - URL: https://treaties.un.org/Pages/ViewDetails.aspx?chapter=3&clang=_en&mtdsg_no=III-11&src=IND
  - Verified point: Status 2026-08-06: NOT YET IN FORCE; 34 Parties; Article 89 requires 35 instruments.

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

承擔海域管轄、武裝衝突、條約、外交領事、國際組織、國家豁免與武力使用等對外專門鏈。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-120-INTERNATIONAL_ORGANIZATIONS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "國際組織法"
  english_name: "International Organizations Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "某個entity是否是international organization、其constituent instrument授予哪些express/implied powers、哪個organ有competence作哪種act、該act對members／non-members／individuals有何法律效果；organization、members、officials與representatives各享哪些privileges/immunities；wrongful conduct如何歸責、由organization或member State負責；內部行政、人事與外部爭端由哪個forum處理？"

  historical_reason:
    "國家把和平安全、衛生、金融、勞工、航空、通訊、發展與司法等持續性功能交給常設國際組織後，出現一種不同於State的國際法主體：它沒有一般主權，只有constituent instrument與necessary functions所賦予的powers。國際組織法因此一方面讓組織取得足夠的legal personality、privileges與institutional autonomy，另一方面必須防止『國際』二字使組織的competence、責任、staff accountability與access to justice變成不可審查黑箱。"

  specialization_note:
    relation_to_dlm_006:
      "DLM-006保留general public-international-law actor/source/jurisdiction framework；DLM-120專門處理constituent instruments、organizational powers、organs, membership, legal personality, privileges/immunities, staff/internal justice與IO responsibility。"
    status: "SPECIALIZATION_REFINEMENT_CANDIDATE"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "functional autonomy vs member-State control"
    - "implied powers vs principle of attributed competence"
    - "institutional effectiveness vs legality"
    - "organizational immunity vs access to justice"
    - "staff independence vs accountability"
    - "collective decision vs individual member responsibility"
    - "political organs vs legal limits"
    - "internal law vs external international law"
    - "host-State sovereignty vs headquarters independence"
    - "member financing/control vs separate legal personality"
    - "organizational responsibility vs State responsibility"
    - "global public functions vs fragmented constituent instruments"

  shortest_maxim:
    "國際組織法第一眼不是『UN／WHO／某國際機構說了，所以有法律』；先問『它是哪一個organization、constituent instrument給哪個organ什麼competence、這份resolution／decision對誰有何legal effect、privilege／immunity服務哪個function、責任最後歸到organization還是member State』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-11.1 第一眼｜ENTITY → CONSTITUENT INSTRUMENT → MEMBERSHIP → ORGANS → COMPETENCE → ACT / DECISION → LEGAL EFFECT → PERSONALITY / PRIVILEGES → STAFF / INTERNAL LAW → RESPONSIBILITY → DISPUTE SETTLEMENT

```text
ENTITY
＝IO? specialized agency? regional organization? NGO?

CONSTITUENT INSTRUMENT
＝Charter? Constitution? treaty?

MEMBERSHIP
＝who joined? conditions? suspension/withdrawal?

ORGANS
＝assembly? council? secretariat? court? agency?

COMPETENCE
＝express? implied? delegated?

ACT / DECISION
＝resolution? regulation? recommendation? sanction? administrative act?

LEGAL EFFECT
＝binding on members? internal? recommendatory? external?

PERSONALITY / PRIVILEGES
＝legal capacity? immunity? host agreement?

STAFF / INTERNAL LAW
＝independence? employment? ethics? tribunal?

RESPONSIBILITY
＝organization? member State? dual attribution?

DISPUTE SETTLEMENT
＝internal tribunal? arbitration? ICJ advisory? domestic court?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-11.36 Evidence architecture

- constituent instrument；
- membership instrument；
- organ rules；
- meeting record；
- voting record；
- resolution/decision text；
- legal basis article；
- delegation of powers；
- internal rules；
- headquarters agreement；
- privileges convention；
- staff status；
- waiver；
- employment/tribunal rules；
- operation command/control；
- budget/financing；
- contract；
- responsibility claim；
- DARIO/customary sources；
- domestic implementing legislation；
- ICJ/other tribunal jurisprudence。

核心：

> **國際組織案最危險的句型是「UN／WHO／某組織決定了」。法律分析必須把那句拆成：哪個organ、哪個competence、什麼procedure、哪個text、對誰binding、什麼source、誰負責。組織的institutional authority不能替每個具體act自動封聖。**

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-121-STATE_RESPONSIBILITY_LAW"
  chinese_name: "國家責任法"
  english_name: "State Responsibility Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "某一conduct是否依國際法可歸責於State；當時對該State生效的哪一項primary obligation是否被breached；是否存在consent、self-defence、countermeasure、force majeure、distress、necessity等circumstance precluding wrongfulness；若responsibility成立，cessation、non-repetition、restitution、compensation、satisfaction與invocation/countermeasure如何配置？"

  historical_reason:
    "國際法的primary rules告訴國家『應該做什麼／不得做什麼』，但不同條約、人權、領土、外交、環境與戰爭規則如果各自重建一套責任後果，整個國際法會碎裂。國家責任法因此發展成一套secondary rules：不先決定原義務內容，而回答conduct何時算State的、何時構成breach、何時wrongfulness暫被排除、違法後要停止什麼、修復什麼、誰有資格invoke。"

  specialization_note:
    relation_to_dlm_006:
      "DLM-006保留general public-international-law source/actor框架；DLM-121把attribution、breach、circumstances precluding wrongfulness、reparation、invocation、countermeasures與serious breaches專門化。"
    status: "SPECIALIZATION_REFINEMENT_CANDIDATE"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "State continuity vs changing governments"
    - "attribution vs private autonomy"
    - "primary obligation vs secondary responsibility"
    - "wrongfulness vs excuses/justifications"
    - "full reparation vs practical impossibility"
    - "injured State rights vs collective obligations"
    - "countermeasures vs escalation"
    - "necessity vs abuse"
    - "responsibility vs immunity"
    - "State responsibility vs individual criminal responsibility"
    - "lex specialis vs general secondary rules"
    - "codification influence vs non-treaty status of ARSIWA"

  shortest_maxim:
    "國家責任法第一眼不是『壞事發生在國家境內，所以國家負責』；先問『這個conduct能不能歸責給State、當時到底違反哪項international obligation、wrongfulness是否被排除、責任後果與可主張者各自是誰』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-16.1 第一眼｜PRIMARY OBLIGATION → CONDUCT → ATTRIBUTION → BREACH → TEMPORAL LINK → PRECLUSION → CESSATION → REPARATION → INVOCATION → COUNTERMEASURE / COLLECTIVE CONSEQUENCE

```text
PRIMARY OBLIGATION
＝treaty? custom? jus cogens? unilateral obligation?

CONDUCT
＝act? omission? legislation? judgment? operation?

ATTRIBUTION
＝organ? entity exercising public authority? controlled actor? insurrection?

BREACH
＝what exact obligation? what standard? when?

TEMPORAL LINK
＝obligation in force at time? continuing/composite act?

PRECLUSION
＝consent? self-defence? countermeasure? force majeure? distress? necessity?

CESSATION
＝ongoing wrongful conduct must stop?

REPARATION
＝restitution? compensation? satisfaction?

INVOCATION
＝injured State? non-injured State protecting collective interest?

COUNTERMEASURE
＝lawful conditions? proportionality? reversible purpose?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-16.35 Evidence architecture

- exact primary rule；
- treaty/custom status；
- temporal applicability；
- State organ/entity status；
- domestic delegation；
- instructions/control；
- conduct chronology；
- omission/due diligence evidence；
- consent；
- peril/necessity evidence；
- prior wrongful act for countermeasure；
- notice/demand to comply；
- cessation；
- damage/cause；
- valuation；
- assurances；
- third-State recognition/assistance；
- relevant jurisprudence on customary status。

核心：

> **國家責任法不能被「國家應該負責」這句話取代。真正的責任鏈只有在 attribution 與 breach 先成立後，才輪到preclusion、reparation、invocation與countermeasure。**

# DLM-122｜國家豁免法

```yaml
RUNTIME_CARD:
  DLM_ID: "DLM-122"
  DOMAIN_NAME: "國家豁免法"
  PRIMARY_SUBJECT: "A"
  INTERNAL_PLANE: "EXTERNAL_SOVEREIGNTY"
  STRUCTURAL_ROLE: "EXTERNAL_SPECIALIZATION"
  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_SPECIALIZATION"

  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_II_DLM_115_183"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# G2-17｜DLM-122｜國家豁免法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_II/V0.61-EXP/B41"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "B:司法／爭端／強制裁判、D:組織／企業／市場"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

承擔海域管轄、武裝衝突、條約、外交領事、國際組織、國家豁免與武力使用等對外專門鏈。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-122-STATE_IMMUNITY_LAW"
  chinese_name: "國家豁免法"
  english_name: "State Immunity Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "foreign domestic court面前的respondent／property是否屬State或受State-immunity law保護；conduct是sovereign/public act還是commercial/private-law act；是否存在treaty/domestic/customary exception、express consent或submission；即使court可判，pre-judgment／post-judgment measures of constraint與specific State property是否仍享更強保護？"

  historical_reason:
    "主權平等使一國法院原則上不應像審普通私人那樣審判另一主權國家。隨著國家大量進入商業、僱傭、航運、投資與市場交易，絕對豁免逐步在許多法域發展成restrictive immunity：sovereign acts仍受保護，commercial activity可能例外。但『能不能審』與『能不能扣國家財產』始終是兩個門，且外交官豁免、國家官員刑事豁免、國際組織豁免也不是同一制度。"

  specialization_note:
    relation_to_dlm_119:
      "DLM-119處理diplomatic/consular personal and mission immunities；DLM-122處理State as defendant及State property在foreign domestic courts中的jurisdictional immunity與immunity from measures of constraint。"
    status: "NEW_SPECIALIZED_DOMAIN"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "sovereign equality vs access to court"
    - "public acts vs commercial conduct"
    - "substantive gravity vs procedural immunity"
    - "jurisdiction immunity vs enforcement immunity"
    - "State enterprise separateness vs State control"
    - "arbitration consent vs execution protection"
    - "victim remedy vs foreign sovereign property"
    - "central-bank independence vs creditor enforcement"
    - "waiver certainty vs implied submission"
    - "territorial tort exceptions vs sovereign armed-forces conduct"
    - "domestic restrictive-immunity statutes vs customary law"
    - "future treaty codification vs current non-entry into force"

  shortest_maxim:
    "國家豁免法第一眼不是『這國家做錯事，所以可以告』或『它是國家所以永遠不能告』；先問『誰是被告、哪一個conduct、foreign court依哪個immunity source、有沒有commercial／employment／tort等exception或consent，最後判決能不能對哪一項State property執行』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-17.1 第一眼｜DEFENDANT → SOURCE → ACT / TRANSACTION → JURISDICTION IMMUNITY → EXCEPTION / CONSENT → PROCEEDINGS → JUDGMENT → PRE-JUDGMENT CONSTRAINT → POST-JUDGMENT EXECUTION → PROPERTY CATEGORY

```text
DEFENDANT
＝foreign State? ministry? central bank? SOE? official?

SOURCE
＝custom? domestic statute? treaty? regional law?

ACT
＝sovereign/public? commercial? employment? tort?

JURISDICTION IMMUNITY
＝court may hear?

EXCEPTION / CONSENT
＝commercial? arbitration? express waiver? submission?

PROCEEDINGS
＝service? default? appearance only to assert immunity?

JUDGMENT
＝merits reached?

PRE-JUDGMENT CONSTRAINT
＝attachment before judgment?

POST-JUDGMENT EXECUTION
＝can assets be seized?

PROPERTY CATEGORY
＝commercial-use asset? embassy? military? central-bank reserve?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-17.32 Evidence architecture

- exact defendant legal identity；
- ministry/agency/SOE status；
- State ownership/control；
- nature/purpose of transaction；
- contract/arbitration clause；
- express waiver；
- court appearance；
- commercial activity；
- employment role；
- tort location/context；
- property ownership；
- property use/intended use；
- embassy/central-bank/military designation；
- pre/post judgment measure；
- applicable domestic immunity statute；
- customary-law evidence；
- 2004 Convention party/status；
- foreign judgment record。

核心：

> **國家豁免案一定要至少做兩次分類：第一次問court可不可以審；第二次問就算判了，這一項specific State property可不可以被扣。任何只寫『State waived immunity』而不說waive哪一層，都還沒完成。**

```yaml
  DLM_ID: "DLM-124"
  DOMAIN_NAME: "武力使用／自衛與集體安全法"

  STRUCTURAL_ROLE: "EXTERNAL_SPECIALIZATION"

  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# G2-24｜DLM-124｜武力使用／自衛與集體安全法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_II/V0.61-EXP/B42"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "B:司法／爭端／強制裁判、E:人／家庭／勞動／社會關係"
```


## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "UN_CHARTER_FRAMEWORK_VERIFIED__FACT_SPECIFIC_SECURITY_COUNCIL_PRACTICE_CHECK_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "international/current framework status only; domestic merits not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

The Charter framework remains controlling: Article 51 is treated by the Security Council Repertoire as an exception to Article 2(4) in the event of armed attack, with immediate reporting to the Council. Whether a particular use of force is lawful remains intensely fact- and practice-specific.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **UN Security Council Repertoire — Article 51 / Chapter VII**
  - URL: https://main.un.org/securitycouncil/en/content/repertoire/actions
  - Verified point: Current Repertoire page: Article 51 is the Charter self-defence exception; measures must be reported to the Security Council.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列框架／條約狀態。若案件答案取決於特定國家、保留／聲明、領域範圍、生效日期、清單、主管機關或個案事實，仍必須在 runtime 重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

承擔海域管轄、武裝衝突、條約、外交領事、國際組織、國家豁免與武力使用等對外專門鏈。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-124-USE_OF_FORCE_SELF_DEFENCE_COLLECTIVE_SECURITY_LAW"
  chinese_name: "武力使用／自衛與集體安全法"
  english_name: "Law on the Use of Force / Self-Defence / Collective Security"

  domain_core_question:
    "一個State或受其控制／授權的actor所作conduct是否構成international relations中的threat or use of force；若原則上受UN Charter Article 2(4)禁止，是否存在Security Council authorization、individual／collective self-defence、valid consent／invitation或其他可識別法律基礎；若主張self-defence，armed attack、necessity、proportionality、collective-defence request、reporting與temporal scope是否成立；使用武力合法性與後續IHL、State responsibility及個人crime of aggression又如何分離？"

  historical_reason:
    "1945年以後的國際秩序不再把戰爭視為State的一般政策工具。UN Charter把threat or use of force置於原則禁止之下，同時保留Security Council的collective-security權限與Article 51的individual／collective self-defence。現代爭議真正困難之處，不是找到『自衛』『反恐』『保護人民』『受邀』『維和』等政治標籤，而是確認哪一個法律入口真的存在、其條件有沒有被具體事實填滿，以及使用武力的開始合法性不能替戰場行為、國家責任或個人刑責提前背書。"

  specialization_note:
    relation_to_dlm_006:
      "DLM-006保留國際公法母卡；本卡把prohibition of force、Security Council collective security、self-defence、consent/invitation與相關爭議專門化。"
    relation_to_dlm_055_056:
      "DLM-055／056處理國內國安、國防組織、部署與軍事制度；本卡處理跨國武力使用在international law上的外部合法性。"
    relation_to_dlm_116:
      "DLM-124處理jus ad bellum／use-of-force legality；DLM-116處理armed conflict存在後的IHL。合法自衛不使每一attack合法，非法開戰也不取消IHL保護。"
    status: "SPECIALIZATION_REFINEMENT_CANDIDATE"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "prohibition of force vs inherent self-defence"
    - "sovereign equality vs collective security"
    - "armed attack threshold vs lower-level force"
    - "necessity vs strategic preference"
    - "proportionality vs punishment / retaliation"
    - "individual self-defence vs collective self-defence"
    - "State consent vs coercion / contested authority"
    - "Security Council authorization vs unilateral interpretation"
    - "territorial sovereignty vs cross-border non-State threats"
    - "anticipatory defence claims vs Charter text / contested custom"
    - "humanitarian protection vs unilateral force"
    - "regional enforcement vs Security Council primacy"
    - "jus ad bellum vs IHL"
    - "State responsibility vs individual aggression liability"

  shortest_maxim:
    "武力使用法第一眼不是『有威脅所以能打』，也不是『任何跨境武力都沒有例外』；先問Article 2(4)是否被觸發，再逐一找Security Council authorization、Article 51 self-defence或valid consent，最後把armed attack、necessity、proportionality、request、reporting、scope與後續IHL各自證明。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-24.1 第一眼｜ACTOR → CONDUCT → FORCE / THREAT → TERRITORIAL NEXUS → PROHIBITION → CLAIMED LEGAL BASIS → CONDITIONS → SCOPE → REPORTING → IHL / RESPONSIBILITY

```text
ACTOR
＝State armed forces? proxy? non-State actor? regional organization?

CONDUCT
＝kinetic strike? blockade? incursion? cyber operation? threat?

FORCE / THREAT
＝Article 2(4) threshold?

TERRITORIAL NEXUS
＝whose territory / sovereignty is affected?

PROHIBITION
＝prima facie prohibited use/threat of force?

CLAIMED LEGAL BASIS
＝Security Council? self-defence? consent/invitation?

CONDITIONS
＝armed attack? necessity? proportionality? valid request/consent?

SCOPE
＝target, geography, duration, means?

REPORTING
＝Article 51 report where self-defence claimed?

AFTER FORCE STARTS
＝IHL classification / State responsibility / individual criminal law separately
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-24.26 Evidence architecture

- exact UN Charter provision；
- Security Council resolutions / presidential statements；
- Article 51 letters to Security Council；
- victim State statements / request；
- consent / invitation documents；
- troop deployment dates；
- geographic scope；
- attack origin / attribution evidence；
- scale / effects；
- necessity evidence；
- proportionality rationale；
- regional organization decisions；
- relevant treaty obligations；
- ICJ judgments / advisory opinions；
- State practice / opinio juris for contested doctrines；
- IHL classification after hostilities begin。

核心：

> **「國安」「自衛」「反恐」「救人」都只是主張入口，不是法律結論。武力取得合法性，必須讓具體法律基礎與具體事實一節一節接上。**

以下必須按事件日期、States、resolution與具體conduct現查：

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "CURRENT_FRAMEWORK_VERIFIED__DOMESTIC_PROCUREMENT_LAW_AND_GPA_COVERAGE_CHECK_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "international/current framework status only; domestic merits not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

UNCITRAL's 2011 Model Law remains a reform model, not direct domestic law; UNCITRAL records enactments based on/influenced by it in 26 States and warns of domestic variation. GPA obligations apply only to covered procurement of GPA Parties/schedules.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **UNCITRAL Model Law on Public Procurement (2011)**
  - URL: https://uncitral.un.org/en/texts/procurement/modellaw/public_procurement
  - Verified point: Current official page: Model Law remains the procurement reform baseline; procurement decisions/actions can be challenged by potential suppliers.
- **UNCITRAL — Status of Model Law on Public Procurement (2011)**
  - URL: https://uncitral.un.org/en/texts/procurement/modellaw/public_procurement/status
  - Verified point: Current official status: legislation based on/influenced by Model Law in 26 States; domestic deviations must be checked.
- **WTO — GPA 2012 legal text**
  - URL: https://www.wto.org/english/docs_e/legal_e/gpa_e.htm
  - Verified point: Article XVIII requires timely, effective, transparent and non-discriminatory administrative or judicial review for covered procurement.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列框架／條約狀態。若案件答案取決於特定國家、保留／聲明、領域範圍、生效日期、清單、主管機關或個案事實，仍必須在 runtime 重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別處理政府採購、移民／難民與海關；都是國家對外部人／市場／邊境的公權力接口。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-021-PUBLIC_PROCUREMENT_LAW"
  chinese_name: "政府採購法"
  english_name: "Public Procurement Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "國家以公共資金購買工程、財物或服務時，如何把行政需求轉化成可競爭、可比較、可追責的採購程序，同時防止偏袒、圍標、腐敗、規格綁標與事後契約失控？"

  historical_reason:
    "私人買家可以基於個人偏好選擇交易相對人，但政府使用的是公共資源，且採購決策可能影響市場競爭、產業政策、公共工程品質與貪腐風險。因此政府採購法把『買東西』轉化成一套需要需求正當性、公開／競爭、資格、評選、紀錄、救濟與履約監督的公共程序。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "效率／急迫性 vs 公平競爭"
    - "最低價格 vs 品質／最佳價值"
    - "機關專業裁量 vs 可審查透明"
    - "規格需求 vs 綁標風險"
    - "採購彈性 vs 程序一致"
    - "本國政策目標 vs 國際不歧視義務"
    - "反腐敗 vs 過度程序成本"
    - "決標完成 vs 履約品質與變更控制"
    - "商業機密 vs 採購透明"

  shortest_maxim:
    "政府採購第一眼不是『誰報價最低』；先問『機關憑什麼買、用什麼程序買、規格有沒有預先排除競爭、評選標準能不能被事前知道與事後追溯』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 27.1 第一眼｜NEED → AUTHORITY → METHOD → SPECIFICATION → COMPETITION → EVALUATION → AWARD → PERFORMANCE

```text
NEED
＝機關真正需要什麼？

AUTHORITY / BUDGET
＝有無預算與採購權限？

METHOD
＝公開招標？選擇性？限制性／單一來源？框架協議？其他？

SPECIFICATION
＝規格功能性還是品牌／特定來源導向？

COMPETITION
＝誰有機會參加？公告／邀請是否充分？

EVALUATION
＝價格？品質？技術？最佳價值？權重？

AWARD
＝決標有無依既定標準？

PERFORMANCE
＝履約、驗收、變更、追加、終止是否受控？
```

- 預算；
- 採購需求；

- 規格草案；

- 利益衝突聲明；
- 評分表；
- 會議紀錄；
- 決標理由；
- 底價／估價；
- 限制性採購簽准；
- 契約；
- 變更；
- 驗收；
- 付款；
- 稽核；
- 廠商往來。

核心：

> **政府採購的法律證據是一條「公共決策留下的紀錄鏈」。**

> **法位提醒：以下段落直接承接來源卡；BUILD01 未執行 web/current-law refresh。若案件依賴條約狀態、生效日期、國內轉置或最新法規，主系統必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## 27.13 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依法域與採購案現查：

1. 採購主體；
2. 採購金額與門檻；
3. 採購方式；
4. 公告與期限；
5. 資格；
6. 規格；
7. 評選；
8. 最低價／最佳價值；
9. 限制性／單一來源；
10. 利益衝突；
11. 異議／申訴／救濟；
12. 履約與契約變更；
13. 停權／黑名單；
14. 公共工程；
15. 國防／緊急例外；
16. WTO GPA coverage；
17. FTA 採購章；
18. UNCITRAL Model Law 是否被國內法採納。

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留行政權限、個案行政行為、一般許可、裁量、理由與good-administration共同層；189行政制裁、194資訊公開、200規則制定取得優先專門路由。

### PARTIAL_SUPERSESSION ROUTING LOCK

```text
GENERAL ADMINISTRATIVE COMPETENCE / INDIVIDUAL ACT / GENERAL LICENSING / DISCRETION / FACTS / REASONS / GOOD ADMINISTRATION → DLM-035
ADMINISTRATIVE / REGULATORY SANCTIONS → DLM-189
OFFICIAL DOCUMENT ACCESS / FOI → DLM-194
ADMINISTRATIVE RULEMAKING / DELEGATED REGULATION → DLM-200
```

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-035-ADMINISTRATIVE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "行政法"
  english_name: "Administrative Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "行政機關在執行法律、分配許可與資源、監管市場、制裁違規、提供公共服務時，每一項具體公權力行為如何取得法源、權限、程序、事實、理由與裁量資格？"

  historical_reason:
    "現代政府不可能只靠國會逐案決定。行政機關必須處理許可、稅捐、環境、醫療、金融、社福、警察、建築、教育等大量專業決策。但行政機關同時掌握資訊、執行力與強制資源。行政法因此不是反行政，而是讓專業行政可以運作，同時要求權力具有法律依據、程序公平、理由、比例、可預測性與可審查性。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "行政效率 vs 程序保障"
    - "專業裁量 vs 法律控制"
    - "個案彈性 vs 一致性／平等"
    - "政策變更 vs 法律安定／信賴"
    - "資訊保密 vs 透明"
    - "監管防弊 vs 過度干預"
    - "行政自我糾錯 vs 獨立司法審查"
    - "自動化行政 vs 可理解理由"
    - "公共利益 vs 個人權利"

  shortest_maxim:
    "行政法第一眼不是『政府說可以不可以』；先問『是哪個機關、依哪一條法、對誰、用哪種行政工具、根據哪些事實、經過什麼程序、留下什麼理由』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 41.1 第一眼｜AUTHORITY → COMPETENCE → INSTRUMENT → FACTS → PROCEDURE → DISCRETION → REASONS → EFFECT

```text
AUTHORITY
＝中央部會？地方？獨立機關？公法人？受委託私人？

COMPETENCE
＝它有沒有管這件事的權限？

INSTRUMENT
＝行政處分？命令／規則？契約？指導？補助？許可？裁罰？

FACTS
＝行政決定依賴哪些事實？

PROCEDURE
＝通知、陳述意見、聽證、利益衝突、期限？

DISCRETION
＝法律給多少選擇空間？

REASONS
＝決定為什麼是這個結果？

EFFECT
＝對誰發生什麼法律效果？
```


UNHCR currently records 149 States party to the 1951 Convention and/or 1967 Protocol and continues to identify non-refoulement as a core principle. Entry, asylum procedure, detention, status determination and removal remain jurisdiction-specific.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **UNHCR — The 1951 Refugee Convention**
  - URL: https://www.unhcr.org/us/about-unhcr/overview/1951-refugee-convention
  - Verified point: Current page: 149 States are party to the 1951 Convention and/or 1967 Protocol; non-refoulement remains a core principle.

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別處理政府採購、移民／難民與海關；都是國家對外部人／市場／邊境的公權力接口。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-047-IMMIGRATION_REFUGEE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "移民／難民法"
  english_name: "Immigration / Refugee Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "人在跨越國境後，國家依什麼法律分類其入境、停留、工作與離境資格；若本人主張迫害、酷刑、家庭、兒童或其他不可回復傷害風險，國家在拒絕入境、拘留、驅逐或遣返前必須完成哪些國際保護與程序審查？"

  historical_reason:
    "國家長期主張控制邊界、移民與外國人入境的主權，但二十世紀戰爭、迫害與大規模流離使國際法承認：邊界控制不能把人送回迫害、酷刑或特定重大危險。現代移民／難民法因此是主權、行政法、人權法與國際保護的交界：一般移民以國內法管理入境與停留，難民法則在符合國際保護條件時對國家移除權形成更強限制。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "邊界主權 vs non-refoulement"
    - "一般移民管理 vs 國際保護"
    - "快速邊境程序 vs 個別風險審查"
    - "不規則入境控制 vs 難民取得庇護程序"
    - "移民拘留 vs 人身自由"
    - "驅逐效率 vs 程序與救濟"
    - "家庭完整 vs 移民政策"
    - "國家安全 vs refugee exclusion / due process"
    - "兒童最佳利益 vs removal"
    - "大量流入管理 vs individual status determination"

  shortest_maxim:
    "移民／難民法第一眼不是『他有沒有簽證』；先問『他現在是哪一種法律身份、為什麼不能回去、哪個國家能處理、移除會把他送到哪裡、那裡有什麼法律上不能忽略的風險』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 53.1 第一眼｜PERSON → STATUS → ENTRY / STAY BASIS → PROTECTION CLAIM → COUNTRY OF RETURN → RISK → PROCEDURE → REMOVAL / STATUS

```text
PERSON
＝citizen？foreigner？stateless？dual national？

STATUS
＝visitor？student？worker？permanent resident？irregular migrant？asylum-seeker？refugee？

ENTRY / STAY BASIS
＝visa？permit？family？work？humanitarian？

PROTECTION CLAIM
＝persecution？torture？armed conflict？serious harm？child / family issue？

COUNTRY OF RETURN
＝準備送到哪個國家？

RISK
＝誰會傷害？為何？國家能否保護？是否可在國內安全遷移？

PROCEDURE
＝申請、面談、證據、翻譯、律師、上訴、suspensive effect？

RESULT
＝admission？status？complementary protection？removal？detention？release？
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 53.24 證據特徵

- passport / ID；
- visa / permit；
- entry record；
- application；
- interview transcript；
- interpreter record；
- country-of-origin information；
- police / political records；
- medical evidence；
- threats；
- social media；
- membership；
- travel route；
- family links；
- detention order；
- removal decision；
- diplomatic assurance；
- appeal；
- refugee / protection decision；
- statelessness documents。

核心：

> **移民案件的文件證明「身份與程序」；難民案件的證據還必須回答「回去會發生什麼、為什麼」。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "MULTILATERAL_FRAMEWORK_VERIFIED__MEMBER_IMPLEMENTATION_AND_NATIONAL_CUSTOMS_CHECK_REQUIRED"

```

The WTO Trade Facilitation Agreement remains current. Tonga's January 2026 ratification brought ratifications to 162 of 166 WTO Members; by June 2026 overall implementation commitments were 89%, with many Category C measures scheduled through 2030. National customs law and implementation dates therefore remain essential.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **WTO — TFA review, 25 February 2026**
  - URL: https://www.wto.org/english/news_e/news26_e/fac_25feb26_296_e.htm
  - Verified point: Tonga ratification brought TFA ratifications to 162 of 166 WTO Members.
- **WTO — TFA review, 10–11 June 2026**
  - URL: https://www.wto.org/english/news_e/news26_e/fac_10jun26_416_e.htm
  - Verified point: Overall implementation commitments stood at 89%; many Category C measures remain scheduled through 2030.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列框架／條約狀態。若案件答案取決於特定國家、保留／聲明、領域範圍、生效日期、清單、主管機關或個案事實，仍必須在 runtime 重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別處理政府採購、移民／難民與海關；都是國家對外部人／市場／邊境的公權力接口。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-085-CUSTOMS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "海關法"
  english_name: "Customs Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "貨物進出 customs territory 時，誰是 declarant / importer / exporter、貨物如何分類、原產地如何認定、customs value 如何形成、應繳何種 duty / tax、是否受 prohibition / restriction、採何種 customs procedure、何時 release / clearance、違規時誰負 administrative / civil / criminal responsibility？"

  historical_reason:
    "海關位於國境與市場的接點，同時承擔 revenue、trade policy、security、health、environment、sanctions 與 statistical functions。若只追求控制，邊境會成為任意阻斷貿易的權力；若只追求速度，又可能讓違禁品、逃漏稅與 supply-chain risk 穿透。因此現代 customs law 以 classification、origin、valuation、risk management、advance rulings、electronic declarations、post-clearance audit、AEO、appeal 與 border-agency cooperation 建立『快放合法貨、精查高風險』的制度。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "revenue collection vs trade facilitation"
    - "border security vs predictable release"
    - "risk targeting vs non-discrimination"
    - "self-declaration vs verification"
    - "transaction value vs anti-undervaluation concerns"
    - "classification certainty vs technological change"
    - "preferential origin vs supply-chain complexity"
    - "AEO facilitation vs equal treatment"
    - "customs enforcement vs procedural rights"
    - "data sharing vs confidentiality"
    - "e-commerce speed vs low-value fraud"
    - "customs control vs postal / express delivery"
    - "trade sanctions / SPS / product controls vs core customs functions"

  shortest_maxim:
    "海關法第一眼不是『東西進國境了』；先問『誰申報哪一批 goods、採何種 customs procedure、HS 怎麼分、origin 怎麼來、value 怎麼算、有哪些 duty / restriction、何時 release、哪個 decision 能 appeal』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 91.1 第一眼｜GOODS → PROCEDURE → DECLARANT → CLASSIFICATION → ORIGIN → VALUATION → DUTY / TAX → RESTRICTION → RISK / CONTROL → RELEASE → POST-CLEARANCE → REMEDY

```text
GOODS
＝what physically crossed the customs territory?

PROCEDURE
＝import for home use？transit？temporary admission？warehouse？export？

DECLARANT
＝importer？customs broker？carrier？postal operator？

CLASSIFICATION
＝HS heading/subheading + national tariff line？

ORIGIN
＝non-preferential？preferential？

VALUATION
＝transaction value or alternative method？

DUTY / TAX
＝tariff？excise？VAT？trade remedy？

RESTRICTION
＝sanctions？SPS？CITES？product safety？export control？

RISK / CONTROL
＝document check？scanner？physical inspection？lab？

RELEASE
＝goods released subject to guarantee / later audit？

POST-CLEARANCE
＝records / audit / reassessment？

REMEDY
＝administrative review？court？refund？
```

## 91.34 證據特徵

- bill of lading / airway bill；
- commercial invoice；

- origin certificate / supplier declaration；

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "TROMSO_STATUS_VERIFIED__17_PARTIES__DOMESTIC_FOI_CHECK_REQUIRED"
```

The Tromsø Convention remains in force and the Council of Europe currently lists 17 Parties. It is a minimum access-to-official-documents treaty for its Parties, not a universal FOI code.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **Council of Europe — Tromsø Convention**
  - URL: https://www.coe.int/en/web/access-to-official-documents/home
  - Verified point: Current page: entered into force 2020-12-01; 17 Parties.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列框架／條約狀態。若案件答案取決於特定國家、保留／聲明、領域範圍、生效日期、清單、主管機關或個案事實，仍必須在 runtime 重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

從一般行政透明中專門化出official records的request/search/exception/partial access/review chain。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-194-FREEDOM_OF_INFORMATION_ACCESS_TO_OFFICIAL_DOCUMENTS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "資訊公開／政府文件近用法"
  english_name: "Freedom of Information / Access to Official Documents Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "任何人、人民、居民、媒體、企業或其他依法有資格之請求人，何時可以要求public authority搜尋、識別並提供其持有或控制的official documents / records；哪些國安、執法、隱私、商業秘密、決策過程等利益可以限制公開，限制是否需要harm test / public-interest balancing，部分公開、期限、費用、理由與獨立救濟如何運作？"

  historical_reason:
    "行政權掌握大量只有國家在履職過程中才會生成的文件。若公民只能看到政府選擇主動發布的資訊，監督權仍由被監督者控制。資訊公開制度因此把『透明』從政策美德轉成request-response legal process：誰可以請、找什麼文件、機關必須怎麼搜尋與作成決定、何種例外可拒絕、拒絕由誰審。它同時要避免把公開權變成國安、個資、商業秘密與有效決策的全面消滅器。"

  first_move:
    "先確認requester是否需要資格、requested body是不是public authority、標的是existing official document / record還是要求機關創造答案；之後固定possession / control、request scope、search duty、time limit、exception、harm、public interest與partial access，最後才處理review。不得把『政府知道這件事』直接翻成『存在可公開文件』。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "government transparency vs confidentiality"
    - "democratic accountability vs effective decision-making"
    - "access to documents vs personal-data protection"
    - "public oversight vs national security"
    - "disclosure vs law-enforcement integrity"
    - "commercial transparency vs trade secrets"
    - "broad request rights vs administrative burden"
    - "record preservation vs deletion / ephemeral communications"
    - "requester equality vs abusive / repetitive requests"
    - "public-interest disclosure vs categorical exceptions"
    - "proactive publication vs request-based access"
    - "journalistic need vs requester-neutral regimes"

  shortest_maxim:
    "資訊公開第一眼不是『政府應該透明』；先問『哪個機關、哪份既存文件、誰持有或控制、請求有沒有具體到能搜尋、哪一個例外真正被觸發、能不能部分公開、拒絕理由和救濟在哪裡』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G3-37.1 第一眼｜REQUESTER → AUTHORITY → RECORD → EXISTENCE → POSSESSION / CONTROL → REQUEST → SEARCH → EXCEPTION → HARM / PUBLIC INTEREST → PARTIAL ACCESS → DECISION → REVIEW

```text
REQUESTER
＝any person? citizen? resident? legal person? journalist?

AUTHORITY
＝ministry? court? parliament? municipality? SOE? contractor?

RECORD
＝official document? file? email? message? database? metadata?

EXISTENCE
＝existing record or request to create explanation?

POSSESSION / CONTROL
＝held by authority? controlled through contractor?

REQUEST
＝identifiable scope? date? subject?

SEARCH
＝reasonable / adequate search?

EXCEPTION
＝security? defence? privacy? investigation? commercial? deliberation?

HARM / PUBLIC INTEREST
＝mandatory? qualified? overriding interest?

PARTIAL ACCESS
＝redaction / severability possible?

DECISION
＝time? reasons? fee? format?

REVIEW
＝internal? information commissioner? court?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G3-37.18 Evidence architecture

- original request；
- acknowledgment；
- clarification；
- search log；
- custodian list；
- records schedule；
- responsive-record index；
- exemption log；
- redacted / unredacted copy；
- third-party consultation；
- harm assessment；
- public-interest reasoning；
- fee calculation；
- decision letter；
- statutory deadline；
- internal review；
- information commissioner decision；
- court judgment；
- retention policy；
- destruction / deletion record。

核心：

> **資訊公開案件的證據同時包括「文件本身」與「政府如何證明自己有合理搜尋、合法拒絕與可審查理由」。**
