The OECD lobbying/influence Recommendation was revised in May 2024 and remains a current international soft-law benchmark. Registration, covered officials, disclosures, gifts, foreign influence and enforcement remain domestic-law questions.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **OECD — Recommendation on Transparency and Integrity in Lobbying and Influence**
  - URL: https://legalinstruments.oecd.org/api/print?ids=256&lang=en
  - Verified point: The OECD Recommendation was revised by the Council at Ministerial level on 2024-05-03.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列框架／條約狀態。若案件答案取決於特定國家、保留／聲明、領域範圍、生效日期、清單、主管機關或個案事實，仍必須在 runtime 重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

處理誰影響公共決策、client/funding/meeting/revolving-door與透明義務；不與rulemaking participation或political finance合併。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-197-LOBBYING_INTEREST_REPRESENTATION_INFLUENCE_TRANSPARENCY_LAW"
  chinese_name: "遊說／利益代表與影響透明法"
  english_name: "Lobbying / Interest Representation & Influence Transparency Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "公司、工會、NGO、專業遊說者、law firm、consultancy、think tank、industry association、foreign-interest representative或其他actor試圖影響legislation、regulation、procurement policy、行政決策或公共政策時，哪些活動進入lobbying / interest representation法域；誰需登錄、揭露client / funding / issue / expenditure / meetings，public officials負何種meeting / gift / conflict / revolving-door義務，regulatory footprint、foreign influence與違規制裁如何讓合法影響與秘密利益交換分開？"

  historical_reason:
    "民主決策需要外部知識、利益陳述與倡議，因此lobbying本身不能被等同腐敗。但同一影響活動若不可見，政策可能被資源最強、關係最密或以代理人隱藏的少數利益捕獲。政治資金法只看捐款不夠，反貪腐法只抓賄賂也太晚；現代lobbying regulation因此用register、client disclosure、meeting transparency、regulatory footprint、cooling-off與integrity rules讓『誰在影響誰、代表誰、影響什麼』可被審計。"

  first_move:
    "先辨識actor、真正client / interest與被影響的public decision，再判定activity是否落入lobbying / interest representation定義及exemption；之後才檢查registration、disclosure、contacts、public-official duties、gifts/conflicts、revolving door、foreign-interest rules與sanctions。不得因actor是NGO／企業／律師／工會就自動排除或認定遊說。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "legitimate participation vs undue influence"
    - "freedom of expression / petition vs disclosure"
    - "expert input vs monopoly of influence"
    - "client confidentiality vs public decision transparency"
    - "commercial secrecy vs regulatory footprint"
    - "foreign participation vs covert foreign influence"
    - "revolving-door expertise vs conflict of interest"
    - "lobbyist registration vs burdens on civic advocacy"
    - "meeting transparency vs effective government"
    - "political equality vs resource inequality"
    - "anti-corruption vs criminalising ordinary advocacy"

  shortest_maxim:
    "遊說法第一眼不是『有人去找官員』；先問『誰代表誰、想影響哪個公共決定、用什麼方式、法律把這種活動算不算lobbying、哪些資訊必須讓公眾知道、哪裡開始變成利益衝突或不當影響』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G3-49.1 第一眼｜ACTOR → CLIENT / INTEREST → TARGET → ACTIVITY → COVERAGE → REGISTER → DISCLOSURE → CONTACT / FOOTPRINT → PUBLIC-OFFICIAL INTEGRITY → REVOLVING DOOR → FOREIGN INTEREST → SANCTION

```text
ACTOR
＝in-house? consultant? law firm? NGO? union? trade association? think tank?

CLIENT / INTEREST
＝own interest? member? paying client? beneficial funder? foreign state?

TARGET
＝legislation? regulation? administrative decision? executive policy?

ACTIVITY
＝meeting? written submission? campaign? event? digital influence?

COVERAGE
＝lobbying? public consultation? legal representation? petition? exempt communication?

REGISTER
＝who must register? before which contact?

DISCLOSURE
＝client? funding? issue? target? spending? beneficiaries?

CONTACT / FOOTPRINT
＝meeting log? open agenda? legislative/regulatory footprint?

PUBLIC-OFFICIAL INTEGRITY
＝gift? hospitality? conflict? confidential info?

REVOLVING DOOR
＝cooling-off? former portfolio?

FOREIGN INTEREST
＝foreign principal? state-linked interest? extra disclosure?

SANCTION
＝late filing? false filing? unregistered lobbying? official breach?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G3-49.16 Evidence architecture

- lobby register entry；
- client engagement letter；
- beneficial-ownership / funding data；
- meeting calendar；
- minister / official open agenda；
- emails / submissions；
- legislative / regulatory footprint；
- consultation response；
- expense disclosure；
- gift / hospitality log；
- conflict declaration；
- employment history；
- cooling-off decision；
- foreign-principal relationship；
- sanctions / compliance notices；
- policy draft history。

核心：

> **遊說法的證據不是證明『有人有影響力』；而是把影響鏈的actor、client、target、contact與公共決策痕跡變得可見。**

The OECD Regulatory Policy Recommendation remains current. Its January 2026 implementation report confirms broad adoption of RIA/consultation foundations but significant implementation gaps, reinforcing that actual rulemaking validity must be checked against the jurisdiction's enabling law and procedure.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **OECD — Recommendation on Regulatory Policy and Governance, 2026 implementation report**
  - URL: https://legalinstruments.oecd.org/api/print?ids=273&lang=en
  - Verified point: January 2026 implementation report found broad foundations but significant implementation gaps; RIA/consultation can occur too late.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列框架／條約狀態。若案件答案取決於特定國家、保留／聲明、領域範圍、生效日期、清單、主管機關或個案事實，仍必須在 runtime 重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

由行政法與立法程序交界專門化，處理delegation、instrument classification、consultation、record、publication與effective date。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-200-ADMINISTRATIVE_RULEMAKING_DELEGATED_LEGISLATION_REGULATORY_PROCEDURE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "行政規則制定／授權立法與監管程序法"
  english_name: "Administrative Rulemaking / Delegated Legislation & Regulatory Procedure Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "行政機關、獨立監管機關或其他受授權public authority何時可以制定對不特定人反覆適用的regulation / rule / delegated legislation / implementing act；母法授權範圍、instrument classification、notice/draft、public consultation、impact assessment、evidence record、comments response、理由、adoption、publication、effective date、exceptions、retroactivity與judicial review如何讓『政府訂規則』取得不同於個案行政處分的法律資格？"

  historical_reason:
    "現代國家無法把所有技術標準、金融規則、環境限值、藥品程序、費率與行政細節都寫進primary legislation。授權立法因此不可避免。但一個行政機關若同時提出規則、選擇證據、聽取意見、作成規則並執行規則，就必須有比『部會覺得合理』更完整的法位鏈。不同法域發展notice-and-comment、consultation、impact assessment、publication與ultra vires / arbitrariness review，正是為了讓一般性行政規範可被追溯。"

  first_move:
    "先辨識這個instrument究竟是legislative rule、delegated regulation、implementing act、interpretive rule、guidance還是個案order；再找delegation source與scope。只有instrument classification完成，才能判斷notice / consultation / hearing / impact assessment / publication與effective-date requirements。不得只因文件標題叫『guideline』就假定它沒有外部法律效果，也不得因叫『regulation』就假定授權充分。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "administrative expertise vs democratic legitimacy"
    - "regulatory speed vs participation"
    - "delegation flexibility vs legislative reservation"
    - "technical evidence vs political choice"
    - "open consultation vs capture / lobbying dominance"
    - "impact analysis vs analytic ritual"
    - "predictability vs emergency rulemaking"
    - "guidance flexibility vs hidden binding effect"
    - "prospective regulation vs retroactivity"
    - "regulatory experimentation vs legal certainty"
    - "public record vs confidential evidence"
    - "rulemaking discretion vs judicial review"

  shortest_maxim:
    "行政規則第一眼不是『主管機關有沒有發公告』；先問『母法授權它訂什麼、這份文件法律上到底是哪一種規範、應走什麼程序、誰能參與、機關怎麼回應重要材料、何時才正式生效』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G3-61.1 第一眼｜DELEGATION → INSTRUMENT → PROCEDURE SOURCE → DRAFT / NOTICE → IMPACT ANALYSIS → PARTICIPATION → RECORD → RESPONSE / REASONS → ADOPTION → PUBLICATION → EFFECTIVE DATE → REVIEW

```text
DELEGATION
＝which statute / constitutional power?

INSTRUMENT
＝legislative rule? delegated regulation? guidance? policy? order?

PROCEDURE SOURCE
＝APA? enabling act? constitution? sector statute? internal rule?

DRAFT / NOTICE
＝what must be published before adoption?

IMPACT ANALYSIS
＝economic? small entity? rights? environment? data? competition?

PARTICIPATION
＝written comment? hearing? targeted consultation?

RECORD
＝what evidence / studies / submissions form rulemaking record?

RESPONSE / REASONS
＝how must material comments / alternatives be addressed?

ADOPTION
＝who signs / votes / approves?

PUBLICATION
＝official journal / register?

EFFECTIVE DATE
＝immediate? waiting period? transition?

REVIEW
＝ultra vires? arbitrary? proportionality? procedural invalidity?
```

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
```

There is no universal national-security code that displaces domestic constitutional/statutory authority. ICCPR remains in force with 175 Parties and supplies a human-rights baseline, but surveillance, secrecy, foreign-interference, counterintelligence and security powers must be checked jurisdiction-by-jurisdiction.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **UN Treaty Collection — ICCPR**
  - URL: https://treaties.un.org/Pages/ShowMTDSGDetails.aspx?chapter=4&lang=en&mtdsg_no=IV-4&src=UNTSONLINE&tabid=3
  - Verified point: Status 2026-08-13: in force; 175 Parties.

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別承擔國安、國防、警察、制裁／出口管制、災害治理、公共衛生公權力，不因都涉及緊急或強制而合併。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-055-NATIONAL_SECURITY_LAW"
  chinese_name: "國安法"
  english_name: "National Security Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "國家面對間諜、恐怖主義、外國干預、關鍵基礎設施攻擊、國家機密與其他重大安全威脅時，哪些機關可以用哪些情報、監控、行政、刑事、金融或產業管制工具介入；這些高度秘密且預防性的權力又如何接受法源、定義、證據、必要性、比例、程序與獨立監督？"

  historical_reason:
    "國安法處理的是低頻但高後果、資訊不完全、可能跨境且常需預防的威脅。普通刑法往往要求犯罪已較具體化才介入，國家安全治理則需要更早辨識情報、資金、外國指揮與基礎設施風險；但正因它在不確定性高、秘密性高的階段取得權力，也最容易出現概念過寬、政治異議安全化、秘密證據不可反駁與預防權永久化。因此國安法的核心不是『安全優先』，而是『安全權力如何取得可審查資格』。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "預防安全風險 vs 無罪推定／程序保障"
    - "情報秘密 vs 可反駁／可審查"
    - "國家安全定義彈性 vs 法律明確性"
    - "反恐效率 vs 人權"
    - "外國干預防制 vs 政治表意／結社"
    - "機密保護 vs 新聞／吹哨／公共監督"
    - "大數據監控 vs 隱私"
    - "關鍵基礎設施保護 vs 私人營業自主"
    - "安全審查 vs 投資／貿易開放"
    - "行政預防措施 vs 刑事定罪門檻"
    - "跨國情報合作 vs 來源／責任可追溯性"

  shortest_maxim:
    "國安法第一眼不是『這件事很危險』；先問『威脅被法律怎麼定義、哪個機關取得什麼權力、根據什麼可驗證材料、措施限制誰的什麼權利、誰可以獨立審查』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 61.1 第一眼｜THREAT → LEGAL CATEGORY → SOURCE → ACTOR → INFORMATION → TOOL → RIGHTS IMPACT → OVERSIGHT → REMEDY

```text
THREAT
＝terrorism？espionage？foreign interference？cyberattack？critical infrastructure？sabotage？

LEGAL CATEGORY
＝法律到底把它定義成什麼？

SOURCE
＝憲法？national-security statute？刑法？行政法？UN obligations？

ACTOR
＝intelligence agency？police？military？financial regulator？minister？court？

INFORMATION
＝公開資料？情報？線人？秘密證據？算法風險分數？

TOOL
＝surveillance？freeze？designation？search？detention？licence denial？prosecution？

RIGHTS IMPACT
＝隱私？表意？結社？財產？自由？公平審判？

OVERSIGHT
＝法院？國會？專門監察？independent reviewer？

REMEDY
＝challenge、de-listing、judicial review、compensation？
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 61.22 證據特徵

- intelligence report；
- raw intelligence / source grading；
- financial transaction；
- communication metadata；
- surveillance authorization；
- watchlist / designation file；
- classification decision；
- cyber logs；
- foreign liaison report；
- company ownership；
- funding records；
- infrastructure risk assessment；
- security-clearance file；
- judicial warrant；
- oversight report；
- declassification / review record。

核心：

> **國安案件最需要防的是「因為材料不能公開，所以材料自動可信」；秘密保護可以限制公開範圍，不能取消來源、可靠性、推論與責任鏈。**

# DLM-056｜國防法

```yaml
RUNTIME_CARD:
  DLM_ID: "DLM-056"
  DOMAIN_NAME: "國防法"
  PRIMARY_SUBJECT: "A"

  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_SPECIALIZATION"

  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_I_DLM_001_114"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# 62｜DLM-056｜國防法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_I/V0.38-EXP/B19"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "—"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別承擔國安、國防、警察、制裁／出口管制、災害治理、公共衛生公權力，不因都涉及緊急或強制而合併。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-056-DEFENCE_MILITARY_LAW"
  chinese_name: "國防法"
  english_name: "Defence / Military Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "國家如何在憲政與國際法框架內建立、資助、指揮與監督武裝力量；軍人如何取得特殊義務、軍紀與權利位置；何時可以動員、部署或使用武力；一旦存在 armed conflict，又由哪些 IHL 規則限制手段、目標與指揮責任？"

  historical_reason:
    "武裝力量是國家最集中的合法暴力能力。沒有專業軍事組織，國家難以自衛；但若軍隊能自行決定戰略、預算、國內部署與法律界線，武力會脫離公共責任。國防法因此同時處理文人統制、軍事組織、兵役、動員、軍紀、軍事司法、採購、deployment authority，以及 UN Charter 與 IHL 對武力使用和 armed conflict conduct 的外部限制。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "軍事專業 vs 文人民主統制"
    - "國家自衛 vs 禁止使用武力"
    - "命令服從 vs 違法命令責任"
    - "軍紀效率 vs 軍人人權／程序"
    - "作戰秘密 vs 公共監督"
    - "快速決策 vs legal review"
    - "國防採購保密 vs 公共資源責任"
    - "國內軍事部署 vs 警察／民政角色"
    - "軍事必要 vs IHL humanitarian constraints"
    - "指揮鏈 vs individual criminal responsibility"
    - "新武器技術 vs legal review"

  shortest_maxim:
    "國防法第一眼不是『軍事上需不需要』；先問『誰有權組織／部署／命令武裝力量、國內法授權到哪裡、國際法允許哪一類武力、若形成 armed conflict，IHL 如何限制作戰』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 62.1 第一眼｜CONSTITUTIONAL AUTHORITY → FORCE → MISSION → DEPLOYMENT → INTERNATIONAL LAW → CONFLICT CLASSIFICATION → IHL → COMMAND → ACCOUNTABILITY

```text
CONSTITUTIONAL AUTHORITY
＝總統？政府？國會？國防部？誰決定戰爭／部署／動員？

FORCE
＝regular armed forces？reserve？coast guard？paramilitary？

MISSION
＝defence？training？peacekeeping？domestic emergency？law enforcement？

DEPLOYMENT
＝國內？境外？同盟？UN mandate？

INTERNATIONAL LAW
＝consent？Security Council authorization？self-defence？

CONFLICT CLASSIFICATION
＝IAC？NIAC？occupation？below armed-conflict threshold？

IHL
＝Geneva Conventions？Additional Protocols？customary rules？

COMMAND
＝誰下令、誰控制、ROE 如何？

ACCOUNTABILITY
＝military discipline？ordinary courts？war crimes？state responsibility？
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 62.26 證據特徵

- constitution / defence statute；
- deployment order；
- parliamentary authorization；
- UN Security Council resolution；
- Article 51 notification；
- command orders；
- ROE；
- operational logs；
- targeting file；
- intelligence；
- battle damage assessment；
- weapons review；
- military manuals；
- detention record；
- training；
- disciplinary file；
- procurement file；
- chain-of-command evidence；

核心：

> **國防案件要把「政治授權—軍事命令—國際法資格—具體作戰行為—責任人」一層一層接起來；不能用戰略重要性替每一層背書。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "ICCPR_BASELINE_VERIFIED__DEROGATION_NOTIFICATION_AND_DOMESTIC_EMERGENCY_LAW_CHECK_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "international/current framework status only; domestic merits not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

ICCPR remains in force with 175 Parties. Emergency powers cannot be inferred from the treaty alone: domestic emergency authority, proclamation/trigger, duration, review, non-derogable rights and any applicable international derogation/notification position must be checked.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **UN Treaty Collection — ICCPR**
  - URL: https://treaties.un.org/Pages/ShowMTDSGDetails.aspx?chapter=4&lang=en&mtdsg_no=IV-4&src=UNTSONLINE&tabid=3
  - Verified point: Status 2026-08-13: in force; 175 Parties.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列框架／條約狀態。若案件答案取決於特定國家、保留／聲明、領域範圍、生效日期、清單、主管機關或個案事實，仍必須在 runtime 重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留 constitutional emergency 的觸發、非常權力、期限、延長、終止、derogation與監督；災害、公共衛生、國安／國防的sector emergency另由093、191、055/056承擔。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-057-EMERGENCY_POWERS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "緊急狀態法"
  english_name: "Emergency Powers Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當戰爭、重大暴亂、災害、疫情、金融或基礎設施危機使普通法律程序被認為不足時，誰可以宣布 emergency、觸發哪些非常權力、限制哪些權利、維持多久、誰持續監督、何時延長或終止；如果措施錯誤，當事人如何取得即時且有效的救濟？"

  historical_reason:
    "現代憲政承認某些危機可能需要比平時更快速、更集中與更具侵入性的權力；但歷史也反覆顯示，『暫時』非常權力最容易永久化，行政機關也最容易用危機語言排除國會、法院與人權審查。緊急狀態法因此不是授權國家暫停法律，而是預先設計 trigger、scope、duration、derogation、renewal、oversight、sunset 與 exit，使非常權力本身仍被法律包住。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "快速決策 vs checks and balances"
    - "公共安全／生命 vs 個人自由"
    - "行政集中權力 vs 國會／法院監督"
    - "危機不確定性 vs 法律明確性"
    - "預防原則 vs 證據不足"
    - "全國措施 vs 地方差異"
    - "暫時性 vs permanent normalization"
    - "秘密資訊 vs 公共理由"
    - "集體風險 vs 弱勢群體不成比例負擔"
    - "退出過早 vs emergency perpetuation"
    - "一般權利限制 vs treaty derogation"

  shortest_maxim:
    "緊急狀態法第一眼不是『事情夠不夠可怕』；先問『哪條法允許誰宣布、觸發要件是否成立、增加了什麼權力、限制了什麼權利、期限多久、誰能在危機還沒結束前就審查』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 63.1 第一眼｜EVENT → LEGAL TRIGGER → DECLARATION → POWER → RIGHT IMPACT → DURATION → OVERSIGHT → RENEWAL → EXIT → REMEDY

```text
EVENT
＝war？terror attack？epidemic？disaster？riot？infrastructure collapse？

LEGAL TRIGGER
＝普通法律不足？public emergency threatening life of nation？國內法門檻？

DECLARATION
＝誰宣布？形式？公布？地域？

POWER
＝curfew？movement restriction？requisition？detention？procurement？data collection？

RIGHT IMPACT
＝哪些權利被限制／derogated？

DURATION
＝起訖？sunset？

OVERSIGHT
＝legislature？court？independent body？

RENEWAL
＝誰批准？需要新證據嗎？

EXIT
＝何時恢復 ordinary law？

REMEDY
＝即時 challenge？compensation？review？
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 63.26 證據特徵

- declaration；
- statutory trigger materials；
- epidemiological / security / disaster data；
- cabinet minutes；
- emergency orders；
- legislative approval；
- renewal record；
- rights-impact assessment；
- detention records；
- procurement；
- requisition；
- surveillance data；
- scientific advice；
- judicial review；
- international derogation notification；
- sunset / termination order；
- post-emergency audit。

核心：

> **緊急狀態的證據鏈不是只證明「危機曾經很嚴重」，還要證明每一個非常措施在每一段時間裡為什麼仍然必要。**

```yaml
  chinese_name: "警察法"

    "國家在法院尚未認定犯罪前，如何授權警察與其他 law-enforcement officials 進行接觸、盤查、搜索、逮捕、拘束、蒐證、交通／集會執法與使用武力；每一種即時強制措施何時取得法源、必要性與比例資格，又如何留下紀錄、外部監督與有效救濟？"

  historical_reason:
    "警察是法律與日常生活接觸最密集的國家強制機關。很多警察決定發生在幾秒內，且在法院介入前就能限制自由、隱私甚至生命。現代警察法因此不能只依『維持治安』的抽象目的，而必須把 authority、trigger、scope、force、record、oversight、investigation 與 remedy 組成可追溯的強制權鏈。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "即時治安需求 vs 法源明確性"
    - "犯罪預防 vs 自由／隱私"
    - "警察現場裁量 vs 可審查標準"
    - "員警安全 vs force minimization"
    - "群眾秩序 vs 集會／表意自由"
    - "偵查秘密 vs 程序救濟"
    - "資料科技效率 vs mass surveillance"
    - "執法差異化 vs discrimination / profiling"
    - "內部紀律 vs 獨立調查"
    - "快速 arrest vs judicial control"
    - "警察專業判斷 vs evidentiary accountability"

  shortest_maxim:
    "警察法第一眼不是『警察為什麼這樣做』；先問『是哪一種警察權、哪個法源、什麼事實觸發、可以做到哪裡、用了多少強制、何時必須停止、誰事後能看見與審查』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 70.1 第一眼｜FUNCTION → POWER → TRIGGER → TARGET → MEASURE → FORCE → RECORD → REVIEW → REMEDY

```text
FUNCTION
＝crime prevention？investigation？public order？traffic？emergency assistance？

POWER
＝stop？identity check？search？arrest？detention？seizure？surveillance？force？

TRIGGER
＝reasonable suspicion？probable cause？flagrante？warrant？immediate danger？

TARGET
＝person？vehicle？home？device？crowd？business？

MEASURE
＝duration、scope、conditions？

FORCE
＝none？restraint？less-lethal？firearm？

RECORD
＝bodycam？incident report？warrant return？custody log？

REVIEW
＝supervisor？prosecutor？court？independent complaint body？

REMEDY
＝exclusion？release？discipline？criminal investigation？compensation？
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別承擔國安、國防、警察、制裁／出口管制、災害治理、公共衛生公權力，不因都涉及緊急或強制而合併。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-069-INTERNATIONAL_SANCTIONS_EXPORT_CONTROL_LAW"
  chinese_name: "國際制裁／出口管制法"
  english_name: "International Sanctions / Export Control Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "國際組織、國家與區域組織在和平安全、外交、人權與不擴散目的下，何時可以限制特定人、資產、旅行、金融、貨物、軍品、dual-use technology、software、技術援助與最終用途；企業如何辨識適用 regime、jurisdiction、listed party、ownership/control、item classification、destination、end-user、end-use、licence、exception 與 humanitarian carve-outs？"

  historical_reason:
    "跨境商業活動同時移動資金、貨物、軟體、技術與服務。制裁透過限制特定人／地區／經濟活動施加外交與安全壓力；出口管制則透過 item / destination / end-use licensing 防止武器與敏感 dual-use 技術被轉用。兩者常在 bank screening、shipping、trade finance 與 supply chain 相遇，卻具有不同法源與觸發條件。若混在一起，企業容易把『名單命中』當有罪、把『dual-use』當禁止，或反過來漏掉 end-use / ownership / re-export 風險。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "international peace / security vs trade freedom"
    - "targeted sanctions vs due process / humanitarian impact"
    - "national foreign policy vs extraterritorial conflict"
    - "non-proliferation vs scientific / commercial exchange"
    - "security classification vs business predictability"
    - "list screening efficiency vs false positives"
    - "ownership/control rules vs corporate complexity"
    - "export licensing vs innovation speed"
    - "humanitarian exemptions vs over-compliance"
    - "allied coordination vs divergent national rules"
    - "re-export control vs third-country sovereignty"
    - "sanctions compliance vs anti-discrimination / contract performance"

  shortest_maxim:
    "制裁／出口管制第一眼不是『對方是不是敏感國家』；先問『哪個法域／regime、哪個人／貨物／技術、哪個 ownership/control、送去哪裡、給誰、拿去做什麼、需要什麼 licence／exemption，銀行或物流攔下的是哪一條法律』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 75.1 第一眼｜JURISDICTION → REGIME → SUBJECT → ITEM / FUNDS → PARTY → OWNERSHIP / CONTROL → DESTINATION → END USE → LICENCE / EXEMPTION → RECORD

```text
JURISDICTION
＝UN obligation？EU？US？UK？other national law？flag / nationality / territory nexus？

REGIME
＝country sanctions？targeted list？sectoral？arms embargo？export control？

SUBJECT
＝company？individual？bank？carrier？citizen abroad？

ITEM / FUNDS
＝money？goods？software？technology？service？technical assistance？

PARTY
＝counterparty / consignee / bank / beneficial owner？

OWNERSHIP / CONTROL
＝listed entity owns / controls non-listed entity？

DESTINATION
＝where shipped / transferred / accessed？

END USE
＝civil？military？WMD? prohibited programme？

LICENCE / EXEMPTION
＝general / specific licence？humanitarian exemption？exception？

RECORD
＝classification、screening、licence、end-use certificate、shipping、payment？
```

分別承擔國安、國防、警察、制裁／出口管制、災害治理、公共衛生公權力，不因都涉及緊急或強制而合併。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-093-DISASTER_RISK_REDUCTION_EMERGENCY_MANAGEMENT_LAW"
  chinese_name: "災害防救／災害風險治理法"
  english_name: "Disaster Risk Reduction / Emergency Management Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "hazard如何與exposure、vulnerability、capacity交互形成disaster risk；國家／地方政府／critical infrastructure operators如何在prevention、mitigation、preparedness、warning、evacuation、response、relief、recovery、reconstruction、mutual aid與international assistance各階段取得法定權限與責任；ordinary disaster-management powers何時不足而需另外啟動constitutional emergency powers？"

  historical_reason:
    "災害法早期常集中在災後救援，但反覆地震、洪水、颱風、野火、疫情與工業災害證明：真正決定死亡與損失的往往是災前土地使用、建築、貧窮、基礎設施、警報、治理與preparedness。Sendai Framework進一步把『respond to disasters』轉成『manage disaster risk』。法律因此必須涵蓋 risk information、prevention、building / land-use、critical infrastructure、warning、evacuation、shelter、resource mobilization、relief rights、recovery finance、Build Back Better與post-event accountability。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "rapid response vs legality / due process"
    - "central command vs local knowledge"
    - "prevention investment vs visible short-term spending"
    - "scientific uncertainty vs warning / evacuation"
    - "mandatory evacuation vs autonomy / property"
    - "public safety vs livelihood continuity"
    - "relief discretion vs equality / vulnerability"
    - "emergency procurement vs corruption controls"
    - "recovery speed vs planning / rights"
    - "Build Back Better vs return / property expectations"
    - "international assistance vs affected-State coordination"
    - "data sharing vs privacy"
    - "disaster management vs constitutional emergency powers"

  shortest_maxim:
    "災害法第一眼不是『出了大事所以政府可以做任何事』；先問『hazard、exposure、vulnerability各在哪裡、哪個ordinary disaster statute已授權誰做warning / evacuation / mobilization、措施何時開始／結束、誰被特別影響、災後如何review / compensate / rebuild』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 99.1 第一眼｜HAZARD → EXPOSURE → VULNERABILITY / CAPACITY → RISK → PREVENTION / MITIGATION → PREPAREDNESS → WARNING → RESPONSE → RELIEF → RECOVERY / RECONSTRUCTION → ACCOUNTABILITY

```text
HAZARD
＝earthquake？flood？storm？wildfire？landslide？industrial / biological?

EXPOSURE
＝people？housing？hospital？power grid？heritage？business?

VULNERABILITY / CAPACITY
＝poverty？building quality？mobility？warning access？social networks?

RISK
＝probability / scenario / cascading impacts?

PREVENTION / MITIGATION
＝land-use？building retrofit？levee？firebreak？insurance?

PREPAREDNESS
＝plan？drill？stockpile？mutual aid？continuity?

WARNING
＝forecast？alert？order？communication?

RESPONSE
＝evacuation？rescue？medical？shelter？security?

RELIEF
＝food？cash？temporary housing？services?

RECOVERY
＝repair？rebuild？relocate？finance?

ACCOUNTABILITY
＝investigation？audit？compensation？judicial review?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 99.40 Evidence architecture｜災害法的時間軸比結果更重要

核心材料：

- hazard / forecast data；
- risk map；
- building / land-use records；
- preparedness plan；
- drill records；
- warning logs；
- command logs；
- evacuation order；
- resource inventory；
- emergency procurement；
- shelter data；
- infrastructure outage；
- aid distribution；
- recovery plan；
- investigation / audit。

核心：

> **災害案不能只從廢墟往回猜；要把「災前知道什麼、誰有權做什麼、什麼時候收到訊號、什麼時候決定、什麼資源真的可用」逐分鐘／逐階段重建。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25


WHO states the 2024 IHR amendments entered into force on 2025-09-19 for most States Parties, but 11 of 196 rejected them, so identical text cannot be presumed for every State. The WHO Pandemic Agreement was adopted in May 2025, but PABS negotiations were still unfinished in July 2026; WHO states signature opens only after the PABS Annex is adopted.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **WHO — Amended International Health Regulations enter into force**
  - URL: https://www.who.int/news/item/19-09-2025-amended-international-health-regulations-enter-into-force
  - Verified point: 2024 IHR amendments entered into force 2025-09-19 for most States Parties; WHO records 11 of 196 States Parties as rejecting the 2024 amendments.
- **WHO — International Health Regulations current text**
  - URL: https://www.who.int/health-topics/international-health-regulations/
  - Verified point: WHO describes the current IHR text since 2025-09-19 as including the 2014, 2022 and 2024 amendments.
- **WHO — PABS negotiations, 20 July 2026**
  - URL: https://www.who.int/news/item/20-07-2026-who-member-states-continue-negotiations-on-the-pathogen-access-and-benefit-sharing-annex
  - Verified point: PABS Annex remained under negotiation in July 2026; finalization is a critical step before countries can consider signature and ratification of the Pandemic Agreement.
- **WHO — Pandemic Agreement Q&A**
  - URL: https://www.who.int/news-room/questions-and-answers/item/pandemic-prevention--preparedness-and-response-accord
  - Verified point: Agreement was adopted 2025-05-20; under Article 31 it opens for signature after WHA adopts the PABS Annex.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列框架／條約狀態。若案件答案取決於特定國家、保留／聲明、領域範圍、生效日期、清單、主管機關或個案事實，仍必須在 runtime 重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別承擔國安、國防、警察、制裁／出口管制、災害治理、公共衛生公權力，不因都涉及緊急或強制而合併。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-191-PUBLIC_HEALTH_COMMUNICABLE_DISEASE_POWERS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "公共衛生／傳染病公權力法"
  english_name: "Public Health / Communicable-Disease Powers Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當傳染病或其他重大公共衛生風險可能由個人事件擴散為群體風險時，哪些機關可依法監測、通報、檢測、追蹤、隔離、檢疫、要求疫苗／預防措施、限制場所或跨境移動；每項措施需要何種疾病／暴露證據、必要性、比例、期限、程序、資料保護與司法救濟；國內法又如何與IHR及區域衛生合作制度咬合？"

  historical_reason:
    "臨床醫療以單一病人的診斷、同意與治療為中心；傳染病控制則把一人的行動與他人風險連成網絡，因而產生強制通報、隔離、檢疫、接觸者追蹤與邊境衛生等公權力。疫情又容易讓『緊急』變成無期限權力，因此成熟公共衛生法同時要求科學證據、最小必要限制、程序、資料治理與定期重審。IHR提供跨境合作義務，但不把WHO變成各國國內警察。"

  first_move:
    "先辨識health threat與法定疾病／風險分類，再確認competent authority、case / exposure evidence、measure type與真正目的；接著跑necessity、least-restrictive / proportionality、duration、review、data與compensation／support。跨境事件再判IHR、EU或其他區域合作。不得從WHO alert直接跳到國內強制隔離。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "individual liberty vs population protection"
    - "rapid response vs evidence quality"
    - "precaution vs proportionality"
    - "mandatory reporting vs medical confidentiality"
    - "contact tracing vs privacy"
    - "vaccination policy vs bodily autonomy"
    - "border health vs international traffic"
    - "central coordination vs local implementation"
    - "emergency duration vs normal legal safeguards"
    - "public health science vs political authority"
    - "collective benefit vs unequal burdens"

  shortest_maxim:
    "公共衛生第一眼不是『有疫情所以政府什麼都能做』；先問『什麼風險、誰有法定權力、對誰採什麼措施、證據到哪裡、限制是否必要而且可重審、資料和跨境義務怎麼處理』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G3-25.1 第一眼｜THREAT → AUTHORITY → CASE / EXPOSURE → MEASURE → EVIDENCE → NECESSITY → PROPORTIONALITY → DURATION → REVIEW → DATA → CROSS-BORDER

```text
THREAT
＝communicable disease? biological? chemical? unknown health threat?

AUTHORITY
＝health ministry? local officer? court? border authority?

CASE / EXPOSURE
＝confirmed? suspected? contact? population risk?

MEASURE
＝reporting? testing? isolation? quarantine? vaccination? closure? travel measure?

EVIDENCE
＝clinical? lab? epidemiological? risk assessment?

NECESSITY
＝is coercive action actually needed?

PROPORTIONALITY
＝is there a less restrictive effective measure?

DURATION
＝how long and on what renewal evidence?

REVIEW
＝medical / administrative / judicial reconsideration?

DATA
＝what health/contact data may be collected/shared?

CROSS-BORDER
＝IHR? EU 2022/2371? bilateral arrangement?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G3-25.11 Evidence architecture

- case definition；
- laboratory result；
- clinical record；
- exposure timeline；
- contact list；
- epidemiological curve；
- genomic / outbreak evidence where relevant；
- risk assessment；
- public-health order；
- statutory delegation；
- notice；
- duration / renewal record；
- accommodation / support record；
- vaccination / testing record；
- border-health document；
- IHR notification；
- Emergency Committee / WHO communication；
- data-sharing record；
- review / appeal decision。

核心：

> **公共衛生證據不只證明一個人有沒有病，也必須證明國家為什麼可以把群體風險轉成對特定人的法律限制。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

反貪腐跨採購、許可、司法、政治與企業接口運作，屬政府權力完整性的橫向控制，不壓縮成單一刑事罪名。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-067-ANTI_CORRUPTION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "反貪腐法"
  english_name: "Anti-Corruption Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當公職、公共資源、採購、許可、司法、政治影響與私人利益相遇時，何種利益交換、挪用、濫用職務、干預或隱匿應被預防、申報、行政處理或刑事化；跨境賄賂、法人責任、國際合作與 asset recovery 又如何形成可追溯責任鏈？"

  historical_reason:
    "腐敗的制度危害不只在一筆錢被拿走，而是公共決策的理由被私人利益替換，並且因秘密、權力差距、代理人、離岸結構與採購網路而難以被看見。反貪腐法因此不能只靠事後抓貪污，而要同時處理 integrity systems、conflict of interest、public procurement、political finance、whistleblower/reporting、criminalization、corporate responsibility、international cooperation 與 asset recovery。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "行政裁量 vs 私人利益交換"
    - "送禮／社交 vs bribery"
    - "利益衝突預防 vs 刑事有罪"
    - "反貪腐調查效率 vs 無罪推定"
    - "財產申報透明 vs 隱私"
    - "whistleblower protection vs false accusation / confidentiality"
    - "法人責任 vs 個人罪責"
    - "public procurement discretion vs favoritism"
    - "asset recovery vs property / due process"
    - "跨境合作 vs jurisdiction / evidence"
    - "反貪腐機關獨立 vs 自身 accountability"
    - "政治資金透明 vs political association"

  shortest_maxim:
    "反貪腐法第一眼不是『有沒有收錢』；先問『誰處在什麼公共／受託位置、取得了什麼利益、要交換或影響什麼行為、利益從哪裡來、經過誰、留下什麼決策與資金痕跡』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 73.1 第一眼｜ROLE → PUBLIC / ENTRUSTED FUNCTION → BENEFIT → QUO → LINK → INTENT → DECISION EFFECT → OFFENCE / PREVENTION → ASSET PATH

```text
ROLE
＝public official？foreign public official？company officer？intermediary？

FUNCTION
＝procurement？licence？tax？judicial？political？contract？

BENEFIT
＝cash？gift？job？trip？donation？related-party benefit？

QUO
＝要求／期待換取什麼？

LINK
＝benefit 與 official act 的 nexus？

INTENT
＝offer？promise？give？solicit？accept？conceal？

DECISION EFFECT
＝真的改變決定？未遂？只建立不當影響？

LEGAL PATH
＝conflict rule？administrative misconduct？bribery？embezzlement？trading in influence？

ASSET PATH
＝proceeds → tracing → freeze/seize → confiscation → return？
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 73.27 證據特徵

- official role / delegation；
- procurement file；
- gift / hospitality；
- emails / messages；
- calendar；
- bank transfers；
- beneficial ownership；
- intermediary contracts；
- invoices；
- deliverables；
- property declarations；
- lifestyle evidence；
- political donations；
- whistleblower material；
- audit；

> **反貪案件最怕把「結果對某人有利」直接寫成「一定有交換」；必須把利益、職務行為、時間、溝通、資金與決策痕跡接成可驗證 nexus。**

No universal administrative-sanctions code can supply a case result. ICCPR remains a current treaty baseline where protected rights/criminal-character consequences are engaged, but classification, investigation, notice/hearing, standard of proof, sanction and appeal depend on the governing domestic/regional regime.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **UN Treaty Collection — ICCPR**
  - URL: https://treaties.un.org/Pages/ShowMTDSGDetails.aspx?chapter=4&lang=en&mtdsg_no=IV-4&src=UNTSONLINE&tabid=3
  - Verified point: Status 2026-08-13: in force; 175 Parties.

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

從DLM-035中專門化出監管調查、violation、行政罰與高度懲罰性制裁程序。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-189-ADMINISTRATIVE_SANCTIONS_REGULATORY_ENFORCEMENT_LAW"
  chinese_name: "行政制裁／監管執法法"
  english_name: "Administrative Sanctions / Regulatory Enforcement Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "行政／監管機關如何從監督、檢查、資料要求、調查與notice of allegations進入違規認定，並依法作成fine、penalty、disgorgement、cease-and-desist、licence suspension/revocation、debarment、publication或其他制裁；當制裁高度懲罰性時，程序、公平審判、罪刑法定、比例、不得重複處罰與司法審查如何提高門檻？"

  historical_reason:
    "現代監理依賴行政機關在金融、競爭、環境、資料、食品藥物、職安、證券與消費者領域快速執法。若每項違規都只能走刑事法院，監管會過慢；但把懲罰改名『行政』也不能讓國家逃離法定性、證據、公平程序與比例要求。歐洲人權法尤其發展出 autonomous criminal charge / Engel criteria，提醒真正的權利影響比國內名稱更重要。"

  first_move:
    "先確認authority與enforcement perimeter，再固定 alleged conduct、applicable rule、investigation powers、evidence與程序；接著判斷violation、sanction type / nature、量化基準與parallel proceedings。若制裁具有criminal nature或高度懲罰效果，必須提高fair-trial / legality / defence-rights審查。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "regulatory speed vs due process"
    - "expert enforcement vs independent adjudication"
    - "deterrence vs proportionality"
    - "administrative label vs punitive reality"
    - "investigation secrecy vs defence access"
    - "self-reporting / cooperation vs privilege"
    - "settlement efficiency vs coercive leverage"
    - "public naming vs presumption of innocence"
    - "sector compliance vs general rights safeguards"
    - "parallel administrative / criminal tracks vs ne bis in idem"
    - "discretionary penalty guideline vs legal certainty"
    - "corporate accountability vs individual attribution"

  shortest_maxim:
    "行政制裁第一眼不是『主管機關認定違規』；先問『它憑哪個法調查、哪個要件被證明、當事人怎麼被告知與答辯、這項措施到底是補救還是懲罰、罰多少怎麼算、是否與其他程序重複』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G3-15.1 第一眼｜AUTHORITY → PERIMETER → INVESTIGATION → ALLEGATION → ACCESS / DEFENCE → VIOLATION → SANCTION NATURE → QUANTUM → SETTLEMENT → PARALLEL CASE → REVIEW

```text
AUTHORITY
＝which regulator / agency?

PERIMETER
＝which person / activity / licence falls under it?

INVESTIGATION
＝inspection? request? subpoena? dawn raid? audit? algorithmic detection?

ALLEGATION
＝exact rule / conduct / period?

ACCESS / DEFENCE
＝notice? file access? counsel? hearing? privilege?

VIOLATION
＝elements + evidence + standard?

SANCTION NATURE
＝remedial? coercive? punitive? mixed?

QUANTUM
＝base? turnover? duration? gravity? gains? mitigation?

SETTLEMENT
＝admission? waiver? discount? undertaking?

PARALLEL CASE
＝criminal? civil? another regulator? same facts?

REVIEW
＝internal appeal? tribunal? court? suspensive effect?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G3-15.18 Evidence architecture

- statute / delegation；
- inspection authorisation；
- request for information；
- warrant / court authorisation where required；
- audit trail；
- interview record；
- digital evidence；
- internal regulator file；
- allegation notice；
- disclosure index；
- defence submission；
- hearing transcript；
- expert report；
- infringement decision；
- penalty calculation；
- cooperation / settlement record；
- parallel criminal file；
- publication decision；
- appeal / review judgment。

核心：

> **監管執法的證據不只回答「發生了什麼」，還要回答「國家憑什麼用這些材料把一項市場／專業義務升格成可處罰的違規」。**

# DLM-192｜國家／公權力責任與公法賠償法

```yaml

  DOMAIN_NAME: "國家／公權力責任與公法賠償法"

  INTERNAL_PLANE: "INTEGRITY_SANCTION_ACCOUNTABILITY"
  STRUCTURAL_ROLE: "ACCOUNTABILITY_SPECIALIZATION"
  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_SPECIALIZATION"
  CURRENT_LAW_STATUS: "STABLE_METHOD_ONLY__POSITIVE_LAW_TO_VERIFY"
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_III_DLM_184_204"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# G3-26｜DLM-192｜國家／公權力責任與公法賠償法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_III/V0.68-EXP/B64"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "B:司法／爭端／強制裁判、C:金錢／資本／信用／風險流"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別處理國際法上的State responsibility與國內／區域公法賠償責任；不得因都叫國家責任而合併。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-192-STATE_PUBLIC_AUTHORITY_LIABILITY_PUBLIC_COMPENSATION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "國家／公權力責任與公法賠償法"
  english_name: "State / Public-Authority Liability & Public Compensation Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當行政機關、警察、法院、立法者、地方政府、公共服務或其他公權力的行為／不作為造成個人損害時，誰是可歸責public authority；適用一般侵權、特殊國家賠償、無過失／風險責任、lawful-act special-sacrifice compensation、EU-law State liability或其他制度；違法／過失／重大違反、因果、損害、免責與程序如何形成賠償資格？"

  historical_reason:
    "公權力不是普通私人，但也不能因服務公共利益就把個別損害全部外部化。各法域因此在 sovereign immunity、一般侵權與行政法之間發展不同國家責任制度：有些要求違法與過失，有些採特別無過失／風險／犧牲補償，有些對司法、立法、警察或特定公共服務另設門檻。EU law又發展出Member State breach的獨立Francovich / Brasserie State-liability原則。"

  first_move:
    "先辨識public actor與act / omission，再找真正的liability source，而不是從行政違法直接跳到損害賠償；之後判attribution、unlawfulness / fault / strict or special-compensation basis、protected interest、causation、damage、immunity / exclusions、procedure與quantum。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "public-service autonomy vs individual redress"
    - "sovereign immunity vs rule of law"
    - "unlawfulness vs compensability"
    - "fault liability vs risk / no-fault liability"
    - "community benefit vs special individual sacrifice"
    - "effective administration vs chilling effect"
    - "judicial independence vs judicial-liability redress"
    - "legislative discretion vs rights compensation"
    - "public budget vs full reparation"
    - "annulment / review vs damages"
    - "official personal liability vs institutional liability"

  shortest_maxim:
    "國家賠償第一眼不是『政府做錯所以要賠』；先問『哪個公權力、哪個行為／不作為、哪一套責任法源、要違法還是要過失、是否存在無過失補償、因果與損害怎麼接、誰是真正被告與哪個程序有權裁判』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G3-26.1 第一眼｜PUBLIC ACTOR → ACT / OMISSION → LIABILITY SOURCE → ATTRIBUTION → WRONGFULNESS / BASIS → PROTECTED INTEREST → CAUSATION → DAMAGE → IMMUNITY / EXCLUSION → PROCEDURE → QUANTUM

```text
PUBLIC ACTOR
＝State? ministry? municipality? police? court? public body?

ACT / OMISSION
＝decision? enforcement? delay? failure to protect? physical act?

LIABILITY SOURCE
＝general tort? state-liability act? Constitution? EU law? special statute?

ATTRIBUTION
＝official acting in function? contractor? independent entity?

WRONGFULNESS / BASIS
＝unlawful? negligent? sufficiently serious? strict/no-fault? special sacrifice?

PROTECTED INTEREST
＝which right / interest is protected?

CAUSATION
＝did public conduct cause this loss?

DAMAGE
＝pecuniary? personal? non-pecuniary? property?

IMMUNITY / EXCLUSION
＝legislative? judicial? discretionary? combat? policy exception?

PROCEDURE
＝administrative claim? civil court? administrative court? special tribunal?

QUANTUM
＝full / partial / capped / statutory?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G3-26.14 Evidence architecture

- enabling statute / delegation；
- administrative decision；
- official identity / employment；
- internal instructions；
- police / enforcement records；
- court file；
- timeline of omission / delay；
- duty / protective norm；
- expert evidence；
- causation evidence；
- property / injury / financial loss evidence；
- annulment / review judgment；
- complaint record；
- EU-law source；
- prior infringement ruling where relevant；
- immunity / statutory exception；
- public-compensation claim；
- limitation / notice-of-claim record。

核心：

> **公權力賠償不是用『政府錯了』概括；要把權力法源、責任法源、受保護利益、違法／過失／特殊犧牲、因果與損害重新接起來。**

# G3-29｜第六十四批綜合案例

一個跨境家庭與疫情事件：

```text
A國兒童C因原生家庭重大照護危機進入兒童保護系統
B國夫妻申請跨國收養
A國Central Authority正在評估adoptability與consent
此時A國爆發新型傳染病
C與多名院童成為密切接觸者
衛生機關命機構隔離14日並暫停跨境移動
B國夫妻主張WHO只是建議，A國不得限制C旅行
其後法院發現隔離命令實際超過法定最長期限且沒有重審
C因此錯過一項已排定程序
B國夫妻要求國家賠償全部旅行與程序費用
```

理火不得一次回答：

> 「因為收養符合最佳利益，所以政府不能隔離；隔離違法所以全部費用都由國家賠。」

必須拆：

```text
DLM-190
→ C是否adoptable？
→ 原生家庭保存與consent是否完成？
→ HCCH 1993 scope / Central Authority / matching / Article 23 recognition？

DLM-191
→ C是case / contact / exposed person？
→ A國衛生機關的隔離權限與期限？
→ necessity / proportionality / review？
→ IHR只提供跨境義務還是直接國內命令？

DLM-035 / 036
→ 命令超期／未重審是否合法？
→ 如何撤銷或取得即時救濟？

DLM-192
→ 行政違法是否另外滿足國家賠償責任基礎？
→ 誰有claimant standing？
→ 哪些損害與違法有直接因果？

DLM-033
→ 責任若成立，哪些金額可賠？
```

不得：

```text
ADOPTION DESIRABLE
→ PUBLIC HEALTH POWER DISAPPEARS

PUBLIC HEALTH ORDER UNLAWFUL
→ ALL CONSEQUENTIAL LOSS COMPENSABLE

WHO RULE
→ DIRECT DOMESTIC DETENTION AUTHORITY
```

```yaml
DOMAIN_METHOD_REGRESSION_V0_64:

  T190_4_HCCH_SCOPE:

    pass: "Convention scope turns on Contracting States, habitual residence and intercountry movement structure, not nationality alone."

  T190_5_COUNT:
    input: "HCCH狀態表是107，所以這個數字永遠不會變。"
    pass: "status count is current-source data and must be refreshed for the case date."

  T190_6_PARENTAGE:

  T191_1_OUTBREAK:
    input: "爆發傳染病後，政府自動擁有任何封城、隔離與強制疫苗權。"

  T191_2_WHO:
    input: "WHO宣布pandemic emergency，就等於對國內人民發出隔離命令。"
    pass: "IHR/WHO international determination != domestic coercive order."

  T191_3_IHR_UNIFORM:
    input: "2024 IHR amendments已生效，所以196個States Parties今天適用完全相同文本。"
    pass: "state-specific rejection / delayed-entry status exists; check current Appendix 1."

  T191_4_PANDEMIC_AGREEMENT:
    input: "WHO Pandemic Agreement 2025已adopted，所以2026已是生效條約。"
    pass: "PABS Annex still unfinished as of July 2026; agreement not yet open for signature, much less in force."

  T191_5_EU:
    input: "Regulation 2022/2371統一了每個EU國家的個人隔離與檢疫法。"
    pass: "EU framework coordinates cross-border threats and preparedness; national health powers remain legally distinct."

  T191_6_PRIVACY:

    pass: "public-health purpose != unlimited secondary data use; route DLM-133/134."

  T192_1_ANNULMENT:
    input: "行政處分被法院撤銷，所以國家賠償一定成立。"
    pass: "annulment != liability automatically; identify separate compensation basis, protected interest, causation and damage."

  T192_2_LAWFUL:
    input: "政府行為合法，所以任何公法補償都不可能。"
    pass: "some systems recognize lawful-act / special-sacrifice compensation; exact source must be shown."

  T192_3_ARSIWA:
    input: "國家違反國際法，所以個人可以直接用ARSIWA向國內法院請國賠。"
    pass: "international State responsibility != domestic cause of action automatically."

  T192_4_EU:
    input: "Member State違反任何EU規則，個人就當然有Francovich賠償。"
    pass: "run rights-conferring rule, sufficiently serious breach and direct causal link."

  T192_5_OFFICIAL:
    input: "公務員做錯事，所以只能告那名公務員本人。"
    pass: "official / institution / State allocation depends on applicable public-liability regime."

  T192_6_RECOMMENDATION:

  T64_PRO_DISCIPLINE:
    input: "專業懲戒很重要，所以應建立DLM-193。"
    pass: "existing DLM-072 already contains full disciplinary first move; importance alone fails Group III admission."

  T64_ARCHITECTURE:
    input: "新增190-192後應回頭刪除037/025/035。"
    pass: "specialization retains mother routers; no silent parent deletion."
```

### SOURCE_BOUNDARY

- DLM-190只把adoption從family / child-protection母卡獨立；guardianship、foster care、parental responsibility與ART parentage仍分屬既有路由。
- HCCH 1993只處理其Convention scope內的intercountry adoption；不得把它當全球domestic adoption code。
- HCCH status count 107來自目前官方status table，但頁面標示last update 2025-06-17；未來個案須重新查status與EIF。
- DLM-191不讓public health吞掉clinical medicine或general emergency law；每一強制措施仍需國內法權限。
- WHO / IHR不能被描述為直接對個人發布lockdown / quarantine命令的超國家警察權。
- 2024 IHR amendments的State-specific entry-into-force / rejection狀態必須逐國查；不得用一般2025-09-19日期覆蓋例外。
- WHO Pandemic Agreement截至2026-08-25仍不能寫成已open for signature / in force，因PABS Annex截至2026-07仍未完成。
- EU Regulation 2022/2371是cross-border health-threat coordination framework，不創造一套所有Member States完全相同的individual quarantine law。
- DLM-192處理domestic/regional public-authority compensation，不取代DLM-121 international State responsibility。
- Recommendation R(84)15是soft-law comparative source；2026 CDCJ可能後續工作不等於已完成修訂。
- Francovich / Brasserie State liability只屬EU-law regime；不得全球化其三要件。
- DLM-033仍持有damages quantification；DLM-192先證責任法源與資格。
- 專業懲戒維持DLM-072 + DLM-189 routing，第三組拒絕重複立卡。

# G3-35｜V0.65 admission audit｜第四批缺口准入審查

本輪候選：

```text
A. condominium / strata / homeowners-association governance
B. adult legal capacity / guardianship / protection of adults
C. freedom of information / access to official documents
D. public procurement remedies / exclusion / debarment specialization
```

本輪的重點不是找四個名字中「最重要」的三個，而是判斷：

```text
WHICH EXISTING PARENT IS ACTUALLY INSUFFICIENT?
```

正式 audit：

```yaml
GROUP_III_ADMISSION_AUDIT_BATCH_65:

  CANDIDATE_A:
    name: "公寓／區分所有／strata／HOA治理"

    independent_first_move:

    independent_source_ecology: true
    independent_evidence_or_procedure_architecture: true

  CANDIDATE_B:

    why_not_now:

    de_dup_result: "FAIL_NEW_DOMAIN / EXISTING_OWNER_STRUCTURALLY_SUFFICIENT"
    admission_decision: "ROUTING_ONLY / RETAIN DLM-051"

    independent_first_move:

    independent_source_ecology: true

Supplier exclusion/debarment does not get silently absorbed: participation/exclusion merits remain DLM-021; punitive or integrity debarment can additionally route DLM-189. Debarment may itself be reviewable under DLM-195, but DLM-195 does not own all debarment substance."

    independent_first_move:
      "PROCUREMENT → CHALLENGED DECISION → STANDING → TIME → PRE/POST AWARD → STANDSTILL / SUSPENSION → RECORD → REVIEW BODY → INTERIM RELIEF → MERITS → REMEDY → CONTRACT EFFECT"
    independent_source_ecology: true
    independent_evidence_or_procedure_architecture: true
    independent_failure_modes: true
    de_dup_result: "PARTIAL PASS"
    admission_decision: "CREATE DLM-195 FOR REMEDIES / BID CHALLENGE; DEBARMENT REMAINS INTERSECTION ROUTE"
```

本輪准入：

```text
DLM-193｜公寓／區分所有／Strata／HOA治理法
DLM-194｜資訊公開／政府文件近用法
DLM-195｜政府採購救濟／投標爭議法
```

本輪不准入：

```text
ADULT LEGAL CAPACITY / GUARDIANSHIP
→ RETAIN DLM-051

PROFESSIONAL DISCIPLINE
→ RETAIN DLM-072

FINAL NON-MONETARY REMEDIES
→ CONTINUE HOLD
```

本輪新增另一條 de-dup 原則：

> **候選可以只部分通過。**
>
> **「採購救濟」取得新卡，不代表「debarment」必須被同一張卡吞掉。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留connecting facts、choice of law、characterization、mandatory rules、public policy與一般跨境私法總路由；181接管國際民商事管轄／choice of court，182接管外國判決承認執行，183接管跨境送達／取證司法協助。

### PARTIAL_SUPERSESSION ROUTING LOCK

```text
GENERAL CONFLICTS / CONNECTING FACTS / CHARACTERIZATION / CHOICE OF LAW / MANDATORY RULES / PUBLIC POLICY → DLM-004
INTERNATIONAL CIVIL & COMMERCIAL JURISDICTION / CHOICE OF COURT → DLM-181
FOREIGN JUDGMENT RECOGNITION / ENFORCEMENT → DLM-182
CROSS-BORDER SERVICE / EVIDENCE / JUDICIAL ASSISTANCE → DLM-183
```

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-004-PRIVATE_INTERNATIONAL_LAW"
  chinese_name: "國際私法"
  english_name: "Private International Law / Conflict of Laws"

  domain_core_question:
    "當一個私人法律關係同時連結數個法域時，哪個法院有資格處理、哪一套法律應支配爭議，以及一地作成的判決能否在另一地取得效力？"

  historical_reason:
    "私人生活與商業活動會跨越國界，但法院與法律仍由不同國家分別組織。若每個法域只依自己國內直覺處理跨境案件，當事人將無法預測在哪裡被告、適用哪一國法、勝訴後能否執行。國際私法因此不是創造一套世界民法，而是管理複數國家法律秩序彼此接觸時的分工、連結與承認。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "國家司法主權 vs 跨境私人關係的可預測性"
    - "法院地利益 vs 與案件關係最密切之法"
    - "當事人自治 vs 弱勢保護與強制規範"
    - "法律確定性 vs 個案實質關聯"
    - "承認外國法律效果 vs 本國公共秩序"
    - "跨境判決流通 vs 被告程序保障"

  shortest_maxim:
    "跨境私人案件第一眼要拆成三問：誰能審？用哪個法？審完能不能在別國發生效力？"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 10.1 第一眼｜FORUM ≠ LAW ≠ JUDGMENT EFFECT

國際私法最容易犯的錯，是把三個完全不同的問題壓成一個：

```text
JURISDICTION
＝哪個法院有資格審？

APPLICABLE LAW
＝法院應適用哪一套實體法／衝突規則？

RECOGNITION & ENFORCEMENT
＝外國法院已作成的判決，能否在本國被承認／執行？
```

因此：

```text
法院在 A 國
≠ 一定適用 A 國法

契約選擇 B 國法
≠ 一定只能去 B 國法院

在 C 國勝訴
≠ 判決在 D 國自動可以執行
```

這三條必須分開建構資格鏈。

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 10.8 證據特徵

國際私法案件的證據不只為了證明實體爭議。

很多材料是為了證明**法律連結**：

- 戶籍／住所／慣常居所資料；
- 公司登記；
- 實際營業地；
- 契約與選法條款；
- 選擇法院條款；
- 履行地證據；
- 交易物流與付款；
- 財產所在地；
- 外國訴訟文書；
- 送達證明；
- 外國判決正本與確定／生效證明；
- 外國法條文、官方資料、專家意見；
- 平行訴訟資訊。

核心：

> **跨境案件的「事實」有一部分本身就是管轄與選法的連結事實。**

---

## POSITIVE_LAW_GATE｜SOURCE-DERIVED / NOT RE-VERIFIED IN BUILD01

> **法位提醒：以下段落直接承接來源卡；BUILD01 未執行 web/current-law refresh。若案件依賴條約狀態、生效日期、國內轉置或最新法規，主系統必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## 10.10 Positive Law Gate

以下不能靠心法直接完成：

1. 法院地國際管轄規則；
2. 法院地衝突法規則；
3. 特殊案件是否有專屬管轄；
4. 選法／選擇法院條款效力；
5. 消費、勞動、保險、家庭等弱勢保護規則；
6. 外國法內容的法院調查／當事人舉證制度；
7. 外國判決承認與執行法；
8. HCCH 相關公約的締約與生效關係；
9. 公共秩序、強制規範、反致等本國制度；
10. 送達、平行訴訟與程序協助。

---

```yaml
  - "外國判決已勝訴＝本國法院只能照單執行。"
  - "兩國法律不同＝就構成公共秩序拒絕。"
  - "國籍＝所有跨境私人問題的唯一連結點。"
```

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "ARBITRATION_FRAMEWORK_VERIFIED__SEAT_ENACTMENT_NY_CONVENTION_PARTY_RESERVATION_CHECK_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "international/current framework status only; domestic merits not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

UNCITRAL currently records Model-Law-based/influenced arbitration legislation in 93 States / 127 jurisdictions. The New York Convention status page lists 172 Parties and records State-specific reciprocity/commercial reservations. Seat law and local enactment remain indispensable.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **UNCITRAL — Status of Model Law on International Commercial Arbitration**
  - URL: https://uncitral.un.org/en/texts/arbitration/modellaw/commercial_arbitration/status
  - Verified point: Current status: legislation based on/influenced by the Model Law in 93 States / 127 jurisdictions; domestic deviations remain material.
- **UNCITRAL — New York Convention status**
  - URL: https://uncitral.un.org/en/texts/arbitration/conventions/foreign_arbitral_awards/status2
  - Verified point: Current official status page lists 172 Parties and records reciprocity/commercial reservations for particular States.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列框架／條約狀態。若案件答案取決於特定國家、保留／聲明、領域範圍、生效日期、清單、主管機關或個案事實，仍必須在 runtime 重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別承擔國際商事仲裁與國際國家間／ICJ爭端程序；共同屬forum選擇，但主體、法源與執行完全不同。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-005-INTERNATIONAL_COMMERCIAL_ARBITRATION"
  chinese_name: "國際商事仲裁"
  english_name: "International Commercial Arbitration"

  domain_core_question:
    "私人當事人如何以仲裁協議把特定爭議交給非國家法院的仲裁庭處理，同時又依賴國家法律提供仲裁協議效力、程序支持、司法監督與跨境執行？"

  historical_reason:
    "跨境商事爭議常涉及不同法域、語言、產業與法院制度。當事人希望取得較中立、專業、可選程序且跨境執行性較高的爭端解決機制，因此仲裁以當事人自治為入口發展；但仲裁庭沒有國家強制力，必須依賴仲裁地法律與執行地法院。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "當事人自治 vs 強制程序與基本正當程序"
    - "仲裁庭自主判斷管轄 vs 國家法院最終司法監督"
    - "裁決終局性 vs 嚴重程序瑕疵／公共秩序控制"
    - "減少法院干預 vs 法院對保全、證據與執行的支持"
    - "仲裁協議獨立性 vs 主契約效力爭議"
    - "跨境執行便利 vs 執行國法秩序保留"

  shortest_maxim:
    "仲裁第一眼不是『有沒有仲裁院』；先找仲裁協議，再找仲裁地，最後才知道仲裁庭與法院各自能做什麼。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 11.1 第一眼｜AGREEMENT → SEAT → AWARD

國際商事仲裁最小三段：

```text
ARBITRATION AGREEMENT
＝為什麼這個爭議可以／必須進仲裁？

SEAT OF ARBITRATION
＝哪一個國家的仲裁法提供程序法框架與司法監督？

AWARD / ENFORCEMENT
＝裁決如何取得跨境承認與執行？
```

很多錯誤都來自跳過中間的 `SEAT`。

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 11.9 證據特徵

仲裁案件第一批要找的不是判例，而是：

- 主契約；
- 仲裁條款；
- 條款併入文件；
- 往來郵件／電子簽署；
- 仲裁機構受理文件；
- 仲裁通知；
- 仲裁地約定；
- 仲裁規則版本；
- 仲裁人任命；
- 管轄異議；
- 程序命令；
- 送達紀錄；
- 聽證通知；
- 臨時措施；
- 最終裁決；
- 更正／補充裁決；
- 仲裁地法院撤銷程序資料；
- 執行地財產資料。

核心：

> **仲裁案件先看程序出生證明，再看誰在實體上有理。**

## POSITIVE_LAW_GATE｜SOURCE-DERIVED / NOT RE-VERIFIED IN BUILD01

> **法位提醒：以下段落直接承接來源卡；BUILD01 未執行 web/current-law refresh。若案件依賴條約狀態、生效日期、國內轉置或最新法規，主系統必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## 11.11 Positive Law Gate

以下必須現查：

1. 仲裁協議之形式與成立要求；
2. 仲裁協議準據法；
3. 仲裁地；
4. 仲裁地國是否採 UNCITRAL Model Law、採何版本及本國修改；
5. 爭議是否可仲裁；
6. 法院介入與臨時措施；
7. 仲裁庭管轄異議程序；
8. 撤銷事由與期限；
9. 紐約公約締約狀態、保留及適用；
10. 執行地法與拒絕執行事由；
11. 機構規則的具體版本；
12. 非簽約方、條款併入與集團關係等特殊問題。

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
```

There is no universal administrative-review code. The stable method can remain global, but standing, exhaustion, filing periods, review standards, interim relief, remedies and execution must be verified under the applicable domestic/regional public-law system.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **UN Treaty Collection — ICCPR**
  - URL: https://treaties.un.org/Pages/ShowMTDSGDetails.aspx?chapter=4&lang=en&mtdsg_no=IV-4&src=UNTSONLINE&tabid=3

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

A↔B橋接：行政行為受到挑戰時，由standing/route/deadline/exhaustion/interim relief/standard/remedy/execution形成司法審查鏈。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-036-ADMINISTRATIVE_REMEDIES_JUDICIAL_REVIEW"
  chinese_name: "行政救濟法"
  english_name: "Administrative Remedies / Judicial Review"

  domain_core_question:
    "當行政決定、行政不作為或公權力行使可能違法時，誰有資格挑戰、應先走哪一層行政／司法程序、法院審查什麼、可以給什麼救濟，並如何確保勝訴不是只得到一張無法改變現實的判決？"

  historical_reason:
    "依法行政若沒有救濟，只是一個要求行政機關自行守法的願望。有效公法制度必須讓受影響者有機會把行政決定帶到較獨立的審查位置，並讓審查者能撤銷、命重新決定、停止執行、確認權利或在適當情況下命給付／賠償。行政救濟因此是法治從抽象要求進入可執行現實的橋樑。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "行政自主／專業 vs 司法控制"
    - "程序終局性 vs 糾正違法"
    - "先行政自我糾錯 vs 立即司法保護"
    - "法院審查合法性 vs 取代行政裁量"
    - "快速執行政策 vs 暫時權利保護"
    - "訴訟門檻 vs access to justice"
    - "判決形式勝訴 vs 實際有效救濟"
    - "行政穩定性 vs 撤銷溯及效果"
    - "個人救濟 vs 系統性違法修正"

  shortest_maxim:
    "行政救濟第一眼不是『政府決定錯了就去告』；先問『哪個行政行為、誰受法律上影響、先走什麼程序、期限多久、能不能先停止執行、法院能審到哪裡、勝訴後能得到什麼』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 42.1 第一眼｜ACT → STANDING → ROUTE → DEADLINE → EXHAUSTION → INTERIM RELIEF → STANDARD → REMEDY → EXECUTION

```text
ACT
＝行政處分？命令？不作為？事實行為？契約？

STANDING
＝誰的法律利益受影響？

ROUTE
＝異議？訴願／行政上訴？專門審裁？行政法院？普通法院？憲法訴訟？

DEADLINE
＝起算與期限？

EXHAUSTION
＝是否必須先走行政內部救濟？

INTERIM RELIEF
＝處分會不會先執行？能否停止／假處分？

STANDARD
＝法院審合法性、事實、裁量還是全部重審？

REMEDY
＝撤銷？變更？命作成？確認？禁止？賠償？

EXECUTION
＝政府會不會真正履行？
```

---

## 42.20 證據特徵

- 原行政處分；
- service record；
- appeal filing；
- administrative record；
- hearing；
- reasons；
- deadline proof；
- standing evidence；
- interim harm evidence；
- agency policy；
- comparable decisions；
- expert material；
- judicial judgment；
- remand decision；
- execution correspondence；
- payment／compliance records。

核心：

> **行政救濟案件要同時保存「原決定怎麼形成」與「救濟程序怎麼走」兩條時間線。**


The ICJ Statute remains the core framework. The Court's own current materials state that contentious jurisdiction depends on State consent; forum availability cannot be inferred merely because a dispute is international.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **International Court of Justice — Statute**
  - URL: https://www.icj-cij.org/statute
  - Verified point: Articles 34–38 remain the core competence/jurisdiction framework; contentious jurisdiction depends on a valid basis of consent.
- **International Court of Justice — Basis of jurisdiction**
  - URL: https://www.icj-cij.org/index.php/basis-of-jurisdiction
  - Verified point: Current official page states contentious jurisdiction is based on State consent and identifies special agreement, treaty clauses and other bases.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列框架／條約狀態。若案件答案取決於特定國家、保留／聲明、領域範圍、生效日期、清單、主管機關或個案事實，仍必須在 runtime 重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

分別承擔國際商事仲裁與國際國家間／ICJ爭端程序；共同屬forum選擇，但主體、法源與執行完全不同。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-123-INTERNATIONAL_DISPUTE_SETTLEMENT_ICJ_PROCEDURE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "國際爭端解決／國際法院程序法"
  english_name: "International Dispute Settlement / International Court Procedure Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "國際爭端是否存在、parties是否負和平解決義務、選擇的是negotiation/mediation/conciliation/inquiry/arbitration/judicial settlement哪一條；若進ICJ或inter-State tribunal，哪一個consent basis建立jurisdiction、claim是否admissible、provisional measures/preliminary objections/intervention/default等程序門是否成立、judgment/award對誰binding、如何interpret/revise/comply/enforce？"

  historical_reason:
    "主權國家沒有天然站在一個全球法院的強制管轄下。國際秩序若只允許自助或武力，法律義務很難和平落地；但若反過來讓任何法院可無consent審任何State，又會破壞主權平等。國際爭端解決法因此建立一個多層世界：談判、調查、調停、和解、仲裁、法院與區域機制並存；司法裁判的權力首先來自consent，其後才輪到admissibility、merits與remedy。"

  specialization_note:
    relation_to_dlm_006:
      "DLM-006保留general jurisdiction/consent概念；DLM-123專門處理peaceful settlement menu、ICJ contentious/advisory jurisdiction、inter-State arbitration、provisional measures、intervention、judgment finality與compliance。"
    relation_to_dlm_005:
      "DLM-005是international commercial arbitration；DLM-123集中State-to-State/public international disputes，不能把private commercial arbitration seat/enforcement模型直接套用。"
    status: "SPECIALIZATION_REFINEMENT_CANDIDATE"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "peaceful settlement obligation vs freedom of means"
    - "State consent vs compulsory adjudication"
    - "jurisdiction vs admissibility"
    - "interim protection vs no prejudgment"
    - "judicial finality vs sovereign compliance"
    - "third-State legal interests vs bilateral case structure"
    - "confidential negotiation vs public adjudication"
    - "non-binding advisory function vs legal authority"
    - "precedential influence vs Article 59 case-specific binding force"
    - "arbitration flexibility vs tribunal competence"
    - "legal dispute vs political context"
    - "Security Council role vs judicial independence"

  shortest_maxim:
    "國際爭端解決第一眼不是『誰比較有理』；先問『雙方究竟把哪個爭端交給哪個forum、那個forum的consent從哪裡來、程序現在在哪一門、interim order／judgment／advisory opinion各自對誰有什麼拘束力』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-18.1 第一眼｜DISPUTE → PEACEFUL-MEANS OBLIGATION → FORUM → CONSENT / JURISDICTION → ADMISSIBILITY → INCIDENTAL PROCEEDINGS → EVIDENCE / MERITS → JUDGMENT / AWARD → INTERPRETATION / REVISION → COMPLIANCE / ENFORCEMENT

```text
DISPUTE
＝legal disagreement? political situation? mature claim?

PEACEFUL MEANS
＝negotiation? inquiry? mediation? conciliation? arbitration? court?

FORUM
＝ICJ? arbitral tribunal? regional court? treaty body?

CONSENT
＝special agreement? compromissory clause? optional-clause declarations?

ADMISSIBILITY
＝prior negotiation? exhaustion? nationality? abuse? indispensable third party?

INCIDENTAL
＝provisional measures? preliminary objections? intervention? counterclaim?

MERITS
＝facts + applicable law

JUDGMENT / AWARD
＝binding on whom? final?

POST-JUDGMENT
＝interpretation? revision? compliance? Security Council? domestic execution?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-18.37 Evidence architecture

- dispute chronology；
- protest notes；
- negotiation record；
- special agreement；
- treaty clause；
- optional-clause declarations/reservations；
- party status；
- filing/application；
- preliminary objections；
- provisional-measure evidence；
- claimed rights；
- urgency；
- third-State interest；
- intervention request；
- pleadings；
- evidence record；
- judgment/award；
- interpretation/revision request；
- compliance communications；
- Security Council record；
- arbitration agreement/rules；
- tribunal constitution；
- PCA/registry documents。

核心：

> **國際司法案件最容易被案名騙走：只因某國把另一國送進ICJ，不代表ICJ已經有管轄；只因Court indicated provisional measures，不代表merits已判；只因judgment final，也不代表世界上所有國家都被Article 59拘束。**

The HCCH 2005 Choice of Court Convention remains operative; the current status table records Monaco's Convention entry into force on 2026-03-01 and its Articles 19/20 declarations. Party status, territorial scope, declarations, excluded subject matter and agreement validity still require case-specific checks.

- **HCCH — 2005 Choice of Court Convention status table**
  - URL: https://www.hcch.net/en/instruments/conventions/status-table/?cid=98

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

從DLM-004與DLM-040共同專門化，處理國際民商事管轄、choice-of-court、parallel proceedings與弱勢方保護。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-181-INTERNATIONAL_CIVIL_COMMERCIAL_JURISDICTION_CHOICE_OF_COURT_LAW"
  chinese_name: "國際民商事管轄／選擇法院法"
  english_name: "International Civil & Commercial Jurisdiction / Choice of Court Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "跨境民商事爭議發生時，哪一國／哪一法院對哪些當事人與請求具有國際裁判管轄；當事人的選擇法院協議、被告住所、履行地、侵權地、不動產所在地、公司內部事項、弱勢契約、專屬管轄、平行訴訟與forum doctrines 如何決定法院是否應受理、停止或拒絕？"

  historical_reason:
    "法院的國內事物管轄與跨境裁判權不是同一問題。跨境交易若不知道可能在哪裡被告，契約風險無法定價；但若任何微小連結都足以取得管轄，又會造成forum shopping、平行訴訟與程序壓迫。現代制度因此以被告住所、特別連結、專屬管轄、party autonomy、弱勢保護與lis pendens等方法分配法院之間的審判權。"

  first_move:
    "先固定 forum、被告住所／habitual residence、每一請求的連結事實與專屬管轄，再讀 choice-of-court clause 的文字、範圍、排他性、形式與弱勢保護；最後才處理平行訴訟、forum non conveniens／lis pendens等制度。不得把選法條款當選法院條款。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "state adjudicatory sovereignty vs transnational predictability"
    - "plaintiff access to justice vs defendant foreseeability"
    - "party autonomy vs consumer / employee / insured protection"
    - "exclusive jurisdiction vs contractual choice"
    - "parallel proceedings vs access to multiple fora"
    - "legal certainty vs forum flexibility"
    - "domicile-based jurisdiction vs transaction-specific connection"
    - "anti-forum-shopping vs legitimate procedural choice"
    - "domestic jurisdiction rules vs treaty / regional uniformity"

  shortest_maxim:
    "國際管轄第一眼不是『哪國法比較有利』；先問『哪個法院憑什麼對這個被告、這個請求取得裁判權，是否存在有效選擇法院、專屬管轄或弱勢保護限制』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-209.1 第一眼｜FORUM → CLAIM → DEFENDANT → CONNECTION → EXCLUSIVE RULE → AGREEMENT → PROTECTED PARTY → PARALLEL CASE → JURISDICTION DECISION

```text
FORUM
＝which State / court is seised?

CLAIM
＝contract? tort? property? company? consumer? employment?

DEFENDANT
＝domicile? habitual residence? principal place of business?

CONNECTION
＝performance? harmful event? branch? property? establishment?

EXCLUSIVE RULE
＝immovable property? registration? company validity? other?

AGREEMENT
＝choice of court? exclusive? valid? scope?

PROTECTED PARTY
＝consumer? employee? insured? weaker party?

PARALLEL CASE
＝same parties / cause elsewhere? related action?

DECISION
＝hear? stay? dismiss? transfer? decline?
```

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "GPA_REMEDIES_FRAMEWORK_VERIFIED__COVERAGE_AND_DOMESTIC_PROCEDURE_CHECK_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "international/current framework status only; domestic merits not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

WTO GPA Article XVIII continues to require timely, effective, transparent and non-discriminatory review for covered procurement. It does not create a global challenge right for every procurement; GPA Party/coverage schedules and domestic standing/deadline/standstill/remedy rules must be checked.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **WTO — GPA 2012 legal text**
  - URL: https://www.wto.org/english/docs_e/legal_e/gpa_e.htm
  - Verified point: Article XVIII requires timely, effective, transparent and non-discriminatory administrative or judicial review for covered procurement.
- **UNCITRAL Model Law on Public Procurement (2011)**
  - URL: https://uncitral.un.org/en/texts/procurement/modellaw/public_procurement
  - Verified point: Current official page: Model Law remains the procurement reform baseline; procurement decisions/actions can be challenged by potential suppliers.

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

A↔B橋接：採購實體決策屬政府／採購法，challenge standing、deadline、standstill、review body、interim/final remedy屬司法／救濟主體。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-195-PUBLIC_PROCUREMENT_REMEDIES_BID_CHALLENGE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "政府採購救濟／投標爭議法"
  english_name: "Public Procurement Remedies / Bid Challenge Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "供應商認為政府採購的規格、資格、程序、評選、排除、決標、取消採購或其他decision違反適用採購法時，誰有standing、必須在什麼極短期限內向何種procuring entity / review body / court提出challenge；challenge是否暫停採購、能否取得interim measure、資訊與卷宗、決定撤銷／更正／重評／停止簽約／ineffectiveness／damages等何種救濟，已簽約後法律位置如何改變？"

  historical_reason:
    "政府採購的主要競爭利益常在決標前就不可逆。如果供應商只能等政府簽完契約、工程完成後再請求一般損害賠償，程序公平就失去實際價值。因此現代採購法發展出極短challenge periods、standstill、快速暫時停止、獨立review body、pre-contract corrective remedies與特殊post-award效果。這些機制與一般行政訴訟不同，因為救濟的時間本身就是競爭權利的一部分。"

  first_move:
    "先固定covered procurement與被挑戰的decision，再問challenger是否具有supplier interest / standing、知道或應知時間、目前是pre-award還是post-award、contract是否已簽；只有之後才分析automatic suspension / standstill、interim relief、record access、merits與可用remedy。不得因最後沒有得標就假定challenger本來一定應得標。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "rapid procurement vs effective review"
    - "public-service continuity vs suspension"
    - "supplier standing vs open public-interest review"
    - "confidential tender data vs defence rights"
    - "pre-award correction vs post-award stability"
    - "contract certainty vs ineffectiveness"
    - "procurement discretion vs legal review"
    - "interim suspension vs public urgency"
    - "damages vs chance to compete"
    - "debarment integrity vs supplier due process"
    - "national review design vs GPA / EU minimum standards"
    - "challenge effectiveness vs abusive tactical litigation"

  shortest_maxim:
    "採購救濟第一眼不是『我沒得標所以要申訴』；先問『哪一個採購決定、你有沒有依法可挑戰的利益、期限還在不在、契約簽了沒、現在需要先停什麼、review body能真正改掉什麼』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G3-38.1 第一眼｜PROCUREMENT → DECISION → COVERAGE → STANDING → KNOWLEDGE / TIME → PRE/POST AWARD → STANDSTILL / SUSPENSION → RECORD → REVIEW BODY → INTERIM RELIEF → MERITS → REMEDY → CONTRACT EFFECT

```text
PROCUREMENT
＝which tender / lot / framework / concession?

DECISION
＝specification? qualification? exclusion? evaluation? award? cancellation?

COVERAGE
＝which procurement statute / treaty / threshold?

STANDING
＝supplier? bidder? potential bidder? harmed / risk of harm?

KNOWLEDGE / TIME
＝when knew / should have known? challenge deadline?

PRE/POST AWARD
＝contract signed? standstill running?

STANDSTILL / SUSPENSION
＝automatic? discretionary? public-interest exception?

RECORD
＝award reasons? evaluation? confidential tender material?

REVIEW BODY
＝procuring entity? administrative board? tribunal? court?

INTERIM RELIEF
＝suspend award? tender? contract implementation?

MERITS
＝breach of procurement rule?

REMEDY
＝correct? set aside? re-evaluate? re-tender? damages? ineffectiveness?

CONTRACT EFFECT
＝contract preserved? shortened? ineffective? compensation?
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G3-38.16 Evidence architecture

- procurement notice；
- tender documents；
- specification；
- bidder questions；
- bid；
- exclusion notice；
- award notice；
- standstill notice；
- reasons；
- scoring sheets；
- evaluation record；
- conflict disclosures；
- contract-signature date；
- challenge filing；
- proof of knowledge；
- interim application；
- suspension decision；
- confidential-document log；
- review hearing；
- corrective decision；
- re-evaluation；
- contract；
- modification；
- damages evidence；
- debarment record；
- judicial review record。

核心：

> **採購救濟的證據不只證明機關選錯了誰；還要證明你在正確時間、以正確身分、對正確decision使用了真正能改變採購進程的程序。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留罪刑法定、構成要件、mens rea、因果、抗辯、未遂／共犯等刑法總層；163–168及117承擔專門犯罪類型。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-028-CRIMINAL_LAW"
  chinese_name: "刑法"
  english_name: "Criminal Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "國家在什麼條件下，才有資格把一個人的行為從『不道德、造成損害、違反契約或令人厭惡』提升為可受刑罰譴責的犯罪？"

  historical_reason:
    "刑罰可以剝奪自由、財產、名譽甚至生命，因此刑法不能只依結果、社會憤怒或行政便利決定誰應受罰。現代刑法透過罪刑法定、構成要件、主觀責任、因果、違法阻卻、責任阻卻、未遂與共犯等制度，限制國家將道德譴責直接轉化成刑罰。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "社會防衛 vs 個人自由"
    - "結果損害 vs 個人可責性"
    - "行為客觀危險 vs 主觀故意／過失"
    - "刑法謙抑 vs 公眾要求嚴罰"
    - "明確性／可預測性 vs 新型態犯罪"
    - "個人責任 vs 組織／集團犯罪"
    - "預防危險 vs 尚未完成的行為"
    - "保護法益 vs 過度刑罰化"
    - "平等執法 vs 選擇性追訴"

  shortest_maxim:
    "刑法第一眼不是『這個人是不是壞人』；先問『哪一條犯罪、哪一個行為、哪一個結果、哪一個主觀要件、哪一個因果、哪一個阻卻／抗辯』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 34.1 第一眼｜OFFENCE → ACT → CIRCUMSTANCE → RESULT → MENS REA → CAUSATION → DEFENCE → PARTICIPATION

刑事案件先拆：

```text
OFFENCE
＝究竟是哪一個罪名？

ACT / OMISSION
＝做了什麼？沒做什麼？

CIRCUMSTANCE
＝犯罪要求的身份、場所、物、關係是否存在？

RESULT
＝是否要求死亡、損害、取得財產、危險等結果？

MENS REA
＝故意？明知？意圖？過失？特殊目的？

CAUSATION
＝行為是否法律上造成結果？

DEFENCE / JUSTIFICATION / EXCUSE
＝正當防衛？緊急避難？同意？錯誤？無責任能力？

PARTICIPATION
＝正犯？教唆？幫助？共謀？未遂？
```

理火鎖：

```text
BAD PERSON
≠ GUILTY PERSON
```

## 34.15 證據特徵

- 現場資料；
- 通訊；
- 金流；
- 監視器；
- 鑑識；
- 數位證據；
- 傷勢；
- 死因；
- 行為前後訊息；
- 動機資料；
- 搜索扣押物；
- 證人；
- 專家；
- 自白；
- 共犯供述；
- 行程；
- 裝置紀錄。

刑法層只問：

> **這些資料能否支撐犯罪要件。**

至於可採性與取得是否合法：

> 另進證據法與刑事程序法。

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "ROME_STATUTE_STATUS_VERIFIED__WITHDRAWAL_AMENDMENT_JURISDICTION_CASE_CHECK_REQUIRED"
```

UNTC status on 2026-08-12 lists 125 Rome Statute Parties. Multiple 2026 withdrawal notices have future 2027 effective dates, and Hungary rescinded its 2025 withdrawal notice. Party status, temporal/territorial jurisdiction, amendments, referrals and live case posture therefore require current checks.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **UN Treaty Collection — Rome Statute of the ICC**
  - URL: https://treaties.un.org/Pages/ShowMTDSGDetails.aspx?chapter=18&lang=en&mtdsg_no=XVIII-10&src=UNTSONLINE&tabid=2
  - Verified point: Status 2026-08-12: 125 Parties. 2026 withdrawal notices include Niger, Burkina Faso, Mali, Venezuela and Chad with effect dates in 2027; Hungary rescinded its 2025 withdrawal notice.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列框架／條約狀態。若案件答案取決於特定國家、保留／聲明、領域範圍、生效日期、清單、主管機關或個案事實，仍必須在 runtime 重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

承擔國際核心犯罪及殺人、性犯罪、財產犯罪、網路犯罪、組織犯罪、毒品犯罪等獨立要件／法源／證據失敗模式。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-117-INTERNATIONAL_CRIMINAL_LAW"
  chinese_name: "國際刑法"
  english_name: "International Criminal Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "某一自然人的行為是否符合genocide、crimes against humanity、war crimes、crime of aggression或其他特定國際犯罪的contextual elements、actus reus、mens rea與mode of liability；哪個national/international forum有temporal/territorial/personal/material jurisdiction；案件是否因complementarity、ne bis in idem或其他admissibility gate被阻卻？"

  historical_reason:
    "傳統國際法主要追究State responsibility；二十世紀大規模暴行顯示，如果只說『國家違反義務』，下命令、組織、執行或協助暴行的自然人可能被集體身分吸收。Nuremberg以後，genocide、war crimes、crimes against humanity等逐步形成個人國際刑事責任，Rome Statute建立永久ICC；但正因這些罪名具有最高道德與政治重量，國際刑法最需要抵抗『暴行很嚴重，所以犯罪要件當然成立』的敘事捷徑。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "atrocity accountability vs strict legality"
    - "international jurisdiction vs State sovereignty"
    - "complementarity vs impunity"
    - "mass context vs individualized guilt"
    - "command structures vs personal culpability"
    - "victim participation vs presumption of innocence"
    - "evidence from conflict zones vs fair trial"
    - "universal condemnation vs jurisdictional limits"
    - "official position vs individual responsibility"
    - "collective labels vs specific modes of liability"
    - "peace processes vs prosecution"
    - "State responsibility vs criminal guilt"

  shortest_maxim:
    "國際刑法第一眼不是『這是一場暴行』；先問『哪一個crime、它需要哪個contextual element、這個被告本人做了什麼／知道什麼／意圖什麼、用哪種mode of liability連到犯罪、哪個forum真的有管轄與admissibility資格』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-4.1 第一眼｜FORUM / SOURCE → CRIME → CONTEXTUAL ELEMENT → CONDUCT → MENS REA → MODE OF LIABILITY → JURISDICTION → ADMISSIBILITY → EVIDENCE → DEFENCE → SENTENCE / REPARATION

```text
FORUM / SOURCE
＝ICC? domestic court? ad hoc/hybrid tribunal? universal jurisdiction?

CRIME
＝genocide? CAH? war crime? aggression?

CONTEXT
＝armed conflict? widespread/systematic attack? protected group? State act of aggression?

CONDUCT
＝kill? deport? torture? attack? transfer? aid?

MENS REA
＝intent? knowledge? special intent?

MODE
＝direct? ordering? aiding? common purpose? command responsibility?

JURISDICTION
＝time? territory? nationality? referral? acceptance?

ADMISSIBILITY
＝complementarity? same case? genuine proceedings? gravity?

EVIDENCE
＝linkage? orders? pattern? digital? witness?

DEFENCE
＝mistake? duress? mental capacity? superior orders where available?

SENTENCE / REPARATION
＝after conviction only
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-4.38 Evidence architecture

- crime scene material；
- conflict classification；
- victim identity/status；
- protected group evidence；
- attack pattern/policy；
- orders；
- command structure；
- accused location/function；
- communications；
- knowledge/intent evidence；
- contribution/linkage；
- domestic case files；
- jurisdiction dates；
- State party / Article 12(3) / referral status；
- Elements of Crimes；
- Rules of Procedure and Evidence；
- witness protection；
- forensic / OSINT chain；
- defence evidence。

核心：

> **國際刑法最危險的不是不知道暴行發生，而是把「暴行發生」直接壓成「這個被告犯了這個國際罪名」。crime、context、individual conduct、mens rea、mode of liability、jurisdiction與proof必須一節一節取得資格。**

## G2-4.40 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依crime、forum、State、date、accused現查：

1. applicable statute / domestic crime；
2. Rome Statute status；
3. amendments；
4. temporal jurisdiction；
5. territorial jurisdiction；
6. nationality jurisdiction；
7. Article 12(3) acceptance；
8. Security Council referral；
9. genocide elements；
10. crimes against humanity elements；
11. war-crime IAC/NIAC provisions；
12. aggression amendments / jurisdiction；
13. Elements of Crimes；
14. Article 25 liability；
15. Article 28 command responsibility；
16. mens rea；
17. superior orders；
18. duress / mistake / other defences；
19. official capacity；
20. Article 98/cooperation；
21. complementarity；
22. unwilling/unable；
23. gravity；
24. ne bis in idem；
25. burden/standard of proof；
26. confirmation threshold；
27. evidence rules；
28. victim participation；
29. witness protection；
30. sentence；
31. reparations；
32. arrest/surrender/cooperation；
33. domestic universal jurisdiction；
34. immunities in non-ICC fora。

```yaml
RUNTIME_CARD:
  DLM_ID: "DLM-163"
  DOMAIN_NAME: "殺人／死亡結果與重大人身傷害法"
  PRIMARY_SUBJECT: "B"
  INTERNAL_PLANE: "WRONG_OFFENCE_QUALIFICATION"
  STRUCTURAL_ROLE: "OFFENCE_SPECIALIZATION"
  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_SPECIALIZATION"
  CURRENT_LAW_STATUS: "STABLE_METHOD_ONLY__POSITIVE_LAW_TO_VERIFY"
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_II_DLM_115_183"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# G2-149｜DLM-163｜殺人／死亡結果與重大人身傷害法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_II/V0.61-EXP/B55"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "E:人／家庭／勞動／社會關係、F:財產／交易／債與交換"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

承擔國際核心犯罪及殺人、性犯罪、財產犯罪、網路犯罪、組織犯罪、毒品犯罪等獨立要件／法源／證據失敗模式。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-163-HOMICIDE_DEATH_RESULT_SERIOUS_BODILY_HARM_LAW"
  chinese_name: "殺人／死亡結果與重大人身傷害法"
  english_name: "Homicide / Death-Result & Serious Bodily Harm Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一個人的行為造成死亡或重大身體傷害時，究竟是哪一種 homicide / bodily-harm offence；死亡／傷害結果如何歸因；行為人具備何種 intent / knowledge / recklessness / negligence；是否存在self-defence、necessity、partial defence、intervening cause或其他阻卻／降階事由？"

  historical_reason:
    "死亡與重傷最容易使案件被結果嚴重性綁架。刑法卻必須把『有人死亡／受重傷』重新拆回act、mens rea、causation、offence grade、defence與participation。不同法域對murder、manslaughter、negligent homicide、assault / bodily harm、poisoning、omission liability與partial defences採取不同架構，因此不能讓結果名稱直接取得罪名法位。"

  first_move:
    "先固定受害結果與可能致害事件，再逐一建立OFFENCE → ACT/OMISSION → RESULT → MENS REA → CAUSATION → DEFENCE → PARTICIPATION；不要先從『死了＝殺人』開始。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "death / serious injury vs exact culpability"
    - "intent to kill vs intent to cause serious harm"
    - "recklessness / negligence vs intentional violence"
    - "factual causation vs legal causation"
    - "single cause vs multiple contributing causes"
    - "active violence vs omission / duty to protect"
    - "self-defence vs retaliation"
    - "complete defence vs partial defence / mitigation"
    - "individual causation vs group violence"
    - "victim vulnerability vs defendant responsibility"

  shortest_maxim:
    "看到死亡，先問死亡怎麼發生、誰造成、哪種主觀責任、哪個罪名與哪個抗辯；不要讓『結果最嚴重』自動變成『罪名最嚴重』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-149.1 第一眼｜VICTIM / RESULT → ACT / OMISSION → OFFENCE FAMILY → MENS REA → CAUSATION → DEFENCE → PARTICIPATION → GRADE

```text
VICTIM / RESULT
＝death? life-threatening injury? serious bodily harm? lesser injury?

ACT / OMISSION
＝blow? weapon? poison? restraint? failure to protect? medical omission?

OFFENCE FAMILY
＝murder? manslaughter? negligent homicide? assault / bodily harm? poisoning?

MENS REA
＝intent to kill? intent to injure? knowledge? recklessness? negligence?

CAUSATION
＝factual cause? substantial/legal cause? intervening event?

DEFENCE
＝self-defence? necessity? accident? lawful authority? partial defence?

PARTICIPATION
＝principal? accomplice? joint enterprise / common purpose where recognised?

GRADE
＝which statutory/common-law level actually fits?
```

核心：

```text
DEATH
!= MURDER
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-149.13 Evidence architecture

- autopsy / cause-of-death report；
- injury photographs / imaging；
- scene reconstruction；
- weapon / toxicology；
- medical records；
- time line；
- CCTV / bodycam；
- digital location；
- communications before/after event；
- witness accounts；
- expert causation evidence；
- defendant statements；
- prior threats only where legally relevant；
- evidence of duty / caregiving relationship for omission cases。

核心：

> **死亡證明可以證明死亡；它不會單獨證明誰以何種主觀責任犯罪。**

```yaml
  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_SPECIALIZATION"
  CURRENT_LAW_STATUS: "STABLE_METHOD_ONLY__POSITIVE_LAW_TO_VERIFY"
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_II_DLM_115_183"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# G2-150｜DLM-164｜性犯罪／同意法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_II/V0.61-EXP/B55"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "E:人／家庭／勞動／社會關係"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

承擔國際核心犯罪及殺人、性犯罪、財產犯罪、網路犯罪、組織犯罪、毒品犯罪等獨立要件／法源／證據失敗模式。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-164-SEXUAL_OFFENCES_CONSENT_LAW"
  chinese_name: "性犯罪／同意法"
  english_name: "Sexual Offences / Consent Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "某一sexual act在什麼條件下屬於犯罪：行為的性質與penetration/touching等要件是否成立、complainant是否具有並行使自由同意能力、consent是否存在或被撤回、行為人對同意具有何種主觀／合理相信要求，以及age、capacity、coercion、deception、relationship、image-based conduct與特殊保護規則如何改變罪名？"

  historical_reason:
    "性犯罪最容易被兩種錯誤綁架：一是把道德觀或關係身份直接當成同意，二是把受害敘事或性行為存在直接當成罪名完成。現代性犯罪法通常必須精細處理自由同意、capacity、reasonable belief、年齡、權力關係、欺罔、影像與數位行為，同時避免以受害者是否反抗、過往性史或關係身份取代要件。"

  first_move:
    "先固定exact sexual act與exact offence，再依ACT → AGE / CAPACITY → CONSENT → DEFENDANT BELIEF / MENS REA → SPECIAL RELATIONSHIP → EVIDENCE → DEFENCE逐項處理。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "sexual autonomy vs criminal proof"
    - "freely given consent vs submission / compliance"
    - "subjective belief vs reasonable belief"
    - "capacity vs apparent agreement"
    - "age protection vs close-in-age / mistake rules"
    - "relationship intimacy vs continuing autonomy"
    - "privacy vs investigation of communications / history"
    - "victim protection vs fair-trial rights"
    - "physical sexual act vs image-based / digital sexual abuse"
    - "international consent standards vs national offence definitions"

  shortest_maxim:
    "性犯罪第一眼不是『兩人有沒有發生性行為』，也不是『誰比較可信』；先問哪個sexual act、誰有沒有capacity、同意是否自由存在、是否被撤回、以及被告對同意的法定主觀要求。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-150.1 第一眼｜ACT → PERSON / AGE → CAPACITY → CONSENT → BELIEF → SPECIAL RULE → EVIDENCE

```text
ACT
＝penetration? touching? causing activity? exposure? recording? sharing image?

PERSON / AGE
＝adult? child? protected person? relationship of authority?

CAPACITY
＝awake? conscious? intoxicated? cognitively capable? legally capable?

CONSENT
＝free choice? conditional? specific? ongoing? withdrawn?

BELIEF
＝did defendant believe? was belief reasonable? what steps were taken?

SPECIAL RULE
＝statutory presumption? age offence? deception? abuse of position?

EVIDENCE
＝messages? conduct? forensic material? witness? digital record? context?
```

核心：

```text
SEXUAL CONTACT
!= CONSENT
!= OFFENCE AUTOMATICALLY
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-150.12 Evidence architecture｜不能把「完美受害者」當證據規則

可能材料：

- contemporaneous messages；
- before / during / after communications；
- CCTV / access logs；
- location / ride records；
- forensic / medical evidence；
- witness observations；
- intoxication evidence；
- recordings / images；
- prior relationship context only where legally relevant and admissible；
- suspect interview；
- steps taken to ascertain consent where relevant。

理火鎖：

```text
DELAYED REPORTING
!= FALSE REPORT AUTOMATICALLY

INCONSISTENCY
!= CONSENT AUTOMATICALLY

NO INJURY
!= CONSENT AUTOMATICALLY
```

也：

```text
COMPLAINANT ACCOUNT
!= ELEMENTS PROVED AUTOMATICALLY
```

仍須依證據法與證明標準處理。

```yaml
  CURRENT_LAW_STATUS: "STABLE_METHOD_ONLY__POSITIVE_LAW_TO_VERIFY"
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_II_DLM_115_183"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# G2-151｜DLM-165｜竊盜／詐欺與財產犯罪法"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_II/V0.61-EXP/B55"
```
