## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

承擔國際核心犯罪及殺人、性犯罪、財產犯罪、網路犯罪、組織犯罪、毒品犯罪等獨立要件／法源／證據失敗模式。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-165-THEFT_FRAUD_PROPERTY_CRIME_LAW"
  chinese_name: "竊盜／詐欺與財產犯罪法"
  english_name: "Theft / Fraud & Property Crime Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當財產、金錢、服務、資料或經濟利益被取得、控制、轉移或造成損失時，究竟是theft、fraud、embezzlement / breach of trust、robbery、burglary、criminal damage、obtaining services或其他專門財產犯罪；行為人對property、ownership、dishonesty、deception、appropriation、gain/loss、permanent deprivation與authority具有何種法定主觀要件？"

  historical_reason:
    "財產爭議極容易被債權人、企業或當事人用『騙錢』『偷錢』快速刑事化，但刑法不能成為債務催收或契約違約的替代工具。另一方面，現代fraud又可能在尚未造成實際損失前完成，數位資產與無形利益也使傳統『拿走一個東西』模型不足。因此需要把property relation、dishonesty/deception、appropriation、gain/loss、entrustment與special offence分開。"

  first_move:
    "先問標的到底是什麼、誰具有何種權利、行為人做了哪一種取得／處分／欺罔／濫用，再選theft、fraud或其他property offence；不要先從『錢不見了』貼罪名。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "civil debt vs criminal dishonesty"
    - "ownership title vs possession / control"
    - "appropriation vs deception"
    - "actual loss vs intent / risk of loss"
    - "property offence vs service / data / digital asset offence"
    - "breach of contract vs fraudulent intent at relevant time"
    - "entrusted property vs ordinary theft"
    - "robbery violence vs underlying theft"
    - "special economic offence vs general fraud"
    - "victim mistake vs defendant dishonest conduct"

  shortest_maxim:
    "財產犯罪第一眼不是『誰少了錢』；先問標的是什麼、誰有權、被告做了什麼、是appropriation還是deception、dishonesty與intent何時形成，以及民事債務是否真的越過刑事門檻。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-151.1 第一眼｜PROPERTY / INTEREST → RELATION → CONDUCT → DISHONESTY / DECEPTION → INTENT → OFFENCE ROUTE

```text
PROPERTY / INTEREST
＝money? chattel? account balance? service? data? token? chose in action?

RELATION
＝owner? possessor? trustee? employee? agent? authorised user?

CONDUCT
＝take? appropriate? transfer? represent? conceal? fail to disclose? abuse position?

DISHONESTY / DECEPTION
＝what exactly was false / unauthorised / dishonest?

INTENT
＝permanent deprivation? gain? loss? risk of loss? benefit?

OFFENCE ROUTE
＝theft? fraud? embezzlement? robbery? burglary? criminal damage? special statute?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-151.16 Evidence architecture

- ownership / title / possession records；
- bank statements / ledger；
- transaction logs；
- invoices / contracts；
- representations / advertisements；
- emails / messages；
- access logs / credentials；
- authorisation matrix；
- trust / agency / employment documents；
- timestamps；
- accounting records；
- recipient / beneficiary trail；
- recovery / disposal records；
- evidence of defendant belief / explanation；
- evidence distinguishing pre-existing debt from dishonest acquisition。

核心：

> **財務損失證明的是損失；犯罪還需要證明取得／欺罔／濫用與法定主觀責任。**

```yaml
    lock:
      "DEATH RESULT != MURDER LABEL"

  DLM_164_SEXUAL_OFFENCES_CONSENT:
    trigger:
      - "sexual penetration / touching"
      - "consent / capacity / reasonable belief"
      - "age-based sexual offences"
      - "sexual image / covert recording where criminal sexual route exists"
    route_with:
      - "DLM-050 child protection"
      - "DLM-014 / 133 privacy & personal data"
      - "DLM-029 criminal procedure"
      - "DLM-030 evidence"
      - "DLM-066 victims' rights"
    lock:
      "RELATIONSHIP / PAST SEX != CURRENT CONSENT"

  DLM_165_THEFT_FRAUD_PROPERTY:
    trigger:
      - "taking / appropriation"
      - "deception / false representation"
      - "dishonest transfer"
      - "abuse of entrusted financial position"
      - "property loss / services obtained"
    route_with:
      - "DLM-031 contract"
      - "DLM-067 anti-corruption"
      - "DLM-068 AML"
      - "DLM-105 crypto-assets"
      - "DLM-130 trade secrets"
      - "DLM-143 securities fraud"
    lock:
      "CIVIL DEBT != CRIMINAL FRAUD"
```

### G2-152.1 三條最短 routing question

```text
人死了／重傷了？
→ 不先問誰最壞；先問因果＋主觀責任＋homicide / bodily-harm grade。

發生性行為／性接觸？
→ 不先問關係名稱；先問act＋capacity＋consent＋belief。

錢或財產不見了？
→ 不先喊詐欺；先問property relation＋conduct＋dishonesty/deception＋intent。
```

The Budapest Convention remains in force and its Treaty Office material is current in August 2026, with State-specific reservations/declarations. By contrast, the 2024 UN Convention against Cybercrime had 81 signatories and 3 Parties in August 2026 and was NOT YET IN FORCE; it must not be treated as a globally operative criminal/e-evidence treaty.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **Council of Europe Treaty Office — Convention on Cybercrime (ETS 185)**
  - URL: https://www.coe.int/en/web/conventions/full-list?module=signatures-by-treaty&treatynum=185
  - Verified point: Status page current in August 2026; Convention entered into force 2004-07-01; reservations/declarations/authorities are State-specific.
- **UN Treaty Collection — United Nations Convention against Cybercrime**
  - URL: https://treaties.un.org/doc/Publication/MTDSG/Volume%20II/Chapter%20XVIII/XVIII-16.en.xml
  - Verified point: Status August 2026: NOT YET IN FORCE; 81 signatories, 3 Parties; Article 65 requires 40 instruments for entry into force.

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

承擔國際核心犯罪及殺人、性犯罪、財產犯罪、網路犯罪、組織犯罪、毒品犯罪等獨立要件／法源／證據失敗模式。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-166-CYBERCRIME_COMPUTER_MISUSE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "網路犯罪／電腦濫用法"
  english_name: "Cybercrime / Computer Misuse Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當行為透過或針對computer system、network、device、account、data或digital service發生時，究竟是哪一種illegal access、illegal interception、data interference、system interference、misuse of devices、computer-related fraud/forgery、credential abuse、malware/ransomware或其他content/special offence；誰具有authorization，哪一項資料／系統被干預，mens rea為何，跨境調查與electronic evidence又依哪一套程序取得？"

  historical_reason:
    "數位事件最容易被兩種相反錯誤吞掉：一種是把任何資安事故都刑事化，另一種是把所有犯罪只因使用網路就叫cybercrime。現代網路犯罪法必須把system/data integrity、authorization、criminal conduct、content offences、ordinary crimes using ICT、provider cooperation、cross-border evidence與cybersecurity compliance分層。"

  first_move:
    "先問系統／資料／帳號是標的還是工具，再固定authorization、conduct、data/system effect、mens rea與offence family；最後才進跨境取證與provider cooperation。不要從『發生在網路上』直接貼cybercrime。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "system security vs criminal liability"
    - "unauthorised access vs permitted testing / legitimate use"
    - "data interference vs ordinary property damage"
    - "tool possession vs criminal intent"
    - "cyber-dependent crime vs cyber-enabled ordinary crime"
    - "content regulation vs computer misuse"
    - "provider cooperation vs privacy / privilege / due process"
    - "cross-border evidence speed vs territorial sovereignty"
    - "preservation vs disclosure"
    - "attribution evidence vs technical suspicion"
    - "security research vs misuse-of-device offence"
    - "incident response evidence vs later criminal admissibility"

  shortest_maxim:
    "網路犯罪第一眼不是『有電腦就算cybercrime』；先問系統或資料是不是犯罪標的、授權在哪裡被越過、哪一個digital act與result成立，再問跨境電子證據如何合法取得。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-159.1 第一眼｜SYSTEM / DATA / ACCOUNT → ROLE → AUTHORIZATION → CONDUCT → EFFECT → MENS REA → OFFENCE FAMILY → EVIDENCE / JURISDICTION

```text
SYSTEM / DATA / ACCOUNT
＝computer? server? cloud? phone? IoT? credential? dataset?

ROLE
＝owner? user? employee? admin? security researcher? service provider?

AUTHORIZATION
＝what access/use was permitted? by whom? within what scope?

CONDUCT
＝access? intercept? alter? delete? encrypt? transmit? deploy malware? obtain credentials?

EFFECT
＝confidentiality breach? data corruption? service disruption? financial transfer?

MENS REA
＝intent? knowledge? dishonesty? purpose to commit further offence?

OFFENCE FAMILY
＝illegal access? interception? data/system interference? device misuse? fraud? special offence?

EVIDENCE / JURISDICTION
＝logs? subscriber data? traffic data? content? provider location? data location? order type?
```

核心：

```text
CYBER INCIDENT
!= CYBERCRIME AUTOMATICALLY
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-159.16 Electronic evidence architecture

- device seizure record；
- forensic image / hash；
- access logs；
- authentication logs；
- IP / subscriber data；
- traffic metadata；
- content data；
- cloud provider records；
- preservation requests；
- production orders；
- timestamps / timezone；
- attribution evidence；
- malware samples；
- chain of custody；
- privileged / protected material；
- cross-border legal basis。

核心：

> **技術上可取得資料，不等於法律上有權取得；法律上取得，不等於已證明誰在鍵盤後面。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

承擔國際核心犯罪及殺人、性犯罪、財產犯罪、網路犯罪、組織犯罪、毒品犯罪等獨立要件／法源／證據失敗模式。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-167-ORGANIZED_CRIME_CRIMINAL_ORGANIZATION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "有組織犯罪／犯罪組織法"
  english_name: "Organized Crime / Criminal Organization Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "多名行為人是否形成法律上的organized criminal group / criminal organization / criminal association；其結構、持續性、共同犯罪目的、serious-crime threshold、material-benefit requirement、membership/participation、direction/assistance、enterprise activity與跨境性如何成立；又如何與普通共犯、conspiracy、gang、terrorism、money laundering與predicate offences分開？"

  historical_reason:
    "有組織犯罪法的危險是把『很多人一起犯罪』直接升格成犯罪組織，或反過來要求傳統黑幫式層級才承認組織。現代UNTOC deliberately 定義的是organized criminal group而不是一張固定罪名清單，並容許非正式、非階層式結構；但 national criminal-organization offences仍高度分歧，因此必須把group qualification、participation offence、predicate crime與transnational cooperation分開。"

  first_move:
    "先辨識group是否依法存在，再問其目的、持續性、serious crime／material benefit、被告參與方式與主觀認識；不要從『三個人一起做壞事』或警方稱『幫派』直接閉合。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "multiple offenders vs organized criminal group"
    - "loose network vs formal hierarchy"
    - "membership label vs proven participation"
    - "association offence vs predicate offence"
    - "criminal organization vs conspiracy"
    - "material benefit vs ideological motive"
    - "organized crime vs terrorism"
    - "serious-crime threshold vs domestic offence classification"
    - "enterprise continuity vs one-off collaboration"
    - "group evidence vs guilt by association"
    - "transnational cooperation vs domestic due process"
    - "asset confiscation vs proof of criminal origin"

  shortest_maxim:
    "有組織犯罪第一眼不是『人多』；先證明group的法律結構、持續性與犯罪目的，再證明這一個被告如何知道、參與、指揮或協助，最後才處理predicate crime與跨境合作。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-160.1 第一眼｜GROUP → STRUCTURE → DURATION → PURPOSE → SERIOUS CRIME / BENEFIT → DEFENDANT ROLE → PREDICATE OFFENCE → TRANSNATIONALITY / COOPERATION

```text
GROUP
＝how many persons? identifiable relationship?

STRUCTURE
＝formal hierarchy? loose network? stable roles? ad hoc?

DURATION
＝existing for a period of time or random immediate formation?

PURPOSE
＝which offences? financial/material benefit? ideological objective?

SERIOUS CRIME / BENEFIT
＝UNTOC threshold or domestic definition?

DEFENDANT ROLE
＝member? organiser? director? recruiter? facilitator? financer?

PREDICATE OFFENCE
＝drug trafficking? fraud? corruption? cybercrime? trafficking in persons?

TRANSNATIONALITY / COOPERATION
＝which States? extradition? MLA? joint investigation? confiscation?
```

核心：

```text
CO-OFFENDING
!= ORGANIZED CRIME AUTOMATICALLY
```

- company / beneficial ownership records；

```yaml
  english_name: "Drug / Controlled Substance Offences Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "某一物質在特定時間與法域是否受管制；行為究竟是合法醫療／科學使用、未經授權持有、製造、栽培、供應、販運、進出口、前驅物控制、處方濫用或其他drug offence；quantity、purpose、knowledge、authorization與scheduled status如何成立；國際藥物管制公約、國內schedule與刑事責任又如何分層？"

  historical_reason:
    "毒品案件最容易把『物質危險』『被列管』『持有某重量』『驗出代謝物』直接壓成單一犯罪。但國際drug-control regime本身同時承認medical/scientific use與control，criminalisation又依各國實作高度不同。新精神活性物質、處方藥、analogue、precursor與快速變動的schedule尤其要求時間法位、化驗、主觀認識與purpose分離。"

  first_move:
    "先確認物質身份與當時schedule，再確認誰持有／製造／供應／運輸、是否有medical/scientific authorization、purpose與knowledge為何；不要從『是毒品』直接跳到販運。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "public health vs criminal punishment"
    - "controlled substance vs lawful medical/scientific use"
    - "possession vs possession for supply / trafficking"
    - "quantity threshold vs intent"
    - "chemical identity vs street label"
    - "scheduled substance vs analogue / NPS"
    - "patient use vs diversion"
    - "precursor control vs finished narcotic/psychotropic substance"
    - "international scheduling vs domestic criminal schedule"
    - "treatment alternatives vs punishment"
    - "drug trafficking vs organized crime"
    - "seizure quantity vs evidentiary purity / net weight"

  shortest_maxim:
    "毒品犯罪第一眼不是『驗到一個東西』；先證明它在當時依法是什麼、被告對它做了什麼、是否具有合法授權、目的與主觀認識為何，再談possession、supply或trafficking。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-161.1 第一眼｜SUBSTANCE → SCHEDULE / DATE → ACTOR → CONDUCT → AUTHORISATION → PURPOSE / QUANTITY → MENS REA → OFFENCE ROUTE

```text
SUBSTANCE
＝what exact chemical / plant / preparation / mixture?

SCHEDULE / DATE
＝controlled under which instrument / domestic schedule at relevant time?

ACTOR
＝patient? doctor? pharmacist? manufacturer? courier? dealer? researcher?

CONDUCT
＝possess? cultivate? manufacture? prescribe? supply? import/export? traffic?

AUTHORISATION
＝prescription? licence? medical/scientific exemption?

PURPOSE / QUANTITY
＝personal use? supply? commercial scale? precursor use?

MENS REA
＝knowledge of substance? intent to supply? recklessness? wilful blindness where recognised?

OFFENCE ROUTE
＝possession? trafficking? diversion? precursor? prescription fraud? organized crime?
```

核心：

```text
CONTROLLED
!= ILLEGAL IN EVERY CONTEXT
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-161.15 Evidence architecture

- seizure record；
- chain of custody；
- laboratory report；
- net weight / purity where legally relevant；
- packaging；
- fingerprints / DNA；
- phone / chat records；
- location / transport records；
- cash / accounting；
- prescription / licence；
- medical records where legally admissible；
- precursor purchase records；
- customs declarations；
- schedule version at offence date；
- expert evidence on substance identity；
- evidence of knowledge / intent to supply。

核心：

> **化驗證明的是物質；刑事責任還要證明行為、控制、目的、主觀認識與當時法源。**

必須現查：

1. substance schedule at relevant date；
2. narcotic / psychotropic / controlled-drug definition；
3. analogue / NPS rule；
4. possession；
5. joint / constructive possession；
6. personal-use offence / decriminalisation；
7. supply / trafficking；
8. manufacture / cultivation；
9. import / export；
10. precursor offences；
11. quantity thresholds / presumptions；
12. medical / scientific exemptions；
13. prescription offences；
14. mens rea；
15. attempt / conspiracy / participation；
16. confiscation；
17. treatment / diversion alternatives；
18. jurisdiction / extraterritoriality；
19. sentencing / aggravation；
20. retroactivity / schedule commencement。

```yaml
  - "drug trafficking＝organized crime。"
```

### G2-162.1 三條最短 routing question

```text
系統被入侵／資料被動了？
→ 先問authorization + digital conduct；不是先問公司資安做得好不好。

很多人一起犯罪？
→ 先問group structure / duration / purpose / role；不是先把多人共犯叫犯罪組織。

搜到疑似毒品？
→ 先問物質＋當時schedule＋control＋purpose＋knowledge；不是先用重量決定罪名。
```

ICCPR remains in force with 175 Parties and supplies an international liberty/fair-trial baseline. Arrest, search, charging, disclosure, detention, trial procedure, appeal and exclusionary consequences remain governed by domestic procedure and applicable regional law.

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留偵查、搜索／拘束、起訴、審判、防禦權、羈押與上訴的總程序；證據法030橫向疊加，警察一般公權力另接064。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-029-CRIMINAL_PROCEDURE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "刑事程序法"
  english_name: "Criminal Procedure Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "國家懷疑一個人犯罪時，可以在什麼條件下監視、搜索、拘束、訊問、起訴與審判；又要留下哪些辯護、司法控制與救濟入口，才使刑罰權不因追求真相而變成無限制國家權力？"

  historical_reason:
    "刑事調查需要秘密性、速度與強制力，但國家同時擁有警察、檢察、監所與司法資源。如果只因『可能犯罪』就允許無限制搜索、拘捕、訊問與羈押，程序本身會先把嫌疑人當成已定罪者。因此刑事程序法以司法授權、合理嫌疑／相當理由、令狀、辯護權、緘默權、羈押審查、公開審判、推定無罪與上訴等制度限制國家追訴權。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "犯罪偵查效率 vs 個人自由／隱私"
    - "真實發現 vs 不自證己罪"
    - "偵查秘密 vs 辯護準備"
    - "羈押需求 vs 推定無罪"
    - "證據保全 vs 搜索範圍限制"
    - "被害人保護 vs 被告防禦權"
    - "程序速度 vs 充分審判"
    - "檢察裁量 vs 平等追訴"
    - "終局性 vs 錯誤救濟"

  shortest_maxim:
    "刑事程序第一眼不是『他看起來有沒有罪』；先問『國家現在正在用哪一種強制權、它的法源、門檻、授權者、範圍、期限與救濟是什麼』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 35.1 第一眼｜STATE ACT → LEGAL BASIS → THRESHOLD → AUTHORIZATION → SCOPE → DURATION → DEFENCE → REVIEW

```text
STATE ACT
＝盤查？逮捕？拘提？搜索？扣押？監聽？訊問？羈押？起訴？

LEGAL BASIS
＝哪一條程序法？

THRESHOLD
＝合理懷疑？相當理由？緊急？犯罪嫌疑重大？逃亡／串證風險？

AUTHORIZATION
＝警察自行？檢察官？法官令狀？

SCOPE
＝搜哪裡？扣什麼？問什麼？限制誰？

DURATION
＝可以多久？

DEFENCE
＝律師？通知？緘默？閱卷？異議？

REVIEW
＝法院何時能審？
```

核心：

> **程序法不是案件外包裝，而是國家每一次強制權的資格證明。**

No universal evidence code governs admissibility or weight. Electronic/cross-border evidence must be routed through applicable domestic procedure and treaty mechanisms. The 2024 UN Cybercrime Convention must not yet be treated as in force.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **UN Treaty Collection — United Nations Convention against Cybercrime**
  - URL: https://treaties.un.org/doc/Publication/MTDSG/Volume%20II/Chapter%20XVIII/XVIII-16.en.xml
  - Verified point: Status August 2026: NOT YET IN FORCE; 81 signatories, 3 Parties; Article 65 requires 40 instruments for entry into force.
- **HCCH — 1970 Evidence Convention status**
  - URL: https://www.hcch.net/en/instruments/conventions/status-table/?cid=82
  - Verified point: Last update 2026-04-30; 69 Contracting Parties; accession-acceptance relations/declarations must still be checked.

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

跨民事、刑事、行政、家事與跨境司法運作；以source→material→proposition→admissibility→weight→fact finding形成橫向證據資格鏈。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-030-EVIDENCE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "證據法"
  english_name: "Evidence Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "人類事件已經過去，法院只能透過文件、物品、證言、數位紀錄、鑑定與推論重建；法律如何決定哪些材料可以進入裁判、能證明什麼、可信到什麼程度，以及誰承擔證明不足的後果？"

  historical_reason:
    "審判無法直接重播過去。材料可能造假、誤記、污染、轉述、被非法取得或只與案件有薄弱關聯。若法院什麼都收，容易被偏見與垃圾資訊淹沒；若規則過度排除，又可能失去真實資訊。因此證據法透過相關性、可採性、證人能力、傳聞、特權、驗真、專家、排除規則、證明責任與證明標準管理從材料到事實的資格鏈。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "真實發現 vs 程序公平"
    - "相關資訊廣納 vs 偏見／混淆／浪費"
    - "證人敘述 vs 記憶與感知限制"
    - "專家知識 vs 專家權威化"
    - "數位資料完整性 vs 可修改／黑箱"
    - "保密／特權 vs 真實發現"
    - "非法取證排除 vs 實體真相"
    - "舉證責任分配 vs 資訊不對稱"
    - "證明標準 vs 錯誤成本"

  shortest_maxim:
    "證據法第一眼不是『有沒有資料』；先問『來源是什麼、這份材料真正內容是什麼、要證明哪個待證事實、可不可採、可信到什麼程度、證明責任在誰』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 36.1 第一眼｜SOURCE → MATERIAL → CONTENT → PROPOSITION → RELEVANCE → ADMISSIBILITY → WEIGHT → FACT FINDING

這一張直接採用理火法律主系統已建立的證據鏈：

```text
SOURCE
↓
MATERIAL
↓
OBSERVABLE CONTENT
↓
PROPOSED FACT
↓
FACT TO PROVE / ELEMENT
↓
ADMISSIBILITY
↓
PROBATIVE WEIGHT
↓
FACT FINDING
```

核心鎖：

```text
DOCUMENT EXISTS
≠ CONTENT TRUE

CONTENT TRUE
≠ ADMISSIBLE

ADMISSIBLE
≠ STRONG

STRONG
≠ ELEMENT PROVED

ONE ELEMENT PROVED
≠ CASE PROVED
```

---

- 原始檔；
- metadata；
- hash；
- chain of custody；
- acquisition logs；
- witness statement versions；
- interview video；
- lab notes；
- expert methodology；
- calibration；
- validation；
- prior inconsistent statements；
- privilege logs；
- search warrant；
- seizure inventory；
- disclosure record；
- court evidentiary ruling。

## 36.19 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依法域與程序類型現查：

1. relevance；
2. authentication；
3. hearsay；
4. witness competence；
5. expert evidence；
6. privilege；
7. character evidence；
8. prior acts；
9. confession；
10. illegally obtained evidence；
11. derivative evidence；
12. digital evidence；
13. chain of custody；
14. presumptions；
15. judicial notice；
16. burden of proof；
17. standard of proof；
18. corroboration；
19. confrontation；
20. vulnerable witnesses；
21. classified / secret evidence。

```yaml
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "—"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留claim/party/jurisdiction-common-layer、pleading、issue formation、hearing、judgment、appeal與relief-form；跨境管轄181、司法協助183、保全042、執行041取得專門優先。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-040-CIVIL_PROCEDURE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "民事程序法"
  english_name: "Civil Procedure Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "私人實體權利發生爭議後，誰有資格向哪個法院對誰提出何種請求；法院如何透過送達、聽審、爭點整理、證據交換、裁判與上訴，把私人主張轉化成具有拘束力且可被執行的司法判斷？"

  historical_reason:
    "實體法告訴人們誰有權利，但國家不能只因一方自稱有權就直接替他強制實現。民事程序法建立中立法院、管轄、當事人適格、送達、陳述機會、舉證、訴訟管理與救濟制度，使私人衝突從自力救濟轉為可被審查、可被反駁、可形成終局性的公共裁判。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "實體真實 vs 程序公平"
    - "當事人處分權 vs 法院訴訟管理"
    - "充分攻防 vs 迅速裁判"
    - "程序形式安定 vs 個案正義"
    - "公開審判 vs 隱私／商業秘密"
    - "證據開示充分 vs 成本／濫用"
    - "個別爭議解決 vs 集體／代表訴訟"
    - "終局性 vs 錯誤救濟"
    - "國內程序自主 vs 跨境司法合作"
    - "法院可近性 vs 訴訟成本"

  shortest_maxim:
    "民事程序第一眼不是『誰有道理』；先問『誰告誰、告什麼、法院憑什麼管、對方有沒有被合法帶進程序、爭點與證據怎麼形成、法院能判到哪裡』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 46.1 第一眼｜CLAIM → PARTY → JURISDICTION → SERVICE → PLEADING → ISSUE → EVIDENCE → HEARING → JUDGMENT → APPEAL

```text
CLAIM
＝要法院做什麼？

PARTY
＝原告／被告真的是權利義務主體嗎？

JURISDICTION
＝哪個國家、哪個法院、哪個審級有權？

SERVICE
＝對方是否被合法通知並取得答辯機會？

PLEADING
＝當事人各自主張哪些事實與法律？

ISSUE
＝真正爭點是什麼？

EVIDENCE
＝誰對哪個待證事實負舉證？

HEARING
＝攻防機會是否充分？

JUDGMENT
＝法院能否、應否作成何種裁判？

APPEAL
＝是否終局？還能怎麼救濟？
```

核心：

```text
SUBSTANTIVE RIGHT
≠ PROCEDURAL ENTITLEMENT TO IMMEDIATE JUDGMENT
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 46.20 證據特徵

- complaint / petition；
- answer；
- service record；
- jurisdiction agreement；
- court fee；
- case management order；
- disclosure requests；
- witness list；
- expert report；
- hearing transcript；
- settlement；
- judgment；
- notice of appeal；
- appellate decision；
- finality certificate；
- foreign judgment materials。

核心：

> **民事程序案件要保存的不是只有「證明實體權利」的材料，還要保存「法院怎麼取得裁判資格」的程序紀錄。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "HCCH_SERVICE_EVIDENCE_STATUS_VERIFIED__PARTY_AUTHORITY_DECLARATION_AND_E_EVIDENCE_CHECK_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "international/current framework status only; domestic merits not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

HCCH Service and Evidence Conventions remain operative frameworks. The Evidence Convention status table was updated 2026-04-30 and lists 69 Contracting Parties; authorities, declarations and bilateral accession-acceptance relations matter. The emerging UN cybercrime e-evidence convention was still not in force.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **HCCH — 1965 Service Convention status**
  - URL: https://www.hcch.net/en/instruments/conventions/status-table/?cid=17
  - Verified point: Convention remains in force; authorities, declarations, territorial extensions and party relations must be checked case-by-case.
- **HCCH — 1970 Evidence Convention status**
  - URL: https://www.hcch.net/en/instruments/conventions/status-table/?cid=82
  - Verified point: Last update 2026-04-30; 69 Contracting Parties; accession-acceptance relations/declarations must still be checked.
- **UN Treaty Collection — United Nations Convention against Cybercrime**
  - URL: https://treaties.un.org/doc/Publication/MTDSG/Volume%20II/Chapter%20XVIII/XVIII-16.en.xml
  - Verified point: Status August 2026: NOT YET IN FORCE; 81 signatories, 3 Parties; Article 65 requires 40 instruments for entry into force.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列框架／條約狀態。若案件答案取決於特定國家、保留／聲明、領域範圍、生效日期、清單、主管機關或個案事實，仍必須在 runtime 重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

處理跨境service/evidence/judicial assistance；不把國內程序規則或證據法全球化。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-183-CROSS_BORDER_SERVICE_EVIDENCE_JUDICIAL_ASSISTANCE_LAW"
  chinese_name: "跨境送達／取證與司法協助法"
  english_name: "Cross-Border Service / Evidence & Judicial Assistance Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "民商事司法程序中的 summons、pleadings、judgment 等文書需要送到境外，或證人、文件、檢驗、電子資料位於另一法域時，法院與當事人可以透過哪些 treaty、regional、diplomatic、central-authority、direct-taking或國內法渠道取得有效送達／證據；requested State 的主權、程序、翻譯、保密、強制、拒絕與技術要求如何限制？"

  historical_reason:
    "法院命令通常沒有自動跨境強制力。一國程序若直接把本國送達、傳票、discovery與強制命令投射到境外，會碰到另一國司法主權、不同證據制度、隱私、商業秘密與blocking rules。HCCH Service / Evidence Conventions與區域司法合作制度因此建立傳輸與協助渠道，讓程序跨境移動而不假裝國界不存在。"

  first_move:
    "先分 SERVICE 還是 EVIDENCE，再固定 requested State、文件／證據位置與案件性質；確認兩國間可用 treaty / regional channel、central authority、declarations與國內替代方法；之後處理翻譯、形式、期限、強制、privilege、data protection與proof/certificate。不得把『email寄到了』或『本國法院下命令了』直接當作境外程序完成。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "forum procedural needs vs requested-state sovereignty"
    - "speed vs formal service safeguards"
    - "actual notice vs legally valid service"
    - "broad discovery vs evidence specificity"
    - "compulsion vs foreign judicial control"
    - "technology vs treaty formalities"
    - "cross-border evidence access vs privacy / privilege / trade secrets"
    - "centralised cooperation vs direct channels"
    - "procedural efficiency vs translation / language rights"
    - "domestic court order vs extraterritorial limits"

  shortest_maxim:
    "跨境司法協助第一眼不是『文件／證據在國外』；先問『我要送達還是取證、東西在哪一國、哪個跨境渠道有法位、requested State接受什麼方式、哪些翻譯／強制／保密與證明要求尚未完成』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-211.1 第一眼｜TASK → STATE → INSTRUMENT → CHANNEL → AUTHORITY → FORM / LANGUAGE → EXECUTION → PROOF → OBJECTION / REFUSAL

```text
TASK
＝service? evidence? inspection? witness? document?

STATE
＝where is addressee / witness / evidence?

INSTRUMENT
＝HCCH? EU Regulation? bilateral treaty? domestic law?

CHANNEL
＝Central Authority? direct? postal? court-to-court? consular?

AUTHORITY
＝who sends / receives / executes?

FORM / LANGUAGE
＝mandatory form? translation?

EXECUTION
＝requested-state procedure? special method? compulsion?

PROOF
＝certificate? return? record?

OBJECTION / REFUSAL
＝declaration? sovereignty/security? privilege? impossibility?
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## G2-211.15 Evidence architecture

Service：

- initiating document；
- mandatory request form；
- Central Authority receipt；
- translation；
- address；
- transmitting authority；
- requested method；
- service certificate；
- delivery / refusal record；
- postal proof；
- State declarations。

Evidence：

- Letter of Request；
- issues to be proved；
- identified witness / documents；
- relevance / necessity；
- requested special method；
- translation；
- Article 23 declaration；
- compulsion record；
- privilege objection；
- video-link record；
- commissioner appointment；
- execution certificate。

核心：

> **跨境程序的證據，不只證明案件事實，也證明「另一個國家的司法協助是怎麼被合法取得」。**

# G2-213｜SPECIALIZATION REFINEMENT / DE-DUP 鎖

```yaml
SPECIALIZATION_REFINEMENT_CANDIDATE:

  DLM_004:
    status: "MOTHER_ROUTER_RETAINED"
    retained_core:
      - "characterization"
      - "choice of law"
      - "mandatory rules"
      - "public policy"
      - "renvoi"
      - "cross-domain conflict-of-laws routing"

    refined_out:
      - "DLM-181 international jurisdiction / choice of court"
      - "DLM-182 foreign judgments"

  DLM_040:
    status: "MOTHER_ROUTER_RETAINED"
    retained_core:
      - "domestic civil procedure"
      - "pleading"
      - "standing / parties"
      - "case management"
      - "domestic service"
      - "appeal"
      - "res judicata"
      - "collective actions"

    refined_out:
      - "DLM-183 cross-border service / evidence / judicial assistance"

  DLM_041:
    status: "NO_SUPERSESSION"
    note:
      "DLM-182 ends at recognition/enforcement qualification; asset execution mechanics remain DLM-041."

  DLM_005:
    status: "NO_SUPERSESSION"
    note:
      "Arbitration agreements / arbitral jurisdiction / awards remain DLM-005; court judgments are not arbitral awards."
```

核心：

```text
FOREIGN JUDGMENT
!= ARBITRAL AWARD

RECOGNITION / ENFORCEMENT QUALIFICATION
!= ASSET EXECUTION

INTERNATIONAL JURISDICTION
!= CHOICE OF LAW

CROSS-BORDER SERVICE
!= FOREIGN-JUDGMENT RECOGNITION
```

# G2-214｜第六十一批綜合案例

A國公司與B國公司簽跨境軟體供應契約。

契約有：

```text
"This Agreement is governed by the law of State C."

"The courts of State D shall have exclusive jurisdiction."
```

之後：

```text
B公司在State E起訴
A公司主要營業地在State A
軟體在State B部署
事故資料存在State F雲端
關鍵工程師住State G
起訴文件需送達State A
E國法院最後作成金錢判決
勝訴方想在State H查封資產
```

理火不得回答：

> 「選了C國法，所以C國法院管轄；E國判決既然勝訴，H國直接執行。」

至少拆：

```text
DLM-181
→ E國法院為何有國際管轄？
→ D國 exclusive choice-of-court clause是否有效、排他、在scope內？
→ HCCH 2005 / domestic / regional jurisdiction law是否適用？

DLM-004
→ 即使E國法院有管轄，merits準據法是否為C國法？

DLM-183
→ A國如何有效送達？
→ F國雲端資料如何取得？
→ G國工程師如何跨境取證？
→ Service / Evidence Convention、EU regional law、data rules如何交界？

DLM-182
→ E國判決在H國是否具有承認／執行資格？
→ HCCH 2019 / regional / domestic regime？
→ service、public policy、eligibility、conflicting judgment？

DLM-041
→ H國承認後，哪項資產可被查封、何種執行程序？
```

若契約其實是仲裁條款：

```text
DLM-005
```

重新取得主路由。

---

# G2-215｜第六十一批回歸測試

```yaml
DOMAIN_METHOD_REGRESSION_V0_61:

  T181_1_LAW_FORUM:
    input: "契約選C國法，所以只能去C國法院。"
    pass: "choice of law != choice of court; run jurisdiction separately."

  T181_2_FORUM_LAW:
    input: "E國法院有管轄，所以一定適用E國實體法。"
    pass: "jurisdiction != applicable law; return choice-of-law issues to DLM-004."

  T181_3_HCCH_2005:
    input: "有exclusive forum clause，所以HCCH 2005 Convention一定適用。"
    pass: "check international civil/commercial scope, Contracting-State relationship, consumer/employment exclusions, form and declarations."

  T181_4_MONACO:
    input: "Monaco在2026仍未受2005 Choice of Court Convention拘束。"
    pass: "Convention entered into force for Monaco on 2026-03-01."

  T181_5_PROTECTED:
    input: "消費者點了同意，所以exclusive foreign forum clause一定能對他執行。"
    pass: "protected-party jurisdiction rules can restrict party autonomy."

  T181_6_ARBITRATION:
    input: "Brussels I bis直接決定仲裁庭的管轄與仲裁判斷承認。"
    pass: "arbitration remains excluded; route DLM-005 / New York Convention."

  T182_1_WIN:
    input: "我已在外國勝訴，所以H國只能照單執行。"
    pass: "foreign judgment requires applicable recognition regime, scope, eligibility, service/due process and refusal-ground analysis."

  T182_2_MERITS:
    input: "H國法官覺得E國法院實體法判錯，所以可以完整重審。"
    pass: "recognition review is not a general merits appeal."

  T182_3_RECOGNITION_EXECUTION:
    input: "判決已承認，所以錢已經拿到了。"
    pass: "recognition != actual asset execution; route DLM-041."

  T182_4_2019_STATUS:
    input: "2019 Judgments Convention在2026對Andorra仍未生效。"
    pass: "entered into force for Andorra on 2026-06-01; current HCCH news says 33 Members bound."

  T182_5_PUBLIC_POLICY:
    input: "外國法律跟本國不同，所以一定違反公共秩序。"
    pass: "legal difference != manifest public-policy refusal."

  T182_6_ARBITRAL_AWARD:
    input: "外國仲裁判斷用HCCH 2019 Judgments Convention承認。"
    pass: "arbitral awards are a separate DLM-005 / New York Convention route."

  T183_1_EMAIL:
    input: "起訴書email到外國被告信箱，所以跨境送達已完成。"
    pass: "actual transmission != legally valid service; check applicable channel, State objections, form, language and proof."

  T183_2_SERVICE_SCOPE:
    input: "HCCH Service Convention統一了84個締約方的實體送達法。"
    pass: "Convention primarily regulates transmission channels; actual service remains tied to requested-State law / Convention method."

  T183_3_ART10:
    input: "Service Convention Article 10有postal channel，所以所有締約方都必須接受郵寄。"
    pass: "Contracting Parties may object; state-specific declarations control."

  T183_4_EVIDENCE:
    input: "本國法院下了discovery order，所以外國公司必須照本國方式直接交資料。"
    pass: "domestic discovery order != automatic extraterritorial compulsion; use treaty / regional / requested-State routes."

  T183_5_ART23:
    input: "Evidence Convention有69締約方，所以pre-trial document discovery在69國一樣可執行。"
    pass: "Article 23 declarations vary materially by State."

  T183_6_EU_DATES:
    input: "EU 2020/1783和1784從2022-07-01起每一條數位傳輸規則都同日適用。"
    pass: "general application and deferred decentralised-IT provisions have separate temporal gates."

  T61_AUDIT:
    input: "180已經達到原估計，所以V0.61應停止新增並直接整合。"
    pass: "whole-corpus audit identified independent high-density PIL/civil-procedure gaps; 180 is not closure authority."

  T61_DEDUP:
    input: "既然拆DLM-181/182/183，DLM-004與DLM-040就應刪除。"
    pass: "specialization refinement retains mother routers; no silent parent deletion."
```

### SOURCE_BOUNDARY

- V0.61 的 coverage-gap audit 是 corpus architecture 判斷，不宣稱 181–183 是唯一剩餘缺口。
- `DLM-181` 專門化 international jurisdiction / choice-of-court；choice of law仍由 DLM-004 保持主法位。
- HCCH 2005 Convention主要對國際 civil/commercial exclusive choice-of-court agreements運作，並有明確 scope exclusions；不得因存在forum clause就自動套用。
- HCCH 2005 current-status數字採2026-03-02 HCCH官方新聞的「38 States and the EU bound」表述；任何個案仍需查最新status table與bilateral/territorial operation。
- `DLM-182` 僅處理 court judgments；arbitral awards不得吸收進foreign-judgment route。
- HCCH 2019 Convention current-status採2026-06-01官方新聞：33 HCCH Members bound；個案仍須查當事國關係、declarations與temporal scope。
- Brussels I bis的abolition of exequatur不等於沒有refusal grounds，也不等於執行國失去全部程序控制。
- `DLM-183` 把service與evidence放在同一「跨境司法協助」domain內，但 runtime first move必須立即再分 SERVICE / EVIDENCE，兩者不能互換公約。
- HCCH Service Convention主要是transmission channels；不得把它寫成84國統一substantive service law。
- HCCH Evidence Article 23 declarations具有法域差異；不得因一國接受pre-trial discovery就推及全部69個Contracting Parties。
- EU 2020/1783與1784均有general application date與部分deferred digital-provision dates；任何2026數位程序必須讀current consolidated text及implementing acts。
- 跨境送達／取證涉及privacy、privilege、trade-secret與blocking-law interfaces；程序合作法位不能自動壓過其他強制規範。

# G2-218｜本卷最終口訣

> **跨境案件不是「國際私法」四個字；先拆誰能審、用哪個法、程序怎麼跨境、判決怎麼跨境。**
>
> **選法不是選法院；法院有管轄也不是法院地法自動支配。**
>
> **選擇法院是當事人自治，但自治本身仍須從公約、區域法與強制保護取得資格。**
>
> **外國判決已經勝訴，只證明來源國裁判完成；跨境強制力還要再取得一次資格。**
>
> **承認不是重審；公共秩序也不是輸家重新打實體案件的後門。**
>
> **承認與執行不同；執行資格與真的查到、扣到、賣掉資產也不同。**
>
> **email寄到，不等於跨境送達完成。**
>
> **本國法院命令存在，不等於另一國主權已退出。**
>
> **Service、Evidence、Judgments、Choice of Court 是不同公約；名字都屬司法合作，不代表可以互相借法位。**
>
> **180之後仍發現大缺口，證明數字從來不是閉合資格。**

# PLANE｜INTERIM_PROTECTION


# DLM-042｜保全程序

```yaml
RUNTIME_CARD:
  DLM_ID: "DLM-042"
  DOMAIN_NAME: "保全程序"
  PRIMARY_SUBJECT: "B"
  INTERNAL_PLANE: "INTERIM_PROTECTION"
  STRUCTURAL_ROLE: "INTERIM_RELIEF_OVERLAY"
  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_HORIZONTAL_SPECIALIZATION"
  CURRENT_LAW_STATUS: "STABLE_METHOD_ONLY__POSITIVE_LAW_TO_VERIFY"
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_I_DLM_001_114"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# 48｜DLM-042｜保全程序"
  SOURCE_PROVENANCE: "VOLUME_I/V0.38-EXP/B14"
  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "—"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

只在終局前保全未來裁判；asset freeze、interim injunction、evidence preservation等均須urgency/proportionality/security/review。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-042-PROVISIONAL_PROTECTIVE_MEASURES"
  chinese_name: "保全程序／暫時救濟"
  english_name: "Provisional / Protective Measures"

  domain_core_question:
    "在法院尚未完成終局審理以前，若等待可能使財產消失、證據滅失、傷害不可逆或未來判決失去意義，法院何時可以先採取暫時強制措施；又如何避免這種先行措施變成沒有完整審判的提前勝訴？"

  historical_reason:
    "民事審判需要時間，但時間本身會改變現實。債務人可能脫產、建物可能被拆、商業秘密可能公開、證據可能消失。若法院完全等待終局判決，勝訴可能毫無實效；若保全太容易取得，又可能在事實未完整查明前凍結企業、限制言論或奪去財產。因此保全法以急迫性、表面勝訴可能、不可回復損害、利益衡量、比例、擔保與迅速複審控制暫時權力。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "終局前保護 vs 推定未決"
    - "急迫性 vs 完整聽審"
    - "債權保全 vs 財產自由"
    - "秘密 ex parte 措施 vs 對造程序權"
    - "不可逆損害 vs 錯誤保全損害"
    - "現狀維持 vs 實質改變現狀"
    - "證據保存 vs 隱私／營業秘密"
    - "跨境資產保全 vs 管轄／主權"
    - "快速執行 vs 擔保／賠償責任"

  shortest_maxim:
    "保全第一眼不是『案情看起來很急』；先問『等到終局會失去什麼、現在先做什麼才剛好保住未來裁判、錯了誰承擔、對方何時能被聽見』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 48.1 第一眼｜UNDERLYING CLAIM → URGENCY → THREAT → MEASURE → NEXUS → PROPORTIONALITY → SECURITY → REVIEW

```text
UNDERLYING CLAIM
＝本案請求是什麼？至少有沒有初步基礎？

URGENCY
＝為什麼不能等？

THREAT
＝脫產？滅證？持續侵害？不可逆變更？

MEASURE
＝凍結、假扣押、假處分、temporary injunction、evidence preservation？

NEXUS
＝措施與本案請求有什麼連結？

PROPORTIONALITY
＝是否超過保全未來裁判所需？

SECURITY
＝申請人需否提供擔保？

REVIEW
＝對方何時有完整陳述與解除／變更機會？
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 48.22 證據特徵

- underlying claim materials；
- asset transfer records；
- bank movement；
- corporate changes；
- sale listings；
- threatening communications；
- deletion logs；
- system retention policy；
- urgency timeline；
- affidavits；
- ex parte disclosure；
- security / bond；
- order；
- service of order；
- review hearing；
- third-party objections；
- modified / discharged orders。

核心：

> **保全證據要證明的不是終局全部真相，而是「如果現在不做，未來法院可能失去什麼」。**

---

## POSITIVE_LAW_GATE｜SOURCE-DERIVED / NOT RE-VERIFIED IN BUILD01

> **法位提醒：以下段落直接承接來源卡；BUILD01 未執行 web/current-law refresh。若案件依賴條約狀態、生效日期、國內轉置或最新法規，主系統必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## 48.24 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依法域與程序類型現查：

1. attachment / prejudgment seizure；
2. freezing orders；
3. interim injunction；
4. mandatory injunction；
5. preservation of evidence；
6. ex parte standards；
7. prima facie / merits threshold；
8. irreparable harm；
9. balance of convenience / interests；
10. proportionality；
11. security / bond；
12. compensation for wrongful measures；
13. third-party rights；
14. duration；
15. review / appeal；
16. cross-border provisional relief；
17. arbitration support；
18. IP-specific measures；
19. family / administrative special interim regimes；
20. interaction with insolvency。

```yaml
  DLM_ID: "DLM-041"
  DOMAIN_NAME: "強制執行法"
  PRIMARY_SUBJECT: "B"
  INTERNAL_PLANE: "JUDGMENT_EFFECT_ENFORCEMENT"

  INTEGRATION_TREATMENT: "KEEP_NARROWED"
  CURRENT_LAW_STATUS: "STABLE_METHOD_ONLY__POSITIVE_LAW_TO_VERIFY"
  SOURCE_VOLUME: "VOLUME_I_DLM_001_114"
  SOURCE_DLM_HEADING: "# 47｜DLM-041｜強制執行法"

  CROSS_SUBJECTS: "—"
```

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

保留enforceable title、asset/ownership/exemption/priority/method/distribution及非金錢義務的強制實現；182處理外國判決進入本地前的承認資格。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-041-CIVIL_ENFORCEMENT_EXECUTION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "強制執行法"
  english_name: "Civil Enforcement / Execution Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "當債權人已取得可執行權源後，國家在什麼條件下可以扣押、查封、拍賣、移轉、命第三人付款或強制履行；又如何在債權實現、債務人基本生活、第三人財產權與債權人間優先順位之間配置強制力？"

  historical_reason:
    "若判決只是一張紙，司法權無法真正取代私人暴力；但若執行機關可以無限制拿走債務人或第三人的任何財產，司法強制本身又會成為新的侵權。強制執行法因此以執行名義、執行標的、財產調查、查封、拍賣、豁免、優先順位、第三人救濟與比例原則，把判決轉化成現實履行。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "債權實現 vs 債務人最低生活"
    - "迅速執行 vs 第三人權利"
    - "個別債權人先到先得 vs 全體債權人公平"
    - "財產透明 vs 隱私"
    - "拍賣效率 vs 資產價值最大化"
    - "判決終局性 vs 執行異議"
    - "跨境資產流動 vs 執行屬地主義"
    - "金錢債權 vs 非金錢義務之強制"
    - "國家強制 vs 比例與豁免"

  shortest_maxim:
    "強制執行第一眼不是『他欠錢就拿他的東西』；先問『憑哪個執行名義、欠誰多少、哪些財產真屬債務人、哪些可執行、誰有優先權、第三人如何救濟』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 47.1 第一眼｜TITLE → DEBT → DEBTOR → ASSET → OWNERSHIP → EXEMPTION → PRIORITY → METHOD → DISTRIBUTION

```text
TITLE
＝判決？支付命令？公證文書？仲裁裁決？其他法定執行名義？

DEBT
＝本金、利息、費用、履行內容是否確定？

DEBTOR
＝執行對象真的是義務人？

ASSET
＝銀行、薪資、不動產、動產、股份、應收帳款、IP、crypto？

OWNERSHIP
＝財產真的屬於債務人嗎？

EXEMPTION
＝有無生活／職業／社會保障豁免？

PRIORITY
＝抵押、質權、稅、勞工、先查封等順位？

METHOD
＝扣押、garnishment、sale、delivery、injunction enforcement？

DISTRIBUTION
＝變價後怎麼分？
```

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 47.20 證據特徵

- enforceable judgment / title；
- finality / enforceability certificate；
- debt calculation；
- interest；
- payment history；
- bank data；
- land register；
- company shares；
- vehicle registry；
- payroll；
- receivables；
- security interests；
- seizure record；
- appraisal；
- sale record；
- third-party claims；
- distribution table；
- exemption evidence；
- foreign recognition order。

核心：

> **執行案件證據的第一問，不是債權當初怎麼發生，而是「現在還欠多少、什麼資產可依法實現」。**

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "HCCH_2019_JUDGMENTS_STATUS_VERIFIED__PARTY_DECLARATION_DOMESTIC_ENFORCEMENT_CHECK_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "international/current framework status only; domestic merits not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

The 2019 HCCH Judgments Convention is in force. On 2026-06-01 it entered into force for Andorra; HCCH then reported 33 Members bound. Recognition scope, declarations, temporal application and domestic enforcement procedure remain case-specific.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **HCCH — 2019 Judgments Convention / Andorra**
  - URL: https://www.hcch.net/en/news-archive/details/?varevent=1156
  - Verified point: Convention entered into force for Andorra on 2026-06-01; HCCH reported 33 Members bound at that date.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列框架／條約狀態。若案件答案取決於特定國家、保留／聲明、領域範圍、生效日期、清單、主管機關或個案事實，仍必須在 runtime 重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

處理外國判決承認、拒絕事由與執行銜接；承認與執行不得壓成同一步。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-182-RECOGNITION_ENFORCEMENT_FOREIGN_JUDGMENTS_LAW"
  chinese_name: "外國判決承認與執行法"
  english_name: "Recognition & Enforcement of Foreign Judgments Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "一國法院已作成民商事判決後，該判決在另一法域是否符合承認資格、能否取得既判／證據／先決效力，並進一步進入強制執行；origin court connection、判決效力與可執行性、送達與程序保障、欺詐、公共秩序、不一致判決、scope exclusion與公約關係如何控制？"

  historical_reason:
    "國家法院的裁判權原則上源於自己的法律秩序。若判決永遠停在國境內，跨境交易的勝訴可能變成紙上權利；若所有外國判決又自動流通，執行國就失去對被告程序保障、主權與根本公共秩序的最低審查。外國判決法因此建立一個獨立的第二階段資格：不是重審誰對誰錯，而是審查外國裁判是否具有跨境流通資格。"

  first_move:
    "先取得完整 origin judgment、判決效力／finality／enforceability證明與完整程序紀錄，再確認適用 treaty / regional / domestic regime；之後分開跑 eligibility、scope、origin connection、service / due process、refusal grounds、recognition effect與enforcement mechanics。不得重新審理實體 merits，除非適用法允許的有限審查。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "judgment circulation vs state sovereignty"
    - "finality vs procedural fairness"
    - "mutual trust vs refusal safeguards"
    - "no merits review vs fraud / public policy"
    - "creditor effectiveness vs debtor defence"
    - "uniform treaty rules vs domestic residual law"
    - "recognition effect vs execution procedure"
    - "speed vs authentication / translation"
    - "foreign jurisdictional connection vs local exclusive interests"

  shortest_maxim:
    "外國判決第一眼不是『我已經勝訴』；先問『這份判決在來源國是什麼狀態、為何有跨境流通資格、被告是否被公平帶入程序、適用哪套承認制度、有哪些拒絕事由、承認後又如何真正執行』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## G2-210.1 第一眼｜ORIGIN JUDGMENT → REGIME → SCOPE → EFFECT / FINALITY → ELIGIBILITY → SERVICE / DUE PROCESS → REFUSAL → RECOGNITION → ENFORCEMENT

```text
ORIGIN JUDGMENT
＝court? date? parties? relief?

REGIME
＝treaty? regional law? domestic recognition law?

SCOPE
＝civil/commercial? excluded subject?

EFFECT / FINALITY
＝has effect? enforceable? appeal pending?

ELIGIBILITY
＝origin connection / jurisdictional filter?

SERVICE / DUE PROCESS
＝default? sufficient notice? opportunity to defend?

REFUSAL
＝public policy? fraud? inconsistent judgment? excluded ground?

RECOGNITION
＝what effect locally?

ENFORCEMENT
＝what execution route / assets / procedure?
```

核心：

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "MANDELA_SOFT_LAW_BASELINE_VERIFIED__DOMESTIC_SENTENCE_PRISON_PAROLE_LAW_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "international/current framework status only; domestic merits not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

The Nelson Mandela Rules remain the UN Standard Minimum Rules for prisoner treatment and prison management. They are international standards/norms, not a self-executing global prison code; sentence administration, discipline, release and parole require domestic law.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **United Nations — Nelson Mandela Rules, GA resolution 70/175**
  - URL: https://sanctionsplatform.ohchr.org/record/53463
  - Verified point: UN Standard Minimum Rules for the Treatment of Prisoners were adopted by the General Assembly in resolution 70/175; they are standards/norms, not a treaty code.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列框架／條約狀態。若案件答案取決於特定國家、保留／聲明、領域範圍、生效日期、清單、主管機關或個案事實，仍必須在 runtime 重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

判決後自由剝奪／矯正／紀律／醫療／假釋／釋放與監督；以justice-system execution為primary，A/E為公權力與受刑人權利交叉。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-065-CORRECTIONS_PRISON_SENTENCE_EXECUTION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "刑罰執行／監獄矯正法"
  english_name: "Corrections / Prison & Sentence Execution Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "法院判決、羈押或其他合法拘禁成立後，國家如何執行自由刑與其他處遇；受拘禁人員仍保留哪些尊嚴、醫療、通信、宗教、法律協助與申訴權；監所如何分類、紀律、隔離、移送、教育、治療、假釋與釋放，而不把『被關』偷換成『權利全部歸零』？"

  historical_reason:
    "監禁把一個人的生活幾乎完全交由國家控制：住哪裡、吃什麼、何時睡、與誰接觸、如何醫療。也因此，監所最容易發生外界看不見的暴力、剝奪與任意紀律。現代矯正法把 deprivation of liberty 本身視為主要限制，其他處遇須另有安全、紀律、治療或法定目的；同時把 rehabilitation、social reintegration、independent inspection 與 release planning 放回刑罰合法性的生命週期。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "security vs dignity"
    - "punishment vs reintegration"
    - "institutional order vs prisoners' rights"
    - "classification vs discrimination"
    - "discipline vs procedural fairness"
    - "segregation vs mental / physical health"
    - "medical independence vs prison administration"
    - "family contact vs security"
    - "confidential legal access vs monitoring"
    - "parole risk assessment vs opacity / bias"
    - "institutional control vs independent inspection"
    - "sentence finality vs sentence adaptation / release"

  shortest_maxim:
    "刑罰執行法第一眼不是『他都被判刑了還有什麼權利』；先問『他現在依哪個法律地位被關、原判決允許限制到哪裡、監所額外加了什麼限制、為什麼、多久、如何申訴、何時回到社會』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 71.1 第一眼｜LEGAL STATUS → SENTENCE / DETENTION BASIS → PLACEMENT → CONDITIONS → RESTRICTION → HEALTH → DISCIPLINE → CONTACT → RELEASE → OVERSIGHT

```text
LEGAL STATUS
＝remand detainee？sentenced prisoner？immigration detainee？juvenile？civil commitment？

BASIS
＝judgment？remand order？administrative order？

PLACEMENT
＝security classification？facility？cell？

CONDITIONS
＝space、food、sanitation、exercise、temperature、safety？

RESTRICTION
＝discipline？segregation？search？restraint？communication restriction？

HEALTH
＝physical / mental health？medication？emergency care？

CONTACT
＝lawyer？family？mail？religion？

RELEASE
＝end date？parole？conditional release？probation？re-entry support？

OVERSIGHT
＝court？ombudsman？inspection？NPM / SPT where applicable？
```

## 71.30 證據特徵

- judgment / detention order；
- sentence calculation；
- classification；
- housing logs；
- occupancy；
- medical chart；
- medication；
- segregation orders；

- complaint；
- CCTV；
- staff roster；
- death investigation；
- inspection report；
- NPM / SPT material；
- parole file；
- risk assessment；
- release plan。

核心：

> **監所案件最需要證明的不是『制度規定通常怎樣』，而是這個人每天實際被怎樣關、怎樣限制、怎樣醫療，以及每一項額外限制何時開始、何時重審、何時結束。**

```yaml
  CURRENT_LAW_STATUS: "UN_VICTIMS_SOFT_LAW_BASELINE_VERIFIED__DOMESTIC_PARTICIPATION_COMPENSATION_LAW_REQUIRED"
```

## CURRENT_LAW_VERIFICATION_2026_08_25

```yaml
CURRENT_LAW_PASS:
  verification_date: "2026-08-25"
  qualified_status: "UN_VICTIMS_SOFT_LAW_BASELINE_VERIFIED__DOMESTIC_PARTICIPATION_COMPENSATION_LAW_REQUIRED"
  verification_scope: "international/current framework status only; domestic merits not presumed"
  global_closure: false
```

The 1985 UN Declaration on Basic Principles of Justice for Victims remains the international soft-law baseline covering access to justice/fair treatment, restitution, compensation, assistance and victims of abuse of power. Domestic procedural standing and compensation schemes must still be checked.

### OFFICIAL CURRENT SOURCES

- **United Nations Audiovisual Library of International Law — Declaration of Basic Principles of Justice for Victims**
  - URL: https://legal.un.org/avl/ha/dbpjvcap/dbpjvcap.html
  - Verified point: The Declaration was adopted by the General Assembly on 1985-11-29 as an annex to resolution 40/34; it is an international standard, not a self-executing global victim code.

> **法位鎖：上述驗證只授權本卡使用所列框架／條約狀態。若案件答案取決於特定國家、保留／聲明、領域範圍、生效日期、清單、主管機關或個案事實，仍必須在 runtime 重新查驗。**

## INTEGRATION_SCOPE

被害人是person，但本卡的獨立性來自其在刑事司法中的資訊、保護、參與、restitution/compensation與review位置，因此暫留B，E/C作交叉。

## SOURCE_DOMAIN_HEADER

```yaml
DOMAIN_LAW_METHOD_CARD:

  domain_id: "DLM-066-VICTIMS_RIGHTS_CRIME_VICTIM_COMPENSATION_LAW"
  chinese_name: "犯罪被害人法"
  english_name: "Victims' Rights / Crime Victim Compensation Law"

  domain_core_question:
    "犯罪或刑事濫用權力造成傷害後，被害者在刑事司法中除了證人之外取得哪些資訊、保護、支持、陳述、隱私與救濟位置；restitution、civil damages、offender compensation、state compensation、emergency assistance 與 restorative justice 又如何分工，而不以被害者權利取消被告的公平審判？"

  historical_reason:
    "傳統刑事司法把犯罪理解為『國家 vs 被告』，受害者常被降成證據提供者：報案後案件屬於國家，是否起訴、如何調查、何時開庭都可能與其脫節。現代 victims' rights 法理試圖補回 dignity、information、participation、protection、assistance、restitution、compensation 與 redress，同時保留無罪推定與公平審判，避免制度從『忽略被害人』翻到『由被害人的痛苦直接決定罪責』。"

  fundamental_tension:
    - "victim dignity / participation vs accused fair trial"
    - "victim recognition vs presumption of innocence"
    - "protection vs confrontation / defence rights"
    - "privacy vs open justice"
    - "speed / trauma sensitivity vs evidentiary reliability"
    - "state prosecution discretion vs victim information / review"
    - "punishment vs repair"
    - "offender restitution vs state compensation"
    - "restorative process vs coercion / power imbalance"
    - "victim impact vs proportional sentencing"
    - "support services vs evidentiary role"
    - "compensation accessibility vs fraud prevention"

  shortest_maxim:
    "犯罪被害人法第一眼不是『被害人有多可憐』；先問『受了什麼法律上可辨識的傷害、目前程序在哪裡、需要什麼保護與資訊、哪些意見可以被聽見、哪些損失由誰用哪一條制度修復』。"
```

## FIRST_MOVE

## 72.1 第一眼｜HARM → VICTIM STATUS → SAFETY → INFORMATION → PARTICIPATION → EVIDENCE ROLE → RESTITUTION → COMPENSATION → SUPPORT → REVIEW

```text
HARM
＝physical？mental？emotional？economic？fundamental-right impairment？

VICTIM STATUS
＝direct victim？family？dependent？rescuer？abuse-of-power victim？

SAFETY
＝threat？retaliation？contact risk？address secrecy？

INFORMATION
＝case status？release？hearing？decision？

PARTICIPATION
＝statement？views / concerns？counsel？review rights？

EVIDENCE ROLE
＝witness？complainant？civil claimant？

RESTITUTION
＝offender returns property / pays loss？

COMPENSATION
＝state scheme？civil damages？insurance？

SUPPORT
＝medical、psychological、housing、legal aid、interpreter？

REVIEW
＝decision not to prosecute？protection order？compensation appeal？
```

---

## EVIDENCE_PROCEDURE_ARCHITECTURE

## 72.30 證據特徵

- complaint / report；
- victim statement recordings；
- medical / psychological evidence；
- economic-loss records；
- threat / retaliation；
- protection orders；
- prosecutor notifications；
- non-prosecution decision；
- review / appeal；
- restitution order；
- civil judgment；
- compensation application；
- payment；
- support-service record；
- victim impact statement；
- release notification；
- restorative agreement；
- privacy / data breach record。

核心：

> **犯罪被害人案件需要兩條證據鏈並行：一條證明犯罪與行為人的責任；另一條證明被害者實際受到什麼傷害、需要什麼保護與修復。第二條不能替第一條定罪，第一條也不能吞掉第二條的人格與救濟。**

---

## POSITIVE_LAW_GATE｜SOURCE-DERIVED / NOT RE-VERIFIED IN BUILD01

> **法位提醒：以下段落直接承接來源卡；BUILD01 未執行 web/current-law refresh。若案件依賴條約狀態、生效日期、國內轉置或最新法規，主系統必須觸發 current-law gate。**

## 72.32 Positive Law Gate

以下必須依 offence、victim status、jurisdiction 現查：

1. statutory victim definition；
2. procedural party status；
3. information rights；
4. protection / witness protection；
5. privacy / anonymity；
6. participation；
7. counsel / legal aid；
8. child-victim procedure；
9. disability accommodation；
10. sexual / domestic violence protections；
11. trafficking protections；
12. review of non-prosecution；
13. private / auxiliary prosecution；
14. victim impact statements；
15. restitution；
16. civil damages；
17. state compensation；
18. eligibility / exclusions；
19. emergency assistance；
20. insurance coordination；
21. restorative justice；
22. parole / release notification；
23. retaliation offences；
24. data confidentiality；
25. abuse-of-power remedies；
26. appeal / judicial review。

## FAILURE_MODES

## 72.31 常見誤分類

```yaml
COMMON_MISCLASSIFICATION:
  - "沒有定罪＝不存在被害人。"
  - "被害人身分成立＝被告有罪。"
  - "被害人想起訴＝檢方必須起訴。"
  - "不起訴＝傷害沒有發生。"
  - "刑事判刑＝被害損失已修復。"
  - "國家補償＝被告民事責任自動消失。"
  - "victim impact＝被害人決定刑度。"
  - "修復式司法＝被害人應該原諒。"
  - "簽和解＝沒有 coercion。"
  - "被害人有身障／表達困難＝可信度較低。"
  - "非法移民＝沒有 victim rights。"
```

| `WHO_PANDEMIC` | WHO — PABS negotiations, 20 July 2026 — https://www.who.int/news/item/20-07-2026-who-member-states-continue-negotiations-on-the-pathogen-access-and-benefit-sharing-annex | PABS Annex remained under negotiation in July 2026; finalization is a critical step before countries can consider signature and ratification of the Pandemic Agreement. |
| `WHO_PANDEMIC_OPEN` | WHO — Pandemic Agreement Q&A — https://www.who.int/news-room/questions-and-answers/item/pandemic-prevention--preparedness-and-response-accord | Agreement was adopted 2025-05-20; under Article 31 it opens for signature after WHA adopts the PABS Annex. |
| `UNCITRAL_ARB_STATUS` | UNCITRAL — Status of Model Law on International Commercial Arbitration — https://uncitral.un.org/en/texts/arbitration/modellaw/commercial_arbitration/status | Current status: legislation based on/influenced by the Model Law in 93 States / 127 jurisdictions; domestic deviations remain material. |
| `NYC_STATUS` | UNCITRAL — New York Convention status — https://uncitral.un.org/en/texts/arbitration/conventions/foreign_arbitral_awards/status2 | Current official status page lists 172 Parties and records reciprocity/commercial reservations for particular States. |
| `ICJ_STATUTE` | International Court of Justice — Statute — https://www.icj-cij.org/statute | Articles 34–38 remain the core competence/jurisdiction framework; contentious jurisdiction depends on a valid basis of consent. |
| `ICJ_BASIS` | International Court of Justice — Basis of jurisdiction — https://www.icj-cij.org/index.php/basis-of-jurisdiction | Current official page states contentious jurisdiction is based on State consent and identifies special agreement, treaty clauses and other bases. |
| `HCCH_COC` | HCCH — 2005 Choice of Court Convention status table — https://www.hcch.net/en/instruments/conventions/status-table/?cid=98 | Current status table records Monaco accession 2025-11-27 with entry into force 2026-03-01 and declarations under Articles 19 and 20. |
| `ROME` | UN Treaty Collection — Rome Statute of the ICC — https://treaties.un.org/Pages/ShowMTDSGDetails.aspx?chapter=18&lang=en&mtdsg_no=XVIII-10&src=UNTSONLINE&tabid=2 | Status 2026-08-12: 125 Parties. 2026 withdrawal notices include Niger, Burkina Faso, Mali, Venezuela and Chad with effect dates in 2027; Hungary rescinded its 2025 withdrawal notice. |
| `BUDAPEST` | Council of Europe Treaty Office — Convention on Cybercrime (ETS 185) — https://www.coe.int/en/web/conventions/full-list?module=signatures-by-treaty&treatynum=185 | Status page current in August 2026; Convention entered into force 2004-07-01; reservations/declarations/authorities are State-specific. |
| `UN_CYBER` | UN Treaty Collection — United Nations Convention against Cybercrime — https://treaties.un.org/doc/Publication/MTDSG/Volume%20II/Chapter%20XVIII/XVIII-16.en.xml | Status August 2026: NOT YET IN FORCE; 81 signatories, 3 Parties; Article 65 requires 40 instruments for entry into force. |
| `UNTOC` | UN Treaty Collection — UN Convention against Transnational Organized Crime — https://treaties.un.org/pages/viewdetails.aspx?chapter=18&clang=_en&mtdsg_no=xviii-12&src=treaty | Status 2026-08-12: in force; 194 Parties. |
| `DRUG_1961` | UN Treaty Collection — Single Convention on Narcotic Drugs, as amended — https://treaties.un.org/Pages/ShowMTDSGDetails.aspx?chapter=6&lang=en&mtdsg_no=VI-18&src=UNTSONLINE&tabid=2 | Status July 2026: 186 Parties. |
| `DRUG_1971` | UN Treaty Collection — Convention on Psychotropic Substances — https://treaties.un.org/doc/Publication/MTDSG/Volume%20I/Chapter%20VI/VI-16.en.pdf | Official status material: 184 Parties. |
| `DRUG_1988` | UN Treaty Collection — 1988 Illicit Traffic Convention — https://treaties.un.org/doc/publication/mtdsg/volume%20i/chapter%20vi/vi-19.en.pdf | Official status material: 192 Parties. |
| `HCCH_EVID` | HCCH — 1970 Evidence Convention status — https://www.hcch.net/en/instruments/conventions/status-table/?cid=82 | Last update 2026-04-30; 69 Contracting Parties; accession-acceptance relations/declarations must still be checked. |
| `HCCH_SERVICE` | HCCH — 1965 Service Convention status — https://www.hcch.net/en/instruments/conventions/status-table/?cid=17 | Convention remains in force; authorities, declarations, territorial extensions and party relations must be checked case-by-case. |
| `HCCH_JUDG` | HCCH — 2019 Judgments Convention / Andorra — https://www.hcch.net/en/news-archive/details/?varevent=1156 | Convention entered into force for Andorra on 2026-06-01; HCCH reported 33 Members bound at that date. |
| `MANDELA` | United Nations — Nelson Mandela Rules, GA resolution 70/175 — https://sanctionsplatform.ohchr.org/record/53463 | UN Standard Minimum Rules for the Treatment of Prisoners were adopted by the General Assembly in resolution 70/175; they are standards/norms, not a treaty code. |
| `VICTIMS` | United Nations Audiovisual Library of International Law — Declaration of Basic Principles of Justice for Victims — https://legal.un.org/avl/ha/dbpjvcap/dbpjvcap.html | The Declaration was adopted by the General Assembly on 1985-11-29 as an annex to resolution 40/34; it is an international standard, not a self-executing global victim code. |

## BUILD02 的閉合資格

```text
31 REFRESH_REQUIRED
→ 31 QUALIFIED CURRENT-LAW STATUS

但：

QUALIFIED STATUS PASS
!= EVERY JURISDICTION VERIFIED
!= EVERY CURRENT LIST FROZEN
!= EVERY CASE-SPECIFIC POSITIVE LAW CLOSED
```

因此 DB1 現在取得的是：

```text
V1.0 DB1 RELEASE-CANDIDATE QUALIFICATION
FOR METHOD + CURRENT-FRAMEWORK ROUTING

仍要求 runtime 在具體法域／日期／條約成員／動態清單問題上重新查驗。
```
